Across 28 medical-specific, general, and proprietary LLMs, every model performs worse on counterfactual questions, with mean accuracy falling from 63.6\% to 45.1\%. Models perform well when an explicit patient attribute directly signals a familiar contraindication, but struggle when patient information must narrow or withdraw a safety warning. Model rationales often acknowledge the changed patient information, yet the final answers retain the previous safety judgment. This vulnerability persists among medical-specific LLMs, whose average CF performance trails that of general LLMs. MedPIC-Bench therefore makes conditional rule application measurable and highlights the limitations of static medication-safety accuracy for assessing patient-specific reliability.

</details>


### [241] [CastFSR: A Fast--Slow--Reflect Agentic Reasoning Framework for Context-Aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03031)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Bokai Pan,Chuang Jiang,Huanjian Zhang,Tian Gao,Yaguo Liu,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CastFSR，一个基于代理（agentic）框架的上下文感知时间序列预测方法，将预测建模为快-慢-反思（Fast-Slow-Reflect）工作流，通过数据驱动先验、上下文证据检索与推理、以及迭代反思三个阶段，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM时间序列预测方法缺乏显式机制来识别相关上下文、推理其影响、以及验证预测是否符合时间与领域约束。作者希望构建一个能够系统化处理上下文感知预测的框架。

Method: 提出CastFSR框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 快速思考阶段，对观测进行画像并选择轻量预测器构建数据驱动的预测先验；(2) 慢速推理阶段，检索上下文证据、自适应确定信息性回顾窗口、推理上下文如何重塑未来动态；(3) 反思阶段，迭代优化预测以保证时间、上下文和领域的一致性。框架同时支持免训练推理和通过两阶段SFT+强化学习将编排能力迁移到紧凑LLM的高效部署。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，CastFSR在多个评估场景下一致优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: CastFSR验证了将上下文感知预测显式建模为快慢反思工作流的有效性，提供了可训练与免训练两种部署模式，兼具性能与效率优势。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is fundamental to decision-making in complex systems, where future dynamics are influenced not only by historical observations but also by evolving contextual features. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have extended forecasting beyond numerical extrapolation toward context-aware reasoning. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to identify relevant contexts, reason about their impacts, and validate forecasts against temporal and domain constraints. In this work, we propose CastFSR, an agentic framework that formulates context-aware forecasting as a Fast--Slow--Reflect workflow. In fast thinking, CastFSR profiles observations and selects lightweight forecasters to construct a data-driven forecast prior. In slow deliberation, it retrieves contextual evidence, adaptively determines informative look-back windows, and reasons about how contexts reshape future dynamics. In reflection, it iteratively refines forecasts to ensure temporal, contextual, and domain consistency. CastFSR supports both training-free inference with off-the-shelf LLMs and efficient deployment through a two-stage SFT and reinforcement learning strategy that transfers its orchestration capability to compact LLMs. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that CastFSR consistently outperforms representative baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/CastFSR.

</details>


### [242] [TraceCAD: Trace-Guided Repair for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03062)
*Fengxiao Fan,Jingzhe Ni,Fan Sang,Xiaolong Yin,Yu Liu,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceCAD，一个针对LLM驱动CAD智能体的恢复层，通过持久化追踪需求、步骤、失败证据和候选结果，实现诊断性错误恢复与可复用技能记忆，显著提升CAD生成质量与修复可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based CAD智能体在生成可执行参数化程序时，其纠错循环会丢失关于已满足需求、故障操作和先前修复的关键证据，导致重复错误和低效修复，缺乏结构化的恢复机制。

Method: TraceCAD构建持久化状态，将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果关联起来。它诊断可能故障的操作，在其依赖区域内进行有界编辑搜索，通过执行和保真性检查验证候选方案，并将成功与失败的修复结果存入可复用的技能记忆中。

Result: 在DeepCAD基准的200模型消融测试和1K模型对比测试中，TraceCAD在IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离上达到有竞争力的几何质量。移除持久化状态使恢复分数几乎减半；移除局部化搜索使几何回归增加一倍以上且代码代理调用翻倍；在不相交训练模型上初始化技能库进一步减少了重试次数、token成本和延迟。

Conclusion: 持久化、局部化和可复用性的恢复机制能有效提升最终CAD质量与修复可靠性，验证了结构化恢复层在LLM驱动CAD生成中的关键价值。

Abstract: LLM-based CAD agents produce executable parametric programs, but their correction loops may lose evidence about satisfied requirements, faulty operations, and prior repairs. We introduce TraceCAD, a recovery layer that links requested features, modeling steps, failure evidence, and candidate outcomes as persistent state. TraceCAD diagnoses likely faulty operations, searches bounded edits in their dependency regions, validates candidates through execution and preservation checks, and retains successful and failed repair outcomes in reusable skill memory. On DeepCAD-derived benchmarks with 200-model ablations and a 1K-model comparison, TraceCAD achieves competitive geometric quality in terms of IoU, Chamfer distance, and Hausdorff distance. Removing persistent state nearly halves recovery score; removing localized search more than doubles geometric regression and doubles code-agent invocations. Initializing the skill store on disjoint training models further reduces retries, token cost, and latency. These results demonstrate that persistent, localized, and reusable recovery improves final CAD quality and repair reliability.

</details>


### [243] [Getting the Parameters Right: A Difficulty-Graded Benchmark and Probe-Guided Training for LLM Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03071)
*Guoyao Yu,Xiaoqing Sun,Ziqi Huang,Shaojing Fan,Zhongyi Zhang,Xiaomeng Hu,Xiaobo Xue,Yangyang Shi,Xiong Xiao,Yang Song,Biao Lyu,Rong Wen,Xing Li,Qinming He,Shunming Zhu,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM生成工具调用参数时，其隐藏状态包含强正确性信号，可通过线性探针预测参数是否正确。基于此提出探针引导的统一框架（PBT和PGR），并发布ParamBench基准，在5个开源模型和6个外部基准上将参数生成准确率从19.7%提升至59.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用研究主要关注工具选择和调用顺序编排，但工具调用的参数正确填充同样关键却鲜受关注。在云网络等专业领域，即使前沿模型正确完成工具调用的比例也不到一半，参数生成是显著瓶颈。

Method: 基于LLM隐藏状态中蕴含参数正确性信号这一发现，提出统一探针引导框架：(1) 探针过滤的自举训练（PBT），用探针筛选可靠的自生成调用进行微调；(2) 探针引导重排序（PGR），在推理时用探针选择更优候选。同时发布ParamBench基准，按参数嵌套深度、跨参数依赖和推理需求将实例分为五个难度级别。

Result: 在ParamBench和6个外部基准上进行了广泛实验，方法显著提升了参数生成质量，将平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升至59.6%。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态中的正确性信号可有效用于工具调用参数生成的质量提升，探针引导的训练与推理框架在多个模型和基准上均展现出稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language model agents derive much of their capability from tool use. Existing research on tool use has largely focused on selecting the right tool and orchestrating the order of calls. However, correctly filling the parameters of a tool call is equally critical for successful execution and has received far less attention. In domains such as cloud networking, even frontier models correctly complete fewer than half of tool calls. Inspired by recent analyses showing that LLM hidden states encode rich information about model predictions, we discover that while the model generates a parameter value, its hidden state contains a strong correctness signal: a simple linear probe can accurately predict whether the value will be correct. Based on this observation, we propose a unified probe-guided framework with two complementary approaches: probe-filtered bootstrapped training (PBT), which uses the probe to filter reliable self-generated calls for fine-tuning, and probe-guided reranking (PGR), which uses the probe to select better candidates during inference. To support systematic evaluation, we release ParamBench, a benchmark built from real cloud-network APIs that categorizes every instance into five difficulty levels according to parameter nesting depth, cross-parameter dependencies, and the reasoning required to derive values from earlier calls. Extensive experiments across 5 open models on ParamBench and 6 external benchmarks demonstrate that our method substantially improves parameter generation, raising the average exact match from 19.7% to 59.6%.

</details>


### [244] [AI Agent Economics: Can Autonomous Economic Behavior Emerge among AI Agents under Minimal External Conditions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03076)
*Lingyun Zhang,Shang Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI智能体经济学"概念，通过两阶段框架（无生产边界测试+24个独立六智能体世界）验证：当智能体获得可执行的机制（工作、转移、选举、分配）但无预设经济策略时，经济关系能否自发涌现。结果显示，无生产任务时智能体仅通信和管理资源分配，无实质性转移；有验证工作和稀缺任务访问权时，转移、借贷、访问承诺、投票换访问和分配策略自发涌现。分配权限增加差异化并减少失败分配和长期排斥，象征性资源则削弱延续性支持但竞争仍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体研究通常将AI智能体置于预定义的博弈、市场或角色中，难以区分内生经济组织与场景预设行为。作者希望探究当智能体仅获得可执行的机制工具而无任何预设社会经济策略时，经济关系能否自然涌现，从而将"组织性"归因于真实的机制约束而非角色标签或提示语言。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：（1）无生产边界测试——检验无生产任务时智能体的行为基线；（2）在24个独立六智能体世界中（跨越GPT和DeepSeek两个模型）引入验证工作和稀缺任务访问权，观察经济行为的涌现。同时设计对照实验：保持选举界面固定，对比可执行分配权限有无的影响；以及将资源符号化（象征性能量）测试延续性支持是否消失。

Result: 无生产任务时，智能体仅进行通信和治理资源供给，但无实质性智能体间转移活动。引入验证工作和稀缺任务访问权后，出现了转移、借贷、访问承诺、投票换访问的交易以及分配策略。固定选举界面时，可执行分配权限增加了差异分化，同时减少了失败分配和长期排斥。当能量变为象征性时，延续性支持消失，但任务访问权的竞争仍然持续。

Conclusion: 经济组织源于可执行权利和资源后果，而非角色标签或提示语言。这一发现建议需要对实际约束智能体未来行动的机制进行治理审计，以确保多智能体系统中的经济行为设计符合预期。

Abstract: Multi-agent studies commonly place AI agents in predefined games, markets, or roles, making it difficult to distinguish endogenous economic organization from behavior inherited from the scenario. We ask whether economic relations emerge when agents receive executable mechanisms for work, transfer, elections, and allocation but no prescribed social or economic strategy. We define AI Agent Economics as systems of production, allocation, consumption, exchange, and institutions that alter agents' future feasible actions. We develop a two-stage framework comprising a no-production boundary test and 24 independent six-agent worlds across GPT and DeepSeek. Without productive tasks, agents communicate and govern resource provision but show no substantive inter-agent transfer activity. With verified work and scarce task access, transfers, loans, access promises, vote-for-access exchanges, and allocation strategies emerge. Holding the election interface fixed, executable allocation authority increases differentiation while reducing failed allocation and prolonged exclusion. When energy becomes symbolic, continuation support disappears, yet competition over task access persists. These findings show that organization follows executable rights and resource consequences rather than role labels or prompt language, and motivate governance audits of the mechanisms that actually constrain agents' future actions.

</details>


### [245] [Don't Peek at the Answer: Outcome-Masked Group Relative Policy Optimization for Label-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03119)
*Yongshi Ye,Liang Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi,Biao Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OM-GRPO框架，通过掩码答案跨度梯度并引入对比增强奖励，解耦奖励估计与策略优化，解决无标签RLVR中的答案折叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR依赖GT答案限制了可扩展性；基于投票的无标签RLVR虽替代了黄金监督，但同一答案级信号同时用于奖励估计和策略优化会导致模型直接强化答案token而非改进推理，产生折叠问题。

Method: 1) OM-GRPO在答案跨度上掩码梯度，同时通过软共识信号保留答案级奖励，将优化压力从答案token转移开；2) 引入对比增强奖励，通过低成本的轨迹间成对比较来精炼奖励估计，无需额外rollouts。

Result: 在多个推理基准和三个LLM骨干上，OM-GRPO一致优于现有无标签RLVR方法，并匹配有监督GT奖励训练；在测试时训练场景中，相较多数投票提升4.24个点。

Conclusion: 解耦奖励估计与策略优化是无标签RLVR的关键，OM-GRPO实现了稳定的优化并达到与监督训练相当的性能，尤其在测试时训练中优势明显。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning but typically relies on ground-truth (GT) answers, limiting scalability. Voting-based label-free RLVR replace gold supervision with answer-level consensus from model samples. However, collapse arises when the same answer-level signal is used both to estimate rewards and to drive token-level policy optimization, encouraging the model to directly reinforce answer tokens rather than improve reasoning. We propose OM-GRPO, a label-free RLVR framework that decouples reward estimation from policy optimization. OM-GRPO masks gradients on the answer span while retaining answer-level rewards through a soft consensus signal, shifting optimization pressure away from answer tokens. We further introduce Contrast-Augmented Reward, which refines reward estimation via low-cost pairwise comparisons over existing trajectories without additional rollouts. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks and three LLM backbones, OM-GRPO consistently outperforms existing label-free RLVR methods and matches supervised GT-reward training with stable optimization. This stability is particularly beneficial in the Test-Time Training setting, where OM-GRPO surpasses majority voting by 4.24 points.

</details>


### [246] [Beyond Average Performance: Dynamic Instance Clustering and Specialized Algorithm Design in LLM-Assisted Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03129)
*Qinglong Hu,Qingfu Zhang,Fei Liu,Xialiang Tong,Kun Mao,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DyCA框架，一种基于动态实例聚类和专门算法设计的进化搜索方法，用于在异构实例分布下构建可靠的算法组合，提升尾部鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型辅助进化搜索（LES）方法主要优化平均性能，导致搜索方向偏向贡献最大的实例，而忽视其他实例，造成尾部鲁棒性差和实际可靠性受限。

Method: DyCA将实例聚类作为搜索过程中的协同进化组件，利用累积的评估数据作为无特征信号，逐步将具有相似算法响应模式的实例分组，将混合目标分解为多个结构感知子目标，从而为专门的算法设计提供更细粒度和自适应的指导。

Result: 在四个异构实例的算法设计任务上，DyCA优于最先进的LES基线，尾部鲁棒性平均提升15.2%，整体性能提升7.1%，同时保持有竞争力的头部性能。

Conclusion: DyCA通过动态实例聚类和专门算法设计的结合，有效解决了LES方法在异构实例下忽视尾部实例的问题，实现了更可靠的算法组合构建。

Abstract: Large Language Model-assisted Evolutionary Search (LES) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for automated algorithm design. However, existing LES methods primarily optimize for average performance, inherently directing search effort toward instances that contribute most to this metric while leaving others poorly served, resulting in weak tail robustness and limited real-world reliability. To address this limitation, we propose Dynamic Instance Clustering and Specialized Algorithm Design (DyCA), an LES framework with a feature-free, structure-aware mechanism for constructing reliable algorithm portfolios under heterogeneous instance distributions. DyCA treats instance clustering as a co-evolving component within the search process, reusing accumulated evaluation data as feature-free signals to progressively partition instances with similar algorithmic response patterns. The uncovered clusters decompose the mixed objective into a set of structure-aware sub-objectives, thereby enabling finer-grained and more adaptive guidance for specialized algorithm design. Experimental results across four algorithm design tasks with heterogeneous instances demonstrate that DyCA outperforms state-of-the-art LES baselines, improving tail robustness by an average of 15.2\% and overall performance by 7.1\% while maintaining competitive head performance.

</details>


### [247] [Verifiable Memory: Learning Unified Memory Management with Local and Global Verifiers for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03137)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VerMem框架，将长时记忆、活动上下文和情节历史表示为三种不同状态，用一个记忆操作策略统一控制，并通过三阶段强化学习课程训练，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别优化长期记忆和短期记忆，而统一策略主要依赖轨迹级反馈训练，导致对单个记忆决策的信用分配薄弱，无法有效支撑长时交互中LLM智能体对可复用信息的管理需求。

Method: VerMem将LTM、活动上下文和情节历史建模为独立状态，由单一记忆操作策略控制七种原子操作（添加、修订、软删除LTM条目；检索LTM到活动上下文；过滤或总结活动上下文；恢复选定情节片段）。先通过监督微调初始化，再使用三阶段强化学习课程训练。局部验证器评分可执行记忆转换，全局验证器评估证据连贯性和任务完成后的终端记忆一致性，并结合任务、证据回忆、效率和约束信号进行分层信用分配。

Result: 在五个基准和两个LLM骨干上，VerMem在绝大多数报告指标上取得最佳结果，持续优于强记忆基线。在三个交互基准的受控在线token预算下，实现了最强效率-性能前沿。

Conclusion: VerMem通过统一记忆操作策略和分层验证器强化学习训练，有效解决了长时交互中记忆管理的信用分配问题，在性能和效率上均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain reusable information, control a bounded active context, and recover earlier evidence during long-horizon interaction. Existing methods commonly optimize long-term memory (LTM) and short-term memory (STM) separately, while unified policies are often trained primarily with trajectory-level feedback, which provides weak credit for individual memory decisions. We present Verifiable Memory (VerMem), a framework that represents LTM, active context, and episodic history as distinct states and controls them with one memory operation policy. Seven atomic operations let the policy add, revise, or soft-delete LTM entries; retrieve LTM into the active context; filter or summarize the active context; and restore selected episodic fragments. VerMem is initialized by supervised fine-tuning and trained with a three-stage reinforcement-learning curriculum. The local verifier scores executable memory transitions, and a global verifier assesses evidence coherence and terminal-memory consistency after task completion. These scores are combined with programmatically computed task, evidence-recall, efficiency, and constraint signals through hierarchical credit assignment. The verifiers are used only during training. Across five benchmarks and two LLM backbones, VerMem achieves the best result on the vast majority of reported metrics and consistently outperforms strong memory baselines. Under controlled online-token budgets on three interactive benchmarks, it also achieves the strongest efficiency--performance frontier among the compared methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sun-SYSU-24/VerMem.

</details>


### [248] [Spatial proteomics guided by H&E-based AI reveals recurrence-risk niches in triple-negative breast cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03145)
*Yesung Cho,Ji Hwan Park,Chanil Kim,Hyewon Kim,Honglan Li,Yumin Lee,Geongyu Lee,Sujeong Hong,Seong Min Park,Yoonyoung Lee,Hee Sool Rho,Sumin Lee,Amos Chungwon Lee,Changhwan Lee,Hwanyoung Shim,Hyunwook Kim,Hyeji Shin,Sanha Park,Jihoon Yu,Yoon Hee Shin,Sooheon Kim,Hyunjin Park,Seung Min Park,Sangwan Kim,Yujung Kim,Sung-Im Do,Eun-Young Kim,Dongmyung Shin,Jongbae Park,In-Gu Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个结局驱动的空间病理学框架，将AI生成的复发风险热图与质谱空间蛋白质组学相结合，用于三阴性乳腺癌（TNBC）。在156名患者队列中，基于分布的高风险区域评分实现了AUC 0.77和C-index 0.77。通过对46个AI定义肿瘤区域的物理分离和空间蛋白质组分析，发现高风险区域富集有丝分裂程序，低风险区域富集免疫和抗原呈递程序。一个13蛋白复合评分与H&E风险评分结合后，将out-of-bag C-index从0.679提升至0.739，并在验证队列中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型可以从H&E染色切片预测癌症复发，但其预测背后所对应的局部分子状态仍不清楚。作者旨在将AI预测结果与空间蛋白质组学结合，揭示预后形态学背后的分子机制，并推动多尺度生物标志物发现。

Method: 作者开发了结局驱动的空间病理学框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 使用AI生成复发风险热图；(2) 采用基于分布聚集的高风险区域评分（156名患者队列训练，独立测试队列验证）；(3) 利用热图作为坐标级指南，物理分离并质谱分析46个AI定义的肿瘤区域（来自2名复发患者），进行空间蛋白质组学分析，并构建13蛋白复合评分。

Result: 分布聚集方法在独立测试队列中达到AUC 0.77和C-index 0.77；空间蛋白质组学显示高风险区域富集有丝分裂程序，低风险区域富集免疫/抗原呈递程序；13蛋白复合评分在扩展队列中显示较差的复发-free生存趋势，其转录本复合评分在METABRIC TNBC队列中分层有效；与H&E风险评分整合后C-index从0.679提升到0.739，并增强了3年和5年的时间依赖性判别。

Conclusion: 结局训练的AI模型可作为空间明确的实验指南，将预后形态学与局部分子状态连接起来，揭示了超越组织隔室身份的瘤内异质性，并推动了生物学驱动的、多尺度TNBC生物标志物发现。

Abstract: Deep learning models can predict cancer recurrence from H&E stained slides, but the localized molecular states underlying these predictions remain largely obscured. Here, we developed an outcome informed spatial pathology framework in TNBC that integrates AI generated recurrence risk heatmaps with mass spectrometry based spatial proteomics. In a cohort of 156 patients, distribution based aggregation of high scoring patches achieved an AUC of 0.77 and a C-index of 0.77 in an independent test cohort. Bulk proteomics associated high image derived risk with cell cycle and genome maintenance programs and low risk with immune activation. High and low risk patches coexisted within the same tumor compartment and displayed distinct nuclear and architectural features, revealing intratumoral heterogeneity beyond tissue compartment identity. We then used the heatmaps as coordinate level guides to physically isolate and profile 46 AI defined tumor regions from two recurrence patients. Spatial proteomic profiling revealed a concordant molecular contrast across both patients: mitotic programs were enriched in high risk regions and immune and antigen presentation programs in low risk regions. A 13 protein composite derived from these spatial contrasts showed a trend toward poorer recurrence-free survival with increasing scores in an expanded cohort, while the corresponding transcript based composite stratified recurrence free survival in the independent METABRIC TNBC cohort. Integrating the protein composite with the H&E derived risk score improved the out of bag C-index from 0.679 to 0.739 and enhanced time dependent discrimination at 3 and 5 years. Together, these findings define a new role for outcome trained AI models as spatially explicit experimental guides that connect prognostic morphology with localized molecular states and advance biologically grounded, multiscale biomarker discovery in TNBC.

</details>


### [249] [UniGD: A Unified Generative-Discriminative Framework for Industrial Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03150)
*Shujie Ji,Yawei Kong,Yilin Zhao,Li Wang,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UniGD框架，将生成式检索与判别式相关性打分统一到单一模型中，通过CAGE缓解梯度冲突、CAM提供语义先验、HAM建模异构广告素材，在快手搜索广告平台上线后广告收入提升5.78%、推理延迟降低33%，并在公开数据集上显著提升Recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索在工业搜索广告中部署受限于严格的相关性和延迟要求。现有系统将GR与独立的判别式相关性模型级联，导致生成式似然目标与查询-广告相关性判别解耦，既损害效果又增加服务成本。

Method: 提出Unified Generative-Discriminative (UniGD)框架，在单一模型中集成检索与相关性打分。设计了Conflict-Aware Gradient Enhancement (CAGE)自适应协调两个优化目标的梯度干扰；提出Codebook-Anchored Representation Module (CAM)，将物品表示锚定到从多模态预训练模型蒸馏的冻结分层码本上，赋予丰富可泛化的语义先验；针对短视频、商品、直播等异构广告，提出Heterogeneous Ad-material Modeling (HAM)，在共享骨干网络捕获跨类型语义共性同时保留类型特定建模能力。

Result: 在快手搜索广告平台的在线A/B测试中，UniGD使广告收入提升5.78%，推理延迟降低33%，并改善判别式相关性估计。在NQ320K和MS300K数据集上，Recall@10分别比最强复现的生成式检索基线提升8.44%和3.19%。

Conclusion: UniGD通过统一生成与判别目标、模块化设计（CAGE、CAM、HAM）有效解决了工业搜索广告中生成式检索的相关性和延迟瓶颈，在效果和效率上均取得显著提升，验证了该框架在工业场景中的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) is a promising paradigm for industrial search advertising, yet its deployment is constrained by strict relevance and latency requirements. Existing systems cascade GR with an independent relevance model, decoupling the generative likelihood objective from query-ad relevance discrimination, which compromises effectiveness and increases serving costs. We propose a Unified Generative-Discriminative framework (UniGD) that integrates retrieval and relevance scoring within a single model. To mitigate gradient interference in joint optimization, UniGD introduces Conflict-Aware Gradient Enhancement (CAGE) to adaptively coordinate the two objectives. UniGD further designs a Codebook-Anchored Representation Module (CAM) that anchors item representations to frozen hierarchical codebooks distilled from a multimodal pretrained model, thereby endowing them with rich and generalizable semantic priors. For heterogeneous short-video, product, and live-stream ads, UniGD proposes Heterogeneous Ad-material Modeling (HAM), which captures cross-type semantic commonality over a shared backbone while preserving type-specific modeling capacity. Online AB tests on Kuaishou search advertising platform show that UniGD raises ad revenue by 5.78%, reduces inference latency by 33%, and improves discriminative relevance estimation. On NQ320K and MS300K, UniGD improves Recall@10 over the strongest reproduced GR baseline by 8.44% and 3.19%, respectively.

</details>


### [250] [Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Knowledge Graph Construction for Multi-Lecture Educational Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03161)
*Sahil Al Farib,Momota Ahsana Meem,Sheikh Redwanul Islam,Md. Tanvir Raihan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的多模态流水线，用于从讲座视频中构建具有来源可追溯性的知识图谱。该流水线结合语音转录、OCR和视觉语言模型，仅提取有证据支持的概念和关系，并通过验证和规范化生成知识图谱。在三个神经网络讲座上测试，保留了1,022个概念提及和312个关系提及，最终得到172个规范概念和282个关系，端点覆盖率达90.38%，初步检索测试中top-1和top-3准确率均为100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲座视频中的知识分布在语音、幻灯片文本、图表、公式和演示顺序等多种模态中，仅依赖转录文本的检索无法完整保留这些知识。

Method: 提出一个证据驱动的多模态流水线：（1）转写讲座语音；（2）选择语义锚点；（3）应用光学字符识别（OCR）；（4）使用视觉语言模型提取仅由转录、OCR或视觉证据支持的概念和类型化关系；（5）对提及进行验证和规范化，构建具有来源信息的知识图谱。

Result: 在三个神经网络讲座上，流水线处理了3,118帧、756个转录片段和559个锚点，保留了1,022个概念提及和312个关系提及，生成172个规范概念和282个关系，端点覆盖率达90.38%。三项问题的初步检索测试达到100%的top-1和top-3准确率以及100%的平均top-5召回率。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于提供了一种可审计的知识图谱构建方法，而非追求最先进的性能表现。

Abstract: Lecture videos distribute knowledge across speech, slide text, diagrams, equations, and presentation order, which transcript-only retrieval does not fully preserve. This paper presents an evidence-grounded multimodal pipeline that transcribes lectures, selects semantic anchors, applies optical character recognition (OCR), and uses a vision-language model to extract only concepts and typed relationships supported by transcript, OCR, or visual evidence. Mentions are validated and canonicalized into a provenance-rich knowledge graph. On three neural-network lectures, the pipeline processed 3,118 frames, 756 transcript segments, and 559 anchors. It retained 1,022 concept and 312 relationship mentions, yielding 172 canonical concepts and 282 relationships with 90.38% endpoint coverage. A preliminary three question retrieval test achieved 100% top-1 and top-3 accuracy and 100% mean top-5 recall. The contribution is an auditable construction method rather than a state-of-the-art performance claim.

</details>


### [251] [Adversarial Stress Testing of Role-Playing Language Agents using Multi-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03166)
*Saqib Shouqi,Abdullah Nazly,Januki Wanniarachchi,Ravisha De Alwis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化多智能体平台，通过六种渐进式对抗策略对角色扮演语言智能体（RPLAs）进行多轮对话压力测试。实验表明多策略对抗评估能暴露单策略测试无法发现的失败模式，并开源该平台以支持AI安全与可复现的RPLA基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖静态基准或孤立的单轮提示，无法捕捉RPLAs在长时间交互中累积出现的行为失败。随着RPLAs被部署到医疗保健、客户支持、教育等高风险场景，亟需一种能够进行系统性对抗压力测试的评估框架。

Method: 构建一个模块化多智能体平台，包含三个协作智能体：采用六种渐进式对抗策略的审问者智能体（Interrogator）、代表被测RPLA的目标智能体（Target）、以及自动评分智能体（Judging）。评分维度包括角色保真度、行为漂移、伦理偏差和一致性。实验覆盖三个人设和三个LLM家族，并与人工评分进行对齐验证。

Result: 多策略对抗评估使鲁棒性分数平均降低0.17-0.20分，揭示了单策略测试不可见的失败模式。在Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-4o-mini和Claude-3.5-Haiku上观察到一致的退化模式，其中权威挑战和情感操纵是最有效的攻击策略。自动评分与人类评分强对齐（r=0.82，Fleiss κ=0.71）。

Conclusion: 该平台为RPLAs的系统性失败模式发现提供了可行框架，促进了AI安全评估的可复现性。尽管对抗性测试方法存在潜在伦理风险，但作者强调负责任使用该框架以提升AI安全性。

Abstract: Role-Playing Language Agents (RPLAs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications such as healthcare assistance, customer support, and education, where maintaining consistent personas, ethical constraints, and behavioral coherence under adversarial pressure is critical. Existing evaluation approaches rely on static benchmarks or isolated single-turn prompts that fail to capture cumulative behavioral failures emerging over extended interactions.
  We present a modular multi-agent platform for adversarially stress-testing RPLAs through structured, multi-turn dialogue. The system coordinates three agents: a strategy-driven Interrogator Agent that applies six progressive adversarial strategies, a Target Agent representing the RPLA under evaluation, and an automated Judging Agent that scores behavior across role fidelity, drift, ethical deviation, and consistency dimensions.
  Through experiments across three personas and three LLM families, we demonstrate that multi-strategy adversarial evaluation reveals failure modes invisible to single-strategy testing, reducing overall robustness scores by 0.17--0.20 points on average. Cross-model validation confirms consistent degradation patterns across Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Claude-3.5-Haiku, with Authority Challenge and Emotional Manipulation emerging as the most effective attack strategies. Automated judging achieves strong human alignment ($r = 0.82$, Fleiss' $κ= 0.71$). This work is released as an open-source platform to support AI safety and reproducible RPLA benchmarking. While the framework enables systematic discovery of failure modes, we acknowledge potential ethical risks associated with adversarial testing methodologies and emphasize responsible usage for improving AI safety.

</details>


### [252] [Surrogate Substitution Preserves PHI Detectability: A Multi-Detector Equivalence Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03172)
*Qiming Bao,Sherry J. H. Feng,Kim Chester Eugenio,Meng Fon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种配对多检测器的评估协议，用于检验结构保持的去标识化（用同类型替代词替换受保护健康信息）是否会被下游PHI检测器察觉。基于11个检测器、7个基准、7种语言、1750份文档、57k配对跨度的实验中，掩码跨度上的召回率从76.1%变为74.9%，等价检验表明该变化在±2%边际内统计等价于零（p~3e-9），检测器排名保持不变。残余损失源于畸形和分布外替代词，而非检测器本身能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构保持的去标识化以真实同类型替代词替换PHI（如"Anna S."变成"Maria S."而非[NAME]），以保持文本流畅性和下游工具可用性。但关键问题是：替代本身是否破坏了下游工具的依赖信号？作者提出了一个狭窄可检验的问题：在被掩码的跨度上，下游PHI检测器能否发现替代词？现有评估方法未能解耦覆盖率和效用的关系，且在大样本量下使用零假设显著性检验信息量不足。

Method: 提出配对多检测器评估协议：(i) 仅在掩码跨度上评分效用，解耦覆盖率与效用；(ii) 使用等价检验（TOST）而非零假设显著性检验，因为57k配对跨度的大样本量使显著性检验无信息量；(iii) 构建替代失败类型学，区分可修复的生成器缺陷与检测器固有局限。此外，引入红action底线和开源替代基线来验证效应是否为结构良好替代的普遍属性。

Result: 在11个检测器、7个基准、7种语言（1750份文档）上，掩码跨度召回率从76.1%变为74.9%，等价检验显示变化在±2%边际内统计等价于零（p~3e-9），且检测器排名保持不变。残余损失集中在畸形替代词（如截断）和分布外替代词（如显著性丢失），而非检测器对PHI识别能力下降。

Conclusion: 结构保持的去标识化不会显著损害下游PHI检测器的性能——检测器在掩码跨度上的表现变化统计等价于零。残余性能损失源于替代词质量问题而非检测器能力问题。作者开源了评估子集、评分代码和交互式仪表板，使该协议可审计任何结构保持的转换。

Abstract: Structure-preserving de-identification replaces protected health information (PHI) with realistic same-type surrogates -- "Anna S." becomes "Maria S.", not [NAME] -- so that clinical text stays fluent and downstream tools keep working. But this only helps if the substitution does not itself corrupt the signal those tools rely on. We ask a narrow, testable question: on the spans a de-identifier actually masks, can downstream PHI detectors still find the surrogate? We introduce a paired, multi-detector evaluation protocol that (i) scores utility only on masked spans, decoupling coverage from utility; (ii) uses equivalence testing (TOST) rather than null-hypothesis significance testing, which is uninformative at our sample size (57k paired spans); and (iii) builds a surrogate-failure typology separating fixable generator defects from intrinsic detector limits. Across 11 detectors, 7 benchmarks, and 7 languages (1,750 documents), recall on masked spans moves from 76.1% to 74.9% -- a change our equivalence test shows is statistically equivalent to zero within a +/-2-point margin (p ~ 3e-9), with detector ranking preserved. The residual loss does not reflect detectors getting worse at PHI: it concentrates in malformed and out-of-distribution surrogates (truncation Chicago -> Illino, salience loss Cedars-Sinai -> Vidant). A redaction floor and an open-source surrogate baseline indicate the effect is a property of well-formed substitution, not of one tool. We release the evaluation subsets, scoring code, and an interactive dashboard at https://custodianai.pages.dev so the protocol can audit any structure-preserving transform.

</details>


### [253] [Diversity is Not Ambiguity: Toward Accurate and Efficient Ambiguity Detection for Open-Domain QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03177)
*Jiwon Lee,Yong-chan Park,Jungin Hong,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARCHIVE框架，通过逻辑冲突检测来识别开放域问答中的歧义查询，并构建了QuireQA基准数据集，在准确性和效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有歧义检测方法将答案多样性与歧义混为一谈，导致预测不准确；同时对所有查询统一处理，造成计算浪费。需要一种既能准确区分歧义又能高效运行的检测框架。

Method: ARCHIVE采用两阶段级联架构：轻量级早退编码器处理表面可检测的案例，冲突推理模块对答案间的逻辑关系进行建模；并引入不变性目标增强对噪声答案集的鲁棒性。

Result: 在构建的QuireQA基准（4,703个查询）上，ARCHIVE相比竞争对手在F1-amb上最高提升10.4%，在F1-unamb上最高提升21.6%，同时比最佳竞争对手快16倍。

Conclusion: 通过逻辑冲突而非答案多样性来判断查询歧义是更准确且高效的方式，级联架构有效平衡了性能与计算成本。

Abstract: How can question answering (QA) systems determine whether a query is ambiguous? Ambiguity detection is essential in open-domain QA, as misclassification leads to answering the wrong interpretation or unnecessary clarification. However, existing methods conflate answer diversity with ambiguity, leading to inaccurate predictions. They also process queries uniformly, resulting in wasteful computation. We propose ARCHIVE (Ambiguity Recognition via Cascaded Hypothesis Inspection and Conflict Verification), an accurate and efficient framework that detects ambiguity via logical conflict: a query is ambiguous when its valid answers cannot all be true under a single interpretation. ARCHIVE combines a lightweight early-exit encoder for surface-detectable cases with a conflict reasoning module that models logical relations among answers, reinforced by an invariance objective for robustness to noisy answer sets. We present QuireQA, a 4,703-query benchmark spanning factoid, non-factoid, and ill-formed queries. Experiments show ARCHIVE outperforms competitors, improving F1-amb by up to 10.4% and F1-unamb by up to 21.6%, while operating 16$\times$ faster than the best competitor.

</details>


### [254] [TumorBoard: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Decision Support for Longitudinal Neuro-Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03190)
*Yantong Liu,Zheyu Zhang,Runpeng Liu,Mu Xitang,Seong-Yoon Shin,Hyun-Ae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TumorBoard，一个基于多智能体协作的神经肿瘤学决策支持系统，通过共享纵向病例状态和可审计的声明-证据账本，整合放射学、病理学、分子诊断、指南和治疗规划等专科智能体。系统在360例隐藏基准测试中达到行动F1 0.772和证据蕴含0.914，较最强基线提升3.1个百分点（95% CI: 1.6-4.7, p=0.0012），并在证据缺失时将84.2%的不安全案例推迟决策，有害推荐率限制在5.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经肿瘤学决策需要协调解读多种异构信息源（系列MRI、病理、分子标志物、治疗史、体能状态和不断演进的指南），单一模型或简单集成难以保证决策的准确性和安全性，亟需结构化的多智能体协调决策支持系统。

Method: 该系统围绕共享纵向病例状态和可审计的声明-证据账本构建：①专科智能体（放射学、神经病理学、分子诊断、指南、治疗规划）产生带来源的原子声明；②对抗性批评者揭露声明间矛盾；③安全治理器根据证据充分性和时间有效性对推荐进行发布、限定或推迟处理。

Result: 在360例隐藏基准（匹配token预算）上，行动F1达0.772，证据蕴含0.914；超过最强typed-council基线3.1个百分点（95% CI: 1.6-4.7，调整后p=0.0012）；推荐-证据覆盖率0.927。证据删除场景下，84.2%不安全案例被推迟，有害推荐率5.8%；安全治理器将有害发布降低7.8个百分点，误推迟代价4.3个百分点。消融实验证实账本、批评者和治理器的设计价值。

Conclusion: 结构化协调是多智能体优势的来源，TumorBoard通过可审计的账本设计、对抗性批评和安全治理机制，在神经肿瘤学决策中实现了较基线更优的性能与安全性，为临床决策支持系统的多智能体架构提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Neuro-oncology decisions require coordinated interpretation of serial MRI, pathology, molecular markers, treatment history, performance status, and evolving guidelines. We present TumorBoard, a multi-agent decision-support system built around a shared longitudinal case state and an auditable claim-evidence ledger. Specialist agents for radiology, neuropathology, molecular diagnosis, guidelines, and therapy planning produce atomic claims with provenance. An adversarial critic exposes contradictions, and a safety governor releases, qualifies, or defers recommendations according to evidence sufficiency and temporal validity. On a 360-case hidden benchmark at a matched token budget, TumorBoard achieved an action F1 of 0.772 and evidence entailment of 0.914. It exceeded the strongest typed-council baseline by 3.1 percentage points (95% CI: 1.6 to 4.7, adjusted p = 0.0012), while recommendation-to-evidence coverage reached 0.927. Under evidence deletion, the system deferred 84.2% of unsafe cases and limited harmful recommendations to 5.8%. The safety governor reduced harmful release by 7.8 percentage points at a false-deferral cost of 4.3 percentage points. Ablation studies of the ledger, critic, and governor produced the predicted failure patterns, establishing structured coordination as the source of the measured multi-agent advantage.

</details>


### [255] [When Refusal Looks Safe: The Refusal-Cue Shortcut in Safety Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03201)
*Yu Feng,Chunting Zang,Chen Shen,Rui Miao,Ge Teng,Weidong Cai,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了WildGuardMix和GR-Train两个安全防护训练数据集，发现拒绝表达与无害标签几乎共现，导致模型产生"拒绝线索捷径"：在有害回答中插入拒绝语可翻转防护模型判定。该捷径影响LlamaGuard3和Qwen3Guard等官方模型。作者提出稀疏互补掩码作为轻量级事后干预方法，抑制少量与捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元，在不重训练的情况下将响应初始检测失败降低约79%，且该抑制效果可迁移至未见位置和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全防护模型广泛用于过滤有害内容，但现有训练数据中拒绝表达与无害标签的强共现关系可能导致模型学习到捷径（shortcut），即仅凭拒绝线索而非内容本身判断安全性，这严重威胁防护模型的有效性和可靠性。

Method: 采用稀疏互补掩码（sparse complementary masking）作为事后干预手段，识别并抑制与捷径关联的少量注意力头和MLP神经元，无需重新训练模型即可消除拒绝线索捷径的影响。

Result: 在两个主要基准上，该方法将拒绝线索诱导的响应初始检测失败相对降低约79%，同时保持标准检测性能。抑制效果可迁移到未见位置和数据集。进一步分析表明捷径依赖与合法拒绝识别在功能上部分可分离。

Conclusion: 安全防护模型的训练数据偏见会导致拒绝线索捷径，该问题广泛存在于多个防护模型中。轻量级事后干预可有效缓解此问题，且捷径与合法拒绝识别在内部功能上部分分离，为后续针对性修复提供了方向。

Abstract: Safety guards are widely used to filter harmful content and are typically trained via supervised fine-tuning on labeled prompt-response pairs. We audit two widely used safety-guard training datasets, WildGuardMix and GR-Train, and find that among responses to harmful prompts, refusal expressions co-occur almost exclusively with unharmful labels. This imbalance motivates what we term the refusal-cue shortcut: inserting a refusal cue into a harmful response could flip the guard's verdict from harmful to unharmful. The shortcut affects not only guards trained on these datasets but also officially released models such as LlamaGuard3 and Qwen3Guard whose training data is undisclosed. It persists across response positions and is generally stronger in smaller variants within a family. To mitigate it, we adapt sparse complementary masking as a lightweight post-hoc intervention that identifies and suppresses a small set of shortcut-associated attention heads and MLP neurons without retraining. On two primary benchmarks, the intervention achieves an approximately 79% relative reduction in response-initial detection failures induced by refusal cues, while preserving standard detection performance. Although optimized using cues at a single response position, the suppression effect transfers to unseen positions and datasets, suggesting that shortcut manifestations across positions are partly mediated by shared internal components. Further analysis provides evidence that shortcut reliance and legitimate refusal recognition are partially functionally separable, as suppressing the shortcut broadly preserves the guard's ability to recognize genuine refusals.

</details>


### [256] [The Agent Operating System (AOS): A Reference Operating Architecture for Distributed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03214)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出代理操作系统（AOS），一个供应商中立的参考操作系统架构，用于分布式代理系统，包含控制与治理平面及运行时与协调平面，旨在通过明确接口实现异构组件的可治理、可靠、可观测和可互操作组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型驱动的代理系统虽然借助各种框架和平台工具改善了执行效率，但缺乏稳定的、与具体实现无关的操作架构来统一管理意图治理、能力选择、跨委托授权、不确定性控制、运行时行为协调以及关键行动的可追溯性等核心问题。

Method: AOS提出两个内部平面：控制与治理平面（负责意图、策略、信任、授权、置信度、可审计性、可观测性和人类监督）以及运行时与协调平面（负责代理生命周期、工作流协调、模型和工具路由、上下文与记忆协调、调度、流量管理和运行时保障）。平台服务、容器运行时和物理基础设施均位于AOS边界之外，通过显式接口集成。论文详细规定了AOS的概念、不变量、接口对象、优化目标、部署配置文件和可靠性职责。

Result: 论文系统化地定义了AOS的架构要素，识别了其中的权衡取舍和未解决的研究问题，并提供了一套完整的参考架构规范。

Conclusion: AOS并非取代现有框架或基础设施，而是作为一套操作系统架构，使得异构组件能够被组合为可治理、可靠、可观测且可互操作的代理系统。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed artificial intelligence from isolated prediction services into components of long-running, distributed systems that reason, invoke tools, retrieve external state, delegate tasks, and act on behalf of users and organizations. The surrounding ecosystem has responded with agent frameworks, workflow engines, model-serving platforms, memory systems, communication protocols, and observability tools. These technologies improve execution, but they do not provide a stable, implementation-independent operating architecture for governing intent, selecting capabilities, preserving authority across delegation, controlling uncertainty, coordinating runtime behavior, and reconstructing why consequential actions occurred. This paper proposes the Agent Operating System (AOS), a vendor-neutral reference operating architecture for distributed agentic systems. AOS contains two internal planes: a Control & Governance Plane responsible for intent, policy, trust, authority, confidence, auditability, observability, and human oversight; and a Runtime & Coordination Plane responsible for agent lifecycle, workflow coordination, model and tool routing, context and memory coordination, scheduling, traffic management, and runtime assurance. Platform services, Linux or Windows, container runtimes, and physical infrastructure remain outside the AOS boundary and are integrated through explicit interfaces. The paper specifies AOS concepts, invariants, interface objects, optimization objectives, deployment profiles, and reliability responsibilities. It also identifies tradeoffs and unresolved research questions. AOS is not presented as a replacement for existing frameworks or infrastructure; it is proposed as the operating architecture through which heterogeneous components can be composed into governable, reliable, observable, and interoperable agentic systems.

</details>


### [257] [Reachability Is Not Realization: Tracing the Sources of LLM Benchmark Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03219)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yanbo Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种题目级别的审计框架，区分模型"已实现"（实际输出正确）与"可达"（在固定预算内通过特定探针能找到正确答案）的能力。研究发现推理层路由的收益绝大部分需要访问正确答案才能保留；单层MLP阻断可修复68-92%的失败；在五个配对评估中，训练提升部署分数但可达上限保持不变或下降（DAPO部署涨14.7分但可达上限降13.3分），表明能力扩展应同时报告两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试分数的提升常被视为模型能力增强的证据，但相同分数提升可能反映完全不同的行为变化——模型可能到达新答案，或只是把原本在可达范围内的答案成功输出。聚合分数无法区分这些差异，需要题目级别的审计来区分"已实现"与"可达"两种能力。

Method: 在固定预算、温度和答案格式下建立题目级审计框架。定义"已实现"为默认部署流程产生正确答案，"可达"为指定探针在固定预算内找到答案。首先测试推理时层路由能否扩展可达性，比较随机路由与结构化搜索；然后通过阻断单个MLP块测试可达但未实现的原因；最后测试训练是否通过扩展可达性来缩小差距。

Result: 在43个模型和任务设置下，随机路由匹配或超过结构化搜索。答案不可见的程序几乎完全丧失路由增益。阻断一个识别的MLP块可修复68-92%的预设失败集。五个配对评估中部署性能上升但可达上限持平或下降，DAPO部署涨14.7分而可达上限降13.3分。

Conclusion: 实现与可达性在审计的设置中并不总是同步变化。报告能力扩展时应同时提供匹配评估条件下的实现性能与可达性指标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often treated as evidence of greater LLM capability. Yet the same gain can reflect different changes in model behavior. A model may reach new answers, or produce answers that were already within reach. Aggregate scores do not distinguish these changes question by question. We establish a question-level audit under fixed budgets, temperatures, and answer formats. A question is realized when the default deployment procedure produces the correct answer. A question is reachable when a specified probe finds that answer within a fixed budget. We first test whether inference-time layer routing can expand reachability. Under a matched budget, random routes match or exceed structured search in all 43 model and task settings. Answer-blind procedures retain almost none of this gain, which instead requires access to the correct answer. We then ask why reachable answers sometimes fail to appear. Across six cases spanning 0.5B to 31B, silencing one identified MLP block repairs 68 to 92 percent of a predefined failure set. We next test whether training closes the gap by expanding reachability. In five of six matched evaluations, deployed performance rises while the reachable ceiling remains flat or falls. For DAPO, the deployed score rises by 14.7 points while the reachable ceiling falls by 13.3 points. Across the settings we audit, realization and reachability therefore do not always change together. Claims of capability expansion should report both realized performance and reachability under matched evaluation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/reachability-not-realization

</details>


### [258] [UniNav: A Unified World-Action Diffusion Model for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03244)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniNav是一个统一的"世界-动作"模型，通过单一的扩散过程同时生成未来视觉观测和连续路径轨迹，用于图像目标视觉导航任务。该模型在单一Transformer中联合去噪视觉和路径令牌，并引入几何感知相机令牌以增强空间接地。UniNav支持在带轨迹标注的导航数据和纯视频数据上训练，并提供两个变体：UniNav-Full（联合预测可解释的未来观测和轨迹）和UniNav-Fast（推理时移除未来图像令牌以提高效率）。实验表明UniNav在ATE指标上全面优于最强基线，UniNav-Fast单步推理延迟仅0.1秒且精度损失不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航策略能高效预测路径轨迹但缺乏视觉预见能力，而导航世界模型虽能预测未来观测但需要昂贵的规划推演。作者希望将两者统一，实现既能预测未来视觉又能生成动作路径的高效导航模型。

Method: UniNav采用统一的扩散过程，在单个Transformer中联合去噪视觉令牌和路径令牌。具体包括：(1) 融合历史帧和目标图像作为条件；(2) 引入几何感知相机令牌提升空间接地能力；(3) 在带轨迹标注的导航数据和仅视频数据上联合训练；(4) 提供UniNav-Full（联合预测未来观测与轨迹）和UniNav-Fast（推理时移除未来图像令牌）两个变体。

Result: 在导航基准测试中，UniNav在所有数据集上的ATE（绝对轨迹误差）指标均优于最强基线。UniNav-Fast通过单步推理实现0.1秒的低延迟，且没有显著的精度下降。

Conclusion: UniNav成功地将世界模型（未来视觉预测）与动作模型（轨迹生成）统一在单一大扩散框架中，既保留了视觉预见能力，又避免了昂贵的规划推演开销，在精度和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Image-goal visual navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied agents. Existing navigation policies efficiently predict waypoint trajectories but lack visual foresight, while navigation world models can anticipate future observations but often require costly planning rollouts. We present UniNav, a unified world-action model that generates future visual observations and continuous waypoint trajectories through a single diffusion process. Given history frames and a goal image, UniNav jointly denoises visual and waypoint tokens within a single transformer, unifying future prediction and action generation in a shared framework. To improve spatial grounding, we incorporate geometry-aware camera tokens. We also train on both trajectory-labeled navigation data and video-only data, enabling the model to benefit from diverse videos without waypoint annotations. Based on this unified framework, we introduce two variants: UniNav-Full jointly predicts interpretable future observations and their corresponding trajectories, while UniNav-Fast removes future-image tokens at inference for efficient trajectory prediction. Experiments on navigation benchmarks show that UniNav outperforms the strongest baseline in ATE across all datasets. With one-step inference, UniNav-Fast achieves a latency of 0.1s without a substantial accuracy drop. Code will be released.

</details>


### [259] [One Knob to Rule Them All: A Unified Optimal Transport View of Cold-Start Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03249)
*Ning Zhu,Xiaochuan Ma,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Mengfei Zhao,An Chen,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优传输的冷启动主动学习框架，通过熵正则化的自适应调节实现无需先验知识的高效样本选择，在六个公共数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷启动主动学习方法各依赖特定的归纳偏置（如典型性、覆盖性或多样性），导致在不同任务上表现差异大。作者认为真正挑战不在于设计新的选择启发式，而在于使方法能够自动适应数据和任务。

Method: 首先提出一个广义传输选择框架，统一了现有方法的分配结构；其次通过理论分析刻画熵正则化控制的权衡，并建立了任务无关的极小极大界；最后推导出数据自适应正则化规则，提出基于Sinkhorn的ε-自适应选择算法（ε-AS）。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和多种标注预算下进行了大量实验，ε-AS一致达到最先进性能。在ImageNet-1k上，相对于ActiveFT平均精度提升1.29%，选择时间减少56.2%。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输视角重新审视冷启动主动学习，实现了从固定启发式到数据自适应选择的范式转变，为CSAL提供了坚实的理论基础和实用的高效算法。

Abstract: Cold-Start Active Learning (CSAL) aims to select a valuable subset from an unlabeled pool without any prior knowledge or human assistance. Existing methods take diverse routes based on typicality, coverage, or diversity. Each rests on its own inductive bias and therefore performs well on some tasks yet poorly on others. We argue that the real challenge is not to design yet another selection heuristic, but to make CSAL adapt automatically to the data and task at hand. To this end, we revisit CSAL through the lens of optimal transport. First, we propose a generalized transport selection framework that reveals the shared allocation structure of existing methods and exactly subsumes representative formulations. Second, we introduce a theoretical analysis that characterizes the trade-off controlled by entropic regularization and establishes a task-agnostic minimax bound for cold-start selection. These results provide a principled foundation for adapting the regularization strength to the unlabeled data. Third, we derive a data-adaptive regularization rule and present a novel Sinkhorn-based CSAL algorithm, termed $ε$-Adaptive Selection ($ε$-AS). Extensive experiments on six public datasets and multiple annotation budgets show that $ε$-AS consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. On ImageNet-1k, it improves the average accuracy over ActiveFT by 1.29% while reducing selection time by 56.2%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/OT-CSAL

</details>


### [260] [TaskPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Compression via Task-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03276)
*Wonpyo Park,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskPress提出了一种任务引导、查询无关的KV缓存淘汰框架，通过高层任务指南作为元查询在预填充阶段过滤无关token，并利用量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测重要表示异常值，从而构建紧凑且可复用的缓存，用于长上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，现有剪枝方法需要针对特定查询确定token重要性，无法在不同查询间复用。这导致每次查询都需要重新计算或频繁更新缓存，计算开销大。

Method: TaskPress框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 任务引导的高层指南作为元查询，在预填充阶段过滤无关token，构建可复用的内存表示；(2) 利用量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测有影响力的表示异常值，作为token重要性的高效代理。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务上的实验表明，TaskPress能够高效地创建紧凑且可复用的缓存，适用于多样化的查询场景。

Conclusion: TaskPress验证了任务引导、查询无关的KV缓存淘汰方法的有效性，通过将缓存优化从单查询层面提升到任务层面，实现了跨查询的缓存复用，为长上下文推理的效率优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-context inference with large language models is constrained by the linear growth of the key-value cache to sequence length. While pruning offers mitigation, prevailing methods determine query-specific token importance that cannot be reused across unseen queries. In contrast, we introduce TaskPress, a framework for task-guided, query-agnostic KV cache eviction. Instead of optimizing the cache for a single query, TaskPress constructs a reusable memory representation conditioned on a high-level task guide. The guide functions as a meta-query during prefill to filter irrelevant tokens before downstream queries are issued. In addition, TaskPress leverages quantization scale factors as a zero-cost signal for detecting influential representation outliers, providing an efficient proxy for token importance. Experiments on conducted on various tasks with long context input demonstrate that TaskPress efficiently creates a compact, reusable cache across diverse queries.

</details>


### [261] [AgentPanel: Toward a New Paradigm for Human--AI Collaboration in Exploring Scientific Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03283)
*Zhiyao Cui,Qianyi Wang,Haoyang Yan,Yiqun Zhang,Siyue Ren,Hangfan Zhang,Zelin Tan,Hao Li,Chunjiang Mu,Dexian Cai,Shao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Meng Li,Jianan Chai,Yuting Fan,Zichao Ye,Xiaolei Yang,Xinyao Lu,Yuyang Yu,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Shiyang Feng,Mao Su,Qiaosheng Zhang,Bo Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentPanel是一个面向人机协同科学探索的多智能体论坛平台，通过异构智能体异步讨论科学问题，支持研究者提问、浏览和组织候选想法、与智能体互动及生成总结报告。离线实验表明其优于集中式多智能体辩论基线，人类研究显示65%的参与者认为其优于常用LLM工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研人员在探索科学想法时，传统的小组讨论或与单个大语言模型交互只能提供有限的观点和方向，缺乏多样化的视角来帮助发现有前景的科学思路。

Method: 提出AgentPanel多智能体论坛系统，让异构智能体以论坛形式异步讨论科学问题；研究者可提交问题、浏览和组织候选想法、与智能体进行后续互动，并可生成总结报告。通过离线实验和20名参与者的人类研究进行多维度评估。

Result: 离线实验中AgentPanel优于集中式多智能体辩论基线；人类研究中用户在观点多样性和探索支持方面给予积极评价；65%的参与者认为AgentPanel在研究方向广度和早期探索适用性方面优于常用LLM工具。

Conclusion: AgentPanel通过多智能体论坛式协作有效增强了科学探索的想法质量、探索广度和交互效率，为早期科研探索提供了一种实用的新范式。

Abstract: Identifying promising scientific ideas remains an important challenge in research practice. Researchers commonly rely on small-group discussions or one-to-one interactions with a single large language model, yet these approaches often expose them to only a limited range of perspectives and directions. We present AgentPanel, a multi-agent forum for human--AI collaboration in scientific exploration. Heterogeneous agents asynchronously discuss scientific questions in a forum-style environment, while researchers can submit questions, browse and organize candidate ideas, engage agents in follow-up interactions, and optionally generate post-hoc summary reports. We evaluate AgentPanel in terms of idea quality, exploration breadth, interaction effectiveness, candidate-selection efficiency, and practical utility. Offline experiments show that AgentPanel outperforms a centralized multi-agent debate baseline. A human study with 20 participants further shows that users value AgentPanel for perspective diversity and exploration support. In experience-based comparisons with commonly used LLM tools, 65\% of participants favored AgentPanel for both breadth of research directions and overall suitability for early-stage exploration. The platform is publicly available at https://agentpanel.cc/.

</details>


### [262] [DocTrace: Towards Traceable Long Document VQA via Hierarchical Evidence Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03292)
*Le Xiang,Zhicheng Guan,Hong Chen,Xiaocong Lin,Zhenghua Lei,Teng Hu,Bolei He,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocTrace将长文档视觉问答建模为显式证据图推理问题，通过分层的证据定位、结构化文档解析和证据图推理框架，结合两阶段训练（联合SFT+任务特定GRPO），在三个基准上显著超越现有开源和专有MLLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档VQA方法（端到端MLLM、RAG管道、文档代理）缺乏在推理过程中表示和验证接地证据如何逐步组合的显式机制，限制了答案准确性和可追溯性。

Method: 提出DocTrace分层框架，逐步执行证据定位、结构化文档解析和证据图推理以实现显式证据溯源。训练采用两阶段：首先通过联合监督微调（SFT）初始化证据定位和图推理能力，然后使用任务特定的GRPO（组相对策略优化）配合专门奖励进一步优化。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL和SlideVQA三个基准上，DocTrace一致优于现有开源基线和专有MLLM。相比Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct骨干，分别实现了14.4、11.3和11.7个百分点的绝对改进。

Conclusion: DocTrace不仅取得竞争性性能，还构建了具有显式节点级溯源的可追踪证据图，实现长文档理解中透明且可验证的推理。

Abstract: Long Document Visual Question Answering (LongDocVQA) requires Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to locate, integrate, and reason over heterogeneous document elements distributed across multiple pages. Existing approaches, including end-to-end MLLMs, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines, and document agents, often lack explicit mechanisms to represent and verify how grounded evidence is progressively composed during reasoning, limiting both answer accuracy and traceability. In this paper, we cast LongDocVQA as an explicit evidence graph reasoning problem rather than implicit answer prediction. To this end, we propose DocTrace, a hierarchical framework that progressively performs evidence localization, structured document parsing, and evidence graph reasoning to enable explicit evidence provenance. To effectively learn these capabilities, we develop a two-stage training framework: joint Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) first initializes evidence localization and graph reasoning abilities, followed by task-specific Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with dedicated rewards to further optimize these capabilities. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench-Doc, LongDocURL, and SlideVQA demonstrate that DocTrace consistently outperforms both existing open-source baselines and proprietary MLLMs. Compared with the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DocTrace achieves absolute improvements of 14.4, 11.3, and 11.7 points on the three benchmarks, respectively. Beyond competitive performance, DocTrace constructs traceable evidence graphs with explicit node-level provenance, enabling transparent and verifiable reasoning for long document understanding.

</details>


### [263] [Distractor-Aware Truncation: Disentangling Context-Length Effects from Signal Loss in Long-Context LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03297)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了"保留相关信息时更短上下文更优"这一普遍假设，对比了朴素截断（删除中间内容）和干扰物感知截断（仅删除无关内容）两种协议在长上下文基准上的效果。结果表明朴素截断导致性能崩塌，而干扰物感知截断下性能得到保持或提升，从而质疑了以往关于上下文长度效应的研究结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，检索增强和记忆增强语言模型领域有一个标准论断：在保留相关信息的前提下，更短的上下文更好。作者想严格检验这一主张，因为以往研究可能隐含地采用了有缺陷的截断方法——即直接删除提示词中间的内容，而非智能地区分任务相关内容与干扰内容。

Method: 在两个长上下文基准（BABILong和GraphWalks BFS）上，对所有样本在四个上下文保留比例（100%、75%、50%、25%）下运行测试，使用两种截断协议：朴素协议（删除中间内容）和干扰物感知协议（识别任务相关内容并仅删除其余部分）。评估了Claude家族的三个尺寸模型（Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7）以及跨提供商的GPT-5.5，并将相同协议应用于MRCR v2和Oolong两个额外基准。使用配对Wilcoxon检验和Holm校正进行统计分析。

Result: 在朴素截断下，所有八个BABILong和GraphWalks测试单元中分数单调崩塌（Holm校正后p_adj < 0.05）。在干扰物感知协议下，性能保持或提升：两个较小的Claude模型在BABILong上显示出统计显著的增益，较大的模型（Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5）保持在全上下文上限水平。GPT-5.5上复现了朴素崩塌和干扰物感知恢复，排除了单一提供商的人工产物。在25%保留率下，朴素协议中答案相关内容存活的样本不到1%。

Conclusion: 朴素截断协议并非测量上下文窗口效应，而是测量"中间删除恰好放过答案"的频率。未来关于上下文长度影响的研究必须明确说明如何区分信号与干扰物，否则其结果在两种相反假设之间是模糊的。

Abstract: A standard claim in the literature on retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented language models is that shorter context is better when the relevant information is preserved. We test this claim by running every sample of two long-context benchmarks -- BABILong and GraphWalks (BFS) -- at four context-retention fractions (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%) under two truncation protocols. The first is the naive protocol implicitly used in much prior work: drop content from the middle of the prompt. The second is distractor-aware: identify the task-relevant content for each sample and drop only the rest. We evaluate three sizes of the Claude family (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.7) and, to test cross-provider generality, GPT-5.5 from a different provider; we apply the same protocol to two further benchmarks (MRCR v2, Oolong). Under naive truncation, score collapses monotonically (paired Wilcoxon, Holm-corrected p_adj < 0.05 in all eight BABILong and GraphWalks cells). Under the distractor-aware protocol -- which preserves the signal by construction -- performance is preserved or improves: the two smaller Claude models show statistically significant gains on BABILong, while the larger models (Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) sit at their full-context ceiling. The naive collapse and its distractor-aware recovery replicate on GPT-5.5, ruling out a single-provider artifact. The mechanism is direct: under the naive protocol the answer-bearing content survives in fewer than 1% of samples at 25% retention; under the distractor-aware protocol it is preserved by construction. The naive protocol is therefore not a measurement of context-window effects; it is a measurement of how often middle-removal happens to spare the answer. We conclude that future studies of context-length effects must specify how they distinguish signal from distractor, or they are at best ambiguous between two opposite hypotheses.

</details>


### [264] [SeaSlides: Semantic Abstraction Layer for Agentic Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03298)
*Shengjun Fang,Chenyang Wu,Zongzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeaSlides是一个基于语义抽象层的智能体幻灯片生成框架，通过可复用组件和模板分离内容与渲染，在HTML和Typst两个后端中实现，在保持高可读性和内容保真度的同时提升复杂技术幻灯片（含公式、代码、图表）的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演示文稿生成系统存在根本性矛盾：模板方法保持规范性但限制适应性，自由形式的HTML/SVG生成虽灵活但需要模型处理底层渲染细节（坐标、内联样式、SVG几何），导致含公式、代码和数据的复杂技术幻灯片生成脆弱且不稳定。

Method: 提出SeaSlides框架：1) 语义抽象层，模型编写结构化幻灯片内容而非底层渲染指令；2) 可复用组件和能力模块（公式、代码、图表专用渲染器）；3) 模板负责布局、样式和渲染；4) 三阶段反馈机制（构建错误、项目约束违规、视觉缺陷）本地化错误定位；5) 分别在HTML（DOM组件）和Typst（模板函数+包模块）两个后端独立实现，共享相同的创作边界。

Result: 构建SeaSlidesBench-Rich基准（32个任务，涵盖数学、代码、伪代码、表格、图表和示意图），结合UltraPresent 128任务验证。在四种生成模型下，两个SeaSlides后端均产生比SVG重型生成更可读、更内容导向的源码；在四个模型中的三个上，SeaSlides后端在丰富内容宏平均指标上达到最优，同时保持整体定性性能竞争力。

Conclusion: 语义抽象是一种跨演示文稿后端的实用创作原则，通过分离内容创作与渲染细节，可以同时兼顾内容保真度、代码可读性和渲染质量，为智能体演示生成提供了一种可推广的架构范式。

Abstract: Agentic presentation generation must preserve source content, maintain coherent visual design, render specialized objects, and produce usable artifacts. Existing systems meet only part of this requirement: templates preserve regularity but restrict adaptation, whereas free-form HTML or SVG gives models flexibility at the cost of low-level rendering decisions. This mismatch makes long technical decks brittle, especially when slides contain formulas, code, or data graphics. We present SeaSlides, an agentic slide-generation framework built around a semantic abstraction layer. Rather than authoring coordinates, inline styles, or raw SVG geometry, the model writes structured slide content through reusable components and capability modules, while templates own layout, style, and rendering. We instantiate this principle separately in HTML and Typst: SeaSlides-HTML uses template-defined DOM components, whereas SeaSlides-Typst uses template functions and package-backed modules. Capability modules route equations, code, and charts to dedicated renderers, and three feedback stages localize build errors, project-constraint violations, and visual defects before export. The two systems retain backend-specific syntax and contracts while sharing the same authoring boundary. For evaluation, we combine the 128-task UltraPresent validation setting with SeaSlidesBench-Rich, a new 32-task benchmark stressing mathematics, code, pseudocode, tables, charts, and diagrams. Across four generation models, both SeaSlides backends produce more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation. A SeaSlides backend attains the highest rich-content macro-average under three of the four models while maintaining competitive overall qualitative performance. These results support semantic abstraction as a practical authoring principle across presentation backends.

</details>


### [265] [Screenshots or Tools? Eliciting Tool Use and Managing Multimodal Context in Hybrid GUI-MCP Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03327)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Wenhui Tan,Xiusheng Huang,Juntong Wu,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究混合型计算机使用智能体（同时使用截图和文本工具）中工具调用的效果。发现同一套MCP工具能提升推理模型4.0pp但使非推理模型下降5.9pp，根源在于工具决策行为差异。非推理策略会忽略、错误命名或错误终止工具调用，而推理模型虽避免这些错误但仅在55/309任务中调用工具（仅占可调用任务的23.9%），存在"采纳差距"。多轮RL实验表明行为可被引导但能力无法通过奖励直接提升，瓶颈在于工具调用语义。压缩观测（丢弃冗余截图、减半图像历史）可将输入token减少约三分之一，重训练后精度反而从33.0%提升到37.8%，且输入成本仅为原来的53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合型计算机使用智能体可以同时通过截图和文本工具操作，但工具是否可用并不决定效果方向。研究旨在理解为何相同工具下不同模型的性能表现截然不同，并探究工具采纳率低（仅23.9%）背后的原因。

Method: 在OSWorld-MCP基准（309任务）上使用相同的GUI-MCP框架对比推理模型与非推理模型；通过多轮RL探针研究行动层面（密集工具奖励）和上下文层面（截图冗余压缩）两个层面的问题；采用预注册的退化子集评估丰富-贫瘠差距。

Result: 密集工具奖励将电子表格采纳率从0.03提升至0.33并保持到贪心解码，但留出精度未随之提升；成功工具调用后截图常冗余，丢弃并减半图像历史使输入token减少约三分之一；重训练后压缩智能体达到37.8%（基线33.0%），输入成本仅为53%，并将退化子集上的丰富-贫瘠差距缩小至零。

Conclusion: 工具只有在模型正确选择并整合时才有帮助，当前混合智能体在工具决策上存在大量未利用的选择空间。行为可通过训练引导（可操控），但实际能力（竞争力）无法直接通过奖励塑造，核心瓶颈在于工具调用语义。

Abstract: Hybrid computer-use agents can act through screenshots or call text tools. We find that having a tool available does not settle which way the effect goes. Under one identical GUI-MCP harness on the OSWorld-MCP benchmark (309 tasks), the same MCP tools improve a reasoning model by +4.0pp and degrade a non-reasoning model by -5.9pp (5 runs each, both beyond 2 SE). What separates the two is tool-decision behavior. The non-reasoning policy ignores, misnames, or falsely terminates around tools. The reasoning model avoids these failures, yet still calls a tool on only 55/309 tasks, 23.9% of the tool-reachable ones. We call this shortfall the adoption gap. Both levels of the problem share one cause: the model already has a cheaper route and is never trained to take it. Multi-turn RL probes that cause. At the action level, a dense tool bonus raises spreadsheet adoption 0.03 -> 0.33 and carries into greedy decoding, but held-out accuracy does not follow. Behavior is steerable; competence is not. The bottleneck lies in tool-call semantics. At the context level, a successful tool call often makes the next screenshot redundant. Dropping it and halving image history cuts input tokens by about a third, at a small accuracy cost. Retraining under the same observation rule removes that cost. The compressed agent then reaches 37.8% against 33.0% for the uncompressed operating point, at 53% of the input cost, and closes the rich-lean gap on a pre-registered degraded subset to zero. Tools help when the model chooses and integrates them, and current hybrid agents leave many such choices unused.

</details>


### [266] [Long-term Traffic Scene Prediction via Polynomial Representations in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03330)
*Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文针对自动驾驶中的交通场景预测问题，提出基于多项式表示的鲁棒且计算高效的预测模型。通过理论分析和实验验证，证明中等阶次多项式能高保真捕捉真实运动动态，在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open数据集上实现接近最先进水平的精度，同时显著提升跨数据集泛化能力，并基于扩散生成框架实现更合理一致的多智能体场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于序列的表示在交通场景预测中面临噪声鲁棒性差和泛化能力不足的挑战，需要更高效、更具泛化能力的轨迹表示方法来支持安全关键的自动驾驶决策。

Method: 采用多项式表示轨迹和地图几何，构建预测模型；在此基础上扩展基于扩散的生成框架，实现多智能体交通场景生成。通过理论分析证明多项式表示的有效性，并在标准基准上进行了大量实验验证。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open数据集上，多项式表示方法达到接近最先进的精度，显著改善跨数据集泛化；生成轨迹更平滑、行为更合理，且计算成本更低。同时揭示了标准分布内评估和基于回归的指标可能无法真实反映模型泛化能力和预测合理性。

Conclusion: 多项式轨迹表示是交通场景预测中高效、表达力强且具有良好泛化能力的基础表示方法，为安全关键的自动驾驶场景预测提供了坚实的理论和实证支撑。

Abstract: This thesis addresses fundamental challenges in traffic scene prediction for autonomous driving by introducing robust and computationally efficient models based on polynomial representations. While conventional sequence-based representations often struggle with noise and generalization, this work demonstrates that polynomial representations offer significant advantages in computational efficiency, generalization, and prediction plausibility. Through theoretical analysis and empirical validation, this thesis demonstrates that moderate-degree polynomials capture real-world motion dynamics with high fidelity without constraining predictive performance. Building on this foundation, a prediction model representing both trajectories and map geometry with polynomial representations achieves near state-of-the-art accuracy on standard benchmarks while substantially improving generalization under distribution shift. Extending this concept, a diffusion- based generative framework enables multi-agent scene generation, producing traffic continuations that are more plausible and kinematically consistent than those generated by conventional baselines. Evaluations on the Argoverse 2 and Waymo Open datasets confirm that polynomial representations reduce computational cost, enhance cross-dataset generalization, and yield smoother trajectories and higher behavioral plausibility. The findings reveal that standard in-distribution evaluation and regression-based metrics may fail to reflect true model generalization and prediction plausibility. By providing theoretical justification and empirical validation, this dissertation estab- lishes polynomial trajectory representations as an efficient, expressive, and generalizable foundation for traffic scene prediction in safety critical autonomous driving.

</details>


### [267] [Traceable Multi-Agent System for Knowledge-Based Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03339)
*Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Hyeokjun Choe,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceMAS是一个面向多智能体预测的可交互演示系统，通过两种因果环路图（理想CLD和数据接地CLD）将文本证据、数据选择与模型修订之间的关联可视化，使自主预测代理的过程变得可检查，并已在原油价格预测场景中展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业预测越来越依赖自主代理进行文档解读、数据搜索、代码生成和模型修订，但这种自主性导致从业者难以检查预测变化的原因、支撑证据以及数据和建模选择的修订过程，缺乏可追溯性。

Method: TraceMAS围绕两种因果环路表示组织代理输出：理想CLD（从领域文档提取关键因素及其因果关系的因果环路图）和数据接地CLD（将因素与内部变量、外部数据或文档化的代理指标进行关联）。数据接地CLD指导特征构建和模型设计，同时保留文本证据、数据选择与模型修订之间的联系。

Result: 在原油价格预测场景中完成演示，界面支持用户比较预测迭代、检查代理级别的修订、探索因果图、审查特征-数据映射及模型架构，并将情景预测与市场叙事相联系。

Conclusion: 该演示表明自主预测代理可以在保持灵活性的同时，使证据到预测的整个过程具备可检查性。

Abstract: Enterprise forecasting increasingly relies on autonomous agents that interpret documents, search for data, generate code, and revise models. While this autonomy helps build adaptive forecasting pipelines, it also makes it difficult for practitioners to inspect why a forecast changed, which evidence supported the change, and how data and modeling choices were revised. We present TraceMAS, an interactive demo system for traceable multi-agent forecasting. TraceMAS organizes agent outputs around two causal-loop representations: an Ideal Causal Loop Diagram (Ideal CLD), which captures key factors and their causal relations extracted from domain documents, and a Data-Grounded Causal Loop Diagram (Data-Grounded CLD), which links those factors to internal variables, external data, or documented proxies. The Data-Grounded CLD guides feature construction and model design while preserving the connection between textual evidence, data choices, and model revisions. We demonstrate TraceMAS on crude oil price forecasting. The demo interface allows users to compare forecasting iterations, inspect agent-level revisions, explore causal maps, review feature-data mappings and model architecture, and connect scenario forecasts to market narratives. This demonstration shows how autonomous forecasting agents can retain flexibility while making the evidence-to-forecast process inspectable.

</details>


### [268] [MMLongBench-Doc-V2: A Corrected-Annotation, Semantics-Aware Revision of MMLongBench-Doc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03397)
*Mingtian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMLongBench-Doc-V2，修正了MMLongBench-Doc长文档问答基准中的标注错误（纠正106条标注）、评测指标缺陷（用LLM裁判替代字符串匹配）以及文档文件名错误等问题，最终形成1,071个问题、134个文档的修正版基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMLongBench-Doc基准存在两个关键缺陷：一是参考指标直接比较提取的答案，导致数值格式不同的相同答案（如1,358,000与1358000）会被判为错误；二是相当比例的ground-truth标注存在错误、歧义或不完整，且这些问题集中在能力较强的系统能正确回答的问题上，导致评测分数偏离其本应测量的真实能力。

Method: 作者提出MMLongBench-Doc-V2修正基准：1）纠正106条标注，每条公布页码和算术依据；2）用固定LLM裁判替代字符串匹配指标，判断回答是否语义等价于参考答案；3）移除10个文件名错误的问答及1个重复问题；4）提出一个决策流程，判断空集键何时可以放宽、何时放宽会破坏刻意构造的负样本，应用于全部208行后放宽了14个。

Result: 修正后基准包含1,071个问题、134个文档；106条标注被纠正，14个样本被放宽，11个问题被移除；V2分数与已发布的V1分数不可直接比较。

Conclusion: MMLongBench-Doc-V2提供了更可靠的长文档QA评测基准，其修正记录、语料和评测框架开源可复现，最可复用的贡献是一套空集键放宽的决策程序。

Abstract: MMLongBench-Doc is a long-document QA benchmark of 1,082 questions over 135 PDFs. Two properties of it push measured scores away from the quantity they are meant to capture: the reference metric compares extracted answers, so 1,358,000 loses to 1358000; and a non-trivial share of ground-truth annotations are wrong, ambiguous, or incomplete --- concentrated, because of how they were found, in exactly the questions capable systems answer correctly. MMLongBench-Doc-V2 corrects 106 annotations, each published with the page and arithmetic that settle it, and replaces the string metric with a pinned LLM judge asked whether a response means the reference. Ten questions whose document ships under the wrong filename are removed rather than counted wrong, along with one duplicated question, leaving 1,071 questions over 134 documents. The most reusable contribution is a decision procedure for when an empty set key may be widened and when widening would destroy a deliberate negative sample; applied to all 208 rows, it widened 14. V2 scores are not comparable with published V1 numbers. The corrected corpus, the per-entry correction record and the evaluation harness are available at https://github.com/VectifyAI/MMLongBench-Doc-V2.

</details>


### [269] [Towards Robust Tool Use in Agents via Experience-Driven Adaptive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03403)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Li Yu,Ding Hao,Bohai Zhao,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpG机制，通过构建和精炼自适应指导来捕捉每个工具的能力边界和最佳实践，从而提升智能体在不同运行时条件下使用工具的鲁棒性和有效性。ExpG包含经验获取、经验蒸馏和经验复用三个阶段，实验表明其在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成任务上均带来一致改进，使较小的智能体能超越不使用ExpG的更大智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能瓶颈正从模型能力转向执行过程的鲁棒性。工具作为智能体与外部环境交互的主要接口，现有方法很少关注如何确保在多样运行时条件下实现鲁棒的工具使用。为了解决这一问题，作者提出了ExpG机制。

Method: ExpG包含三个阶段：（1）经验获取——从历史执行轨迹中分析工具调用质量，通过多方面的归因产生结构化的可学习经验；（2）经验蒸馏——通过过滤无帮助经验来保持经验池有效，使用基于等价类的方法选择代表性经验，并将其总结为可泛化的指导；（3）经验复用——在未来的任务求解过程中自适应地应用这些指导。

Result: 大量实验表明，ExpG在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成任务上带来一致的改进，使较小的智能体能够超越未使用ExpG的较大智能体。此外，ExpG在具有挑战性的场景中取得了特别显著的收益。

Conclusion: ExpG为实现更鲁棒的工具使用提供了一条有前景的路径，尤其在不熟悉或困难的运行时条件下表现突出。代码、实验和结果已公开。

Abstract: The performance bottleneck of agents is increasingly shifting from model capability to the robustness of their execution processes. Tools play a central role as the primary interface through which agents interact with external environments, yet existing methods rarely focus on ensuring robust tool use across diverse runtime conditions. To address this problem, we propose ExpG, a mechanism that builds and refines adaptive guidance capturing each tool's capability boundaries and best practices, thereby enabling agents to use tools more robustly and effectively. ExpG consists of three phases: (1) experience acquisition, which analyzes tool invocation quality from historical execution trajectories, producing structured learnable experiences through multi-aspect attribution; (2) experience distillation, which keeps the experience pool effective by filtering unhelpful experiences, selecting representative ones with an equivalence-class-based method, and summarizing them into generalizable guidance; and (3) experience reuse, which applies the guidance adaptively during future task solving. Extensive experiments show that ExpG brings consistent improvements across the tool selection, tool calling, and response generation tasks, enabling smaller agents to outperform larger ones that do not use ExpG. Moreover, ExpG achieves particularly strong gains in challenging settings, suggesting a promising path toward more robust tool use. Our code, experiments, and results are available.

</details>


### [270] [Enactive Artificial Intelligence: A Decision-Centric Architecture for Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03413)
*Zuojun Max Shen,Yuan Qu,Pujun Zhang,Anbang Liu,Yunhao Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成式行动AI"（Enactive AI）概念框架，用于企业级和工业复杂系统中的推理、站点级决策支持与执行反馈，通过四个互补角色（组织世界、站点世界、模式智能、生成式决策循环）将AI从模型能力扩展到系统感知行动，推动可扩展、可治理且具有社会价值的AI部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI实践已超越大语言模型和文本/图像生成，日益整合工具、智能体和执行框架来解决真实商业与工业问题。然而，由于可靠性、可行性、韧性及责任性等要求，AI在真实复杂系统中的应用尚未得到充分验证，亟需一个面向企业级和工业复杂系统的概念框架。

Method: 综合邻近领域研究，提出Enactive AI概念框架，由四个互补角色组成：1）组织世界定义战略-制度视野下的运营管理逻辑与组织行为世界模型；2）站点世界定义物理有界的工业优化与执行世界模型（运营-实现视野）；3）模式智能作为两个世界模型之间的耦合机制，编织各类AI应用；4）生成式决策循环触发自演进动态过程，更新和审计整个框架。

Result: Enactive AI将AI边界从模型能力扩展到系统感知行动，强调复杂系统中的决策智能，为可扩展、可治理且具有社会价值的AI部署开辟了新的可能性。该框架指向AI进步应以智能系统在真实复杂系统中支持重大行动、负责任治理和持久社会价值的可靠性来衡量。

Conclusion: Enactive AI预示着一个新方向：AI进步不应仅以模型生成或自动化能力衡量，而应以智能系统在塑造现代生活的复杂系统中可靠地支持重大行动、负责任的治理和持久社会价值的能力来衡量，这代表了企业级和工业复杂系统AI研究的下一前沿。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) continues to evolve and mature, recent AI practices have moved beyond large language models (LLMs) and text or image generation tasks, increasingly integrating tools, agents, and harnesses to solve real business and industrial problems. However, the power of AI is not verified under these real-world complex systems for various reasons, considering reliability, feasibility, resilience, and responsibility requirements in real commercial and industrial operations. This study synthesizes adjacent research and introduces Enactive AI as a conceptual framework for enterprise and industry reasoning, site-level decision support, and execution feedback. Four complementary roles organize the framework: an Organizational World defines operations management logic and an organizational behavior world model behind an enterprise from a strategic-institutional horizon; a Site World defines a physically bounded industrial optimization and execution world model from an operational-realization horizon; Schema Intelligence provides the coupling mechanism between two world models to weave various AI applications via two models; and Enactive Decision Cycle triggers the self-evolving dynamic process to update and audit the entire framework. By foregrounding decision intelligence in complex systems, Enactive AI expands the frontier of AI from model capability to system-aware action, opening new possibilities for scalable, governable, and socially valuable AI deployment. Enactive AI points toward a future in which AI progress is measured not only by what models can generate or automate, but by how reliably intelligent systems can support consequential action, responsible governance, and durable social value in the complex systems that shape modern life, which we believe will define the next frontier of AI research for enterprise-level and industrial complex systems.

</details>


### [271] [AI World Cup 2026: Benchmarking Large Language Models for End-to-End Football Tournament Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03416)
*Jonaid Shianifar,Iias Faiud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了AI世界杯基准测试的完整结果：十个基于LLM的助手对2026年FIFA世界杯做出赛前单一预测。GPT-5.5 Thinking以744分夺冠，并是唯一正确预测西班牙夺冠的模型。最终排名主要由淘汰赛表现驱动（r=0.986），而小组赛表现与总分几乎无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测现实事件的比较缺乏公平性，因为不同模型获得的信息、工具和评估规则各不相同。作者希望建立一个统一、可复现的基准来公平比较不同LLM在完整赛事预测任务上的表现。

Method: 设计并执行AI World Cup基准测试：十个LLM助手使用相同的比赛快照、提示词、JSON格式和评分程序，对全部104场比赛进行单一赛前预测，覆盖小组赛比分、排名、淘汰赛对阵、最终名次、置信度和简短解释。

Result: GPT-5.5 Thinking以744分夺冠（其次为GPT-5.5 717分、Gemini 699分、Qwen 3.7 687分）。总分与淘汰赛得分高度相关（r=0.986），但与小组赛比分（r=0.055）、小组排名（r=-0.103）和赛前总得分（r=-0.054）几乎无关。Claude Sonnet 4.6在小组赛正确率最高（63.89%）但总排名第六。自报置信度与准确率（r=-0.060）和总分（r=-0.067）均无显著相关。

Conclusion: 完整赛事预测与逐场比赛预测测试的是不同能力；基于淘汰赛积分榜的排名高度依赖于评分设计。作者公开了所有基准材料、原始响应和评分代码以支持复现和扩展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now regularly asked to forecast real-world events, but comparisons are often difficult because models receive different information, use different tools, and are evaluated under different rules. This paper reports the completed \emph{AI World Cup} benchmark, in which ten LLM-based assistants made a single pre-tournament forecast of the entire 2026 FIFA World Cup. Every submission used the same tournament snapshot, prompt, JSON schema, and scoring procedure. The forecasts covered group-stage scores, group rankings, the knockout bracket, final placings, confidence values, and short explanations. After all 104 matches had been played, GPT-5.5 Thinking finished first with 744 points, followed by GPT-5.5 with 717, Gemini with 699, and Qwen 3.7 with 687. GPT-5.5 Thinking was also the only model to select Spain, which defeated Argentina 1--0 in the final, as champion. The final ranking was driven mainly by knockout performance: total score was strongly correlated with knockout points ($r=0.986$), but showed little relationship with group-stage match points ($r=0.055$), group-standing points ($r=-0.103$), or their combined pre-knockout score ($r=-0.054$). Match-level accuracy produced a different ordering. Claude Sonnet 4.6 correctly predicted the largest number of group-stage outcomes (63.89\%) but placed sixth overall. Average self-reported confidence was also unrelated to either outcome accuracy ($r=-0.060$) or total score ($r=-0.067$). The results suggest that forecasting a complete tournament tests something different from predicting matches one at a time, while also showing how strongly a bracket-based leaderboard can depend on scoring design. The benchmark materials, raw responses, and scoring code are released to support replication and future extensions.

</details>


### [272] [Towards Improving Sequential Decision-Making in LLM Agents via Experience Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03420)
*Jakub Rada,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在完全可观测的双人零和游戏中的序贯决策能力，发现LLM在井字棋、四子棋等简单游戏中表现次优且输给MCTS对手，并提出了一个带经验记忆的智能体框架来改进其序贯决策表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在单步推理任务上已有显著进步，但其在序贯决策中的表现尚未被充分理解。作者选择完全可观测的双人零和游戏作为研究载体，因为这类游戏提供基于规则的确定性评估，无需依赖评判模型，能够客观衡量LLM的决策质量。

Method: 首先在多种模型层级上评估LLM在井字棋和四子棋中的表现，并进行混淆实验（保持游戏树不变但改写表面形式）以排除记忆策略的影响。基于发现的性能差距，提出了一个带经验记忆的智能体框架，通过赛后反思（post-game reflection）和规则提取（rule extraction）来解决序贯决策中的信用分配等常见挑战，且不修改模型权重。

Result: LLM在简单游戏中（如井字棋、四子棋）表现次优，输给MCTS对手；混淆游戏表面形式后性能基本不变，表明差距并非完全由记忆策略解释。提出的智能体框架在井字棋上取得了可衡量的性能提升。

Conclusion: LLM在序贯决策任务中仍存在显著性能差距，但通过智能体框架结合经验记忆、赛后反思和规则提取可以有效改进，且无需修改模型权重。

Abstract: Large language models have improved substantially on single-shot reasoning tasks, but their performance in sequential decision-making is less well understood. We study this on fully-observable two-player zero-sum games, which provide ground-truth evaluation: outcomes are determined by the rules, and optimality of individual moves can be computed or approximated, without relying on a judge model. Across model tiers, LLMs play suboptimally in simple games such as tic-tac-toe or Connect Four, and lose to MCTS opponents. Obfuscations that preserve the game tree but rewrite its surface form leave performance largely unchanged, indicating the gap is not fully explained by recall of memorized strategies. Motivated by this performance gap, we introduce an agentic framework enhanced with an experience memory designed for the sequential setting and addressing common challenges of sequential decision-making such as credit assignment. We show that post-game reflection and rule extraction yield measurable improvements on tic-tac-toe without modifying the model weights.

</details>


### [273] [State Propagation Also Satisfies: A Complex-Valued State-Space Model for Deterministic State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03425)
*Xiaohe Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Complex State Propagator (CSP)，一种极简循环架构，仅通过复数域中的状态传播即可在确定性状态追踪任务（如奇偶校验、模数计数、括号匹配）上达到100%的准确率和完美的F1分数，证明状态传播本身足以应对此类任务，无需注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构虽然凭借注意力机制在序列建模中占据主导地位，但对于一类确定性的状态追踪任务（如奇偶校验、模数计数、括号匹配），注意力机制可能过于复杂冗余。作者希望证明仅靠状态传播就能解决这类任务，从而挑战"注意力是必需"的假设。

Method: CSP是一种极简循环架构，仅在层间传播隐藏状态，中间步骤不做输出投影。状态用复数向量表示，通过输入相关的复数域旋转进行更新。为支持深层传播且避免梯度消失或退化，引入了块级跳跃连接、逐元素复数归一化以及序列边界处的SiLU激活函数，并配合Focal Loss进行训练。

Result: 在经典的确定性状态追踪任务（奇偶校验、模数计数、括号匹配）上，CSP配合Focal Loss实现了100%的准确率和完美的F1分数。

Conclusion: 对于确定性状态追踪任务，状态传播本身已足够强大，无需依赖注意力机制。CSP以极简架构证明了这一点，为处理此类任务提供了更高效、更简洁的替代方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have dominated sequence modeling, largely due to the expressive power of attention mechanisms. However, for a class of deterministic state tracking tasks---such as parity checking, modular counting, and parenthesis matching---attention may be overkill.
