In this paper, we show that \textbf{state propagation alone is sufficient}.
  We propose the \textbf{Complex State Propagator (CSP)}, a minimalistic recurrent architecture that \textbf{only propagates hidden states} across layers without output projections at intermediate steps. The state is represented as a complex-valued vector, updated via input-dependent rotations in the complex domain. To enable deep propagation without gradient vanishing or degradation, we introduce a \textbf{block-level skip connection} alongside element-wise complex normalization and SiLU activation at sequence boundaries. Applied with Focal Loss, CSP achieves \textbf{100\% accuracy} with perfect F1 scores across canonical tasks.

</details>


### [274] [DataSpace: Benchmarking Data Agents for Verifiable Analytics over Heterogeneous Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03451)
*Boyan Li,Zhuowen Liang,Yupeng Xie,Xiaotian Lin,Tianqi Luo,Xinyu Liu,Yizhang Zhu,Zhangyang Peng,Yuan Li,Zhengxuan Zhang,Jiayi Zhang,Nan Tang,Guoliang Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataSpace是一个数据智能体基准测试，包含410个跨语言任务和7,439个工件（总计15.01 GB），覆盖CSV、JSON、SQLite、Markdown、PDF和视频等多种格式，用于评估数据智能体在异构工作空间中生成可验证表格结果的能力。最佳准确率仅达66.34%，说明该基准尚未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多将结构化查询、检索和开放性分析分开评测，缺乏对异构证据发现、完整表格输出和确定性评估的统一。数据智能体在实际组织中需要处理分散在多种格式中的证据，但缺乏相应的综合评测基准。

Method: 构建DataSpace-Builder框架，包括跨语言转换、约束感知的关系抽样、模态路由和工件渲染、领域专家人工审查与任务修复。采用确定性评估器，包含header不变列对齐、类型和精度感知归一化、顺序感知行比较。该基准被用作KDD Cup 2026官方评测基准。

Result: 在六个前沿多模态模型和五个智能体框架上评测，最佳准确率为66.34%；固定骨干模型时，框架选择造成15.36个百分点的差异。多模态证据整合和连接操作在所有六个骨干模型上均一致降低准确率。

Conclusion: DataSpace仍有较大的性能提升空间，当前模型在异构证据整合和多模态数据连接方面存在显著挑战，为提升数据智能体的可靠性指明方向。

Abstract: Data agents enable natural-language analytics over organizational workspaces, where relevant evidence may be scattered across databases, structured files, long documents, and multimedia. Existing benchmarks largely isolate structured querying, retrieval, or open-ended analysis, leaving heterogeneous evidence discovery, complete tabular outputs, and deterministic evaluation insufficiently unified. We introduce DataSpace, a benchmark in which data agents produce verifiable tabular results from task-local heterogeneous workspaces. It contains 410 cross-language tasks and 7,439 artifacts totaling 15.01 GB across CSV, JSON, SQLite, Markdown, PDF, and video. DataSpace also served as the official evaluation benchmark for the KDD Cup 2026 Data Agents for Complex Data Analysis competition. Each agent receives only a question and workspace and returns the complete requested tabular result. We construct DataSpace with DataSpace-Builder, an execution-grounded framework comprising cross-language transformation, constraint-aware relational sampling, modality routing and artifact rendering, and human review and task repair by 11 domain experts. A deterministic evaluator performs header-invariant column alignment, type- and precision-aware normalization, and order-aware row comparison. Across six recently released frontier multimodal models and five widely used agent harnesses, the best accuracy reaches 66.34%, while harness choice creates a 15.36-point spread with the backbone fixed. Multimodal evidence integration and joins consistently reduce accuracy across all six backbones. These results show that DataSpace remains unsaturated and identify key challenges for improving data-agent reliability.

</details>


### [275] [LLaDA MoE v2: Scaling Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03457)
*Fengqi Zhu,Shaoxuan Xu,Jingyang Ou,Zebin You,Yipeng Xing,Huabin Liu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Zhenzhong Lan,Yankai Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Jianguo Li,Chongxuan Li,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了扩散语言模型（dLLM）中MoE架构的缩放规律，发现其与自回归模型的缩放趋势存在定量差异，并据此训练了LLaDA MoE v2（30B-A3B）模型，在少量训练数据下接近或超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型是自回归模型的有力替代方案，但MoE架构下dLLM的缩放行为尚不明确，缺乏系统的缩放规律和设计原则。

Method: 系统表征优化超参数、计算分配和架构规模三方面的缩放行为：通过IsoFLOP分析确定最优token预算与模型计算量的分配比例，并探究专家池大小、专家粒度和共享专家比例等架构选择在不同规模下的最优配置。

Result: 发现最优名义批次大小增长更快、最优学习率衰减更快；IsoFLOP分析显示最优token预算增长快于模型侧计算量；更大规模更偏好更大的专家池；训练LLaDA MoE v2（30B-A3B，23.5T tokens），仅用Qwen3约65%的预训练数据即在多个基准上接近Qwen3，在SFT后于八项推理与编码基准中七项超越SDAR Chat。

Conclusion: 该研究为MoE dLLM建立了实用的缩放定律和设计原则，证明MoE dLLM在计算效率上具有竞争力，为扩散语言模型的进一步扩展提供了指导。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer an alternative to autoregressive (AR) language modeling, yet the scaling behavior of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) dLLMs remains poorly understood. We systematically characterize how optimization hyperparameters, compute allocation, and architecture scale for MoE dLLMs, identifying quantitative differences from scaling trends previously reported for AR models. Specifically, for optimization, the optimal nominal batch size grows faster, while the optimal learning rate decays more rapidly with compute. For model--data allocation, IsoFLOP analysis reveals a slight data-side tilt: the optimal token budget grows faster than activated model-side computation. For MoE architecture, larger scales increasingly favor larger expert pools at fixed activated capacity, while moderate expert granularity remains consistently effective and the preferred fraction of activated capacity assigned to shared experts remains stable across scales. Guided by these findings, we train LLaDA MoE v2, a 30B-A3B dLLM, from scratch on 23.5T tokens. With approximately 65\% as many pretraining tokens as Qwen3, LLaDA MoE v2 approaches Qwen3 on several knowledge, reasoning, and coding benchmarks. After supervised fine-tuning alone, it outperforms SDAR Chat on seven of eight reasoning and coding benchmarks and remains close to Qwen3 on several tasks. These results establish practical scaling laws and design principles for MoE dLLMs.

</details>


### [276] [Solver-Aware Decompositions for Programming-by-Example: When Dividing Requires Knowing how to Conquer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03461)
*Janis Zenkner,Tobias Sesterhenn,Tim Grams,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Solver-Aware Decomposition (SAD)框架，挑战编程示例（PBE）中分解器必须模仿真实标注（GT）子目标的传统假设。SAD在保留GT监督训练作为结构脚手架的同时，利用冻结的合成器直接反馈来优化分解器，奖励那些合成器能够实际解决的子目标。实验发现一个"准确性悖论"：分解器与GT分解的一致性越高，合成成功率反而越低。SAD通过学习牺牲GT对齐来换取求解器可处理性，在两个PBE领域取得一致改进，甚至能解决GT分解预言机都无法解决的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE方法训练分解器去模仿GT子目标，隐含假设分解质量是任务内在属性。但作者认为对于有界求解器，GT分解反映的是标注者的因式分解选择，而非求解器的搜索动态。GT推导的分解可能逻辑上有效但求解器难以处理，导致分解器提出对求解器不可行的子目标。

Method: SAD框架包含两个部分：1）保留GT子目标的监督训练作为结构脚手架，为分解器提供基础学习信号；2）额外通过冻结合成器的直接反馈优化分解器，根据求解器在目标程序上的损失来奖励子目标——该损失信号反映了子任务难度，鼓励分解器生成求解器能实际处理的分解方案。

Result: 实验揭示了准确性悖论：与GT分解的一致性更高并不能提高合成成功率，尽管合成器本身就是在相同GT数据上训练的。SAD学习的分解方案以牺牲GT对齐换取求解器可处理性，在两个PBE领域均获得合成和端到端任务准确率的一致提升。此外，SAD能解决GT分解预言机失败的任务，证明GT分解对求解器并非普遍最优。

Conclusion: 分解质量是相对于求解器而言的，而非任务的内在属性。GT分解不一定是具有固定归纳偏置的有界求解器的最优选择，SAD通过求解器感知的训练方式能够学习更有效的分解策略。

Abstract: Decomposition-based Programming-by-example (PBE) scales performance by splitting tasks into subtasks that a learned synthesizer solves: a decomposer predicts intermediate subgoals, and a synthesizer generates programs conditioned on them. Current approaches train the decomposer to imitate ground-truth ( GT) subgoals, implicitly treating decomposition quality as intrinsic to the task. We challenge this assumption: for bounded solvers with fixed inductive biases, GT decompositions reflect the annotator's factorization choices - not the solver's search dynamics. A decomposer trained to match GT decompositions may therefore propose subgoals that are logically valid yet intractable for the solver. We propose Solver-Aware Decomposition (SAD), a training framework that retains supervised training on GT subgoals as a structural scaffold, while additionally optimizing the decomposer via direct feedback from a frozen synthesizer. Subgoals are rewarded based on the synthesizer's loss on the target program - a signal of subtask difficulty that encourages decompositions the solver can act on. Our experiments reveal an accuracy paradox: higher agreement with GT decompositions does not improve synthesis success - even though the synthesizer was trained on the very same GT data the decomposer is optimized to mimic. SAD instead learns decompositions that trade GT alignment for solver tractability, yielding consistent gains in synthesis and end-to-end task accuracy across two PBE domains. Moreover, SAD solves tasks that a GT decomposition oracle fails - empirical evidence that GT decompositions are not universally optimal for bounded solvers, and that decomposition quality is solver-relative, not intrinsic.

</details>


### [277] [LeanMem: Simple and Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03463)
*Yuxin Liao,Le Wu,Min Hou,Hao Liu,Han Wu,Zishu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMem 是一个轻量级长时记忆框架，根据对话内容的可压缩性、时间动态性和保真需求进行差异化处理，将信息分别存储为紧凑的配置文件记忆、时间结构化事件记忆或源可追溯记录记忆，并按需动态分配检索预算，在多个基准上以最低或接近最低的成本显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的长期记忆系统通常使用统一的摘要与检索流程处理异构对话内容，导致要么消耗过多token、要么丢失细粒度证据。作者认为历史对话内容应根据其可压缩性、时间动态性和保真度要求进行差异化处理。

Method: LeanMem首先过滤低价值内容，然后根据信息性质将有效片段存储为三类记忆：紧凑的配置文件记忆、时间结构化的事件记忆、源可追溯的记录记忆。维护时仅选择性更新动态演化的事件记忆。推理时根据查询的证据需求动态选择记忆类型并分配检索预算，按需组装相关证据。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上，使用GPT-4.1-mini和Qwen3-8B作为基础模型，LeanMem在所有设置下相比最强的基于记忆的基线最多提升15.1个准确率点，同时保持最低或接近最低的构建成本、推理token和延迟。

Conclusion: 历史对话内容不应被统一处理，差异化的记忆存储与按需检索策略能够同时兼顾高准确率和低资源消耗，为长时记忆系统提供了一种更高效的设计范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM-based agents to sustain interactions and reliably leverage distant history. However, existing memory systems typically process heterogeneous dialogue content through a uniform summarization and retrieval pipeline, leading to either excessive token consumption or irreversible loss of fine-grained evidence. We argue that historical dialogue content should be handled differently according to its compressibility, temporal dynamics, and fidelity requirements. Based on this insight, we propose LeanMem, a lightweight long-term memory framework. LeanMem first filters out low-value content, then stores informative segments as compact profile memory, temporally structured event memory, or source-grounded record memory, depending on the nature of the information. During maintenance, only dynamically evolving event memories are selectively updated, avoiding redundant consolidation of stable profiles and immutable records. During inference, LeanMem dynamically selects memory types and allocates retrieval budgets according to query-specific evidence demands, assembling relevant evidence on demand. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S with GPT-4.1-mini and Qwen3-8B, LeanMem improves accuracy over the strongest memory-based baseline in every setting, by up to 15.1 points, at the lowest or near-lowest construction cost, inference tokens, and latency. The code and datasets are included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [278] [ChartAnno: Evaluating MLLMs for Chart Annotation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03464)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Lidan Tan,Xin Lin,Xingchen Zeng,Yi Shan,Ziyue Lin,Xiaoliang Fu,Xinyuan Liu,Yuetong Guo,Fen Wang,Bongshin Lee,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChartAnno基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图表注释生成任务上的能力。该基准包含1,200张真实世界图表及对应的代码和注释指令，并在三种指令具体程度和多种输入设置下评估了10个代表性MLLM，发现专有模型整体更强，但大规模开源模型正在缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图表理解、生成和编辑方面取得了显著进展，但其对现有图表进行注释标注的能力尚未被充分探索。图表注释是一项常见但具有挑战性的沟通任务，需要模型推断意图、理解图表语义并进行适当的文本或图形元素放置，因此有必要构建专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 构建ChartAnno基准，包含1,200张带配对代码和注释指令的真实世界图表，涵盖三种指令具体性级别。在两种主要输入设置（仅图表代码、图表代码+图表图像）下评估10个代表性MLLM，并额外进行仅图表图像的消融研究。

Result: 专有模型整体表现更强，但大规模开源模型正在缩小差距；更具体的指令能提升注释质量，而推断抽象意图对当前MLLM来说仍然最为困难；提供图表图像带来的整体增益有限，主要改进体现在设计相关指标上。

Conclusion: 图表注释生成是一项需要语义基础和有效注释设计的挑战性任务，当前模型在该任务上仍存在明显不足，特别是在抽象意图推断方面，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in chart understanding, generation, and editing, but their ability to annotate existing charts remains underexplored. Annotating charts is a common yet challenging communicative task, requiring models to infer intended messages, interpret chart semantics, and place appropriate textual or graphical elements. To address this gap, we introduce ChartAnno, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on chart annotation generation. It contains 1,200 real-world charts with paired code and annotation instructions across three levels of instruction specificity. We evaluate 10 representative MLLMs under two primary input settings: (1) chart code alone and (2) both chart code and chart image, and further include a chart image-only ablation study. Results show that proprietary models remain stronger overall, although large-scale open-source models narrow the gap. More specific instructions improve annotation quality, while inferring abstract intent remains most difficult for current MLLMs. Providing chart images brings limited overall gains, with improvements mainly appearing in design-related metrics. These findings highlight chart annotation generation as a challenging task requiring semantic grounding and effective annotation design. Code and data will be released in a future version.

</details>


### [279] [When Correct Solutions Repeat: Rarity-Aware Credit Redistribution for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03467)
*Zhe Cao,Miaowen Wen,Fangjiong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cue-GRPO方法，通过策略线索（Strategy Cues）对验证正确的轨迹进行分区，按簇稀有度重新分配正向优势，解决GRPO中重复正确解导致的结构级信用集中问题，仅增加6%训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO将每个正确完成作为独立学习信号，但完成级均匀性导致结构级偏差：重复出现的正确解法积累大量正向系数，稀有解法获得的信用有限，造成"多样性诱导的结构级信用集中"问题。

Method: 提出分区条件规则（partition-conditioned rule），根据簇稀有度重新分配正向优势。Cue-GRPO使用确定性策略线索（Strategy Cues）构建回放局部分区，无需辅助模型推理；还探索了基于裁判分区（Judge Partitions）的信用再分配（CR）方案。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，Cue-GRPO提升了AIME重复采样性能，在高采样预算下收益最大；裁判分区方案同样有效；训练开销仅比GRPO多6%。

Conclusion: 结构级信用再分配是RLVR中一个实用的设计维度，策略线索为竞赛数学提供了低开销的实现方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) com- monly optimizes each correct completion as an independent learning signal. In GRPO, this completion-level uniformity creates structure-level skew: recurring correct solution forms accumulate positive coefficient mass in proportion to how often they are sampled, while rare forms receive limited credit. We formalize this behavior as multiplicity-induced structure-level credit concentration and introduce a partition- conditioned rule that redistributes positive advantages accord- ing to cluster rarity. Cue-GRPO instantiates this rule with- out auxiliary-model inference by using deterministic Strategy Cues to construct rollout-local partitions of verified-correct traces. Across Qwen2.5-Math-7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Cue-GRPO improves AIME repeated-sampling performance, with the largest gains at high sampling budgets. Credit Re- distribution (CR) under Judge Partitions (JP) further indi- cates that the proposed redistribution mechanism can oper- ate with judge-derived partitions. Cue-GRPO adds only 6% wall-clock training overhead over GRPO. These results sup- port structure-level credit redistribution as a practical design axis for RLVR, with Strategy Cues providing a low-overhead implementation for competition mathematics. Code is avail- able at https://github.com/CzZ12/When-Correct-Solutions- Repeat-Rarity-Aware-Credit-Redistribution-for-GRPO.

</details>


### [280] [ToolLIFT: Lifting Tool-Specific Trajectories into Function-Level Graphs for Generalizable Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03468)
*Xiuhui You,Jiayi Luo,Zichao Shen,Qingyun Sun,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolLIFT是一个将工具级轨迹提升为功能级工作流图（FWG）的框架，通过解耦工作流规划与工具选择，并结合强化学习与源门控奖励，实现跨工具集的泛化工具规划，在多个分布内和分布外基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于历史工具使用轨迹构建工具级图的方法与特定工具绑定，难以在不同工具集之间泛化。研究发现功能级工作流结构在相似任务中具有共性，可作为更可迁移的工具规划抽象。

Method: 1) 提出轨迹提升机制，将工具特定轨迹编码为功能级工作流图（FWG），跨工具共享协作经验；2) 基于FWG全局结构，引入解耦的工作流规划与工具选择，使个体工具选择与整体工作流对齐；3) 采用强化学习（RL），提出源门控和技能特定奖励，确保工具调用间信息的源可追溯数据流。

Result: 在两个分布内（ID）和三个分布外（OOD）基准上，ToolLIFT一致优于最先进的基线方法，展示了在未见工具集上的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: ToolLIFT验证了功能级工作流结构作为工具规划抽象的有效性，能够有效解决工具级图泛化能力不足的问题，为LLM工具规划提供了更通用的框架。

Abstract: Historical tool-use trajectories provide valuable experience for large language model (LLM) agents to plan and coordinate tool usage. Existing approaches directly construct tool-level graphs from these trajectories, but the resulting graphs remain tied to specific tools and are hard to generalize across tool sets. To tackle this challenge, we find that despite differences in the tools involved, analogous tasks often share a common function-level workflow structure, which serves as a potentially more transferable abstraction for tool planning. Based on this insight, we propose ToolLIFT, a framework that lifts tool-specific trajectories into a function-level workflow graph (FWG) for generalizable tool planning. Specifically, we first propose a trajectory-lifting mechanism that encodes workflow structures in the FWG and shares collaboration experience across tools. Then, building on the global structure of the FWG, we introduce decoupled workflow planning and tool selection to align individual tool choices with the overall workflow. Lastly, to ensure reliable tool dataflow, we adopt Reinforcement Learning (RL) and propose source-gated and skill-specific rewards to maintain source-traceable information flow across tool calls. Experiments on two in-distribution (ID) and three out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks show that ToolLIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen tool sets.

</details>


### [281] [WeClawArena: An Auditable Sandbox and Benchmark for Cross-User Agents Collaboration and Security in Human-Centered Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03499)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Aojie Yuan,Haiyue Zhang,Xiyang Hu,Yue Zhao,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WeClawArena，一个用于多方拥有代理协作的可审计基准测试和运行时沙箱，包含124个基础任务和620个场景变体，可同时评估协作效用与攻击成功率，并支持对任务故障、隐私泄露、投毒证据和非法权限路径的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准研究了工具使用和协作，但缺乏端到端的沙箱来验证跨用户代理协作，无法测试有害行为如何在以人为中心的代理网络中传播，也无法在真实的个人工作空间中进行验证。

Method: WeClawArena构建了一个可审计的运行时沙箱，覆盖6个跨用户任务域的124个基础任务，扩展为620个场景变体（每个基础任务含1个良性控制和4个攻击向量变体），沙箱记录对等消息、工具调用、资源操作、治理决策和最终工作区状态，并分别报告效用和攻击成功率。

Result: 基准包含620个场景变体，覆盖多种攻击向量（隐私泄露、毒化证据、非法权限路径等），并支持从有界运行时证据审计攻击成功，能够诊断任务分解失败、隐私泄露、证据投毒和无效权限路径等问题。

Conclusion: WeClawArena为多方拥有代理协作提供了首个可审计的基准和沙箱环境，弥补了现有基准在跨用户协作安全评估方面的空白，为验证有害行为在代理网络中的传播提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in persistent personal-agent frameworks are making human-centered agent networks realistic deployment targets: each user can be served by an AI agent that acts on the user's behalf, maintains state, and communicates with other agents through social and task relations. In these networks, everyday tool use becomes multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces, where files, records, tools, and policies are not directly visible across owners. Existing agent benchmarks study tool use and collaboration, but they do not provide an end-to-end sandbox for verifiable cross-user agent collaboration with realistic user digital workspaces or test how harmful actions can travel through the human-centered agent network. We introduce WeClawArena, an auditable benchmark and runtime sandbox for multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces. WeClawArena targets collaborative tool-use tasks in which personal workspaces serve as both operational tools and personal constraints. The benchmark contains 124 base tasks across six cross-user task domains and expands them into 620 scenario variants, with one benign control and four attack-vector variants per base task. The sandbox records peer messages, tool calls, resource operations, governed decisions, and final workspace states. WeClawArena reports utility and attack success rate separately and audits attack success from bounded runtime evidence, supporting diagnosis of task breakdown, privacy leakage, poisoned evidence, and invalid authority paths.

</details>


### [282] [Can LLM design high-quality experiments? A Comprehensive and Systematic Benchmark on Autonomous Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03501)
*Zejun Liu,Jian Wu,Ru Peng,Yuliang Ji,Dongyuan Li,Renhe Jiang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE基准，用于评估LLM在科研实验设计中的能力，涵盖300篇顶级会议论文、19个研究领域，从高层规划完整性和低层配置准确性两个维度评测；同时提出OptED智能体工作流来优化LLM的实验设计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI4Research研究主要聚焦代码实现与执行，忽略了实验设计这一科研关键阶段，且缺乏系统评估AI实验设计能力的基准。

Method: 构建SCOPE基准（300篇ICML/NeurIPS/ICLR等顶级会议论文、19个领域），从高层规划（主实验、消融实验、分析实验）和低层配置（数据集、基线和指标）两个维度评估LLM；并提出OptED智能体工作流，通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束优化实验设计。

Result: 评测发现：（1）大多数LLM无法直接设计高质量实验；（2）所有LLM在低层配置上存在性能瓶颈；（3）搜索模式不能提升设计质量。

Conclusion: OptED通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束能有效缓解配置瓶颈，提升LLM的实验设计能力。

Abstract: AI for Research (AI4Research) leverages AI to automate and improve scientific workflows. While experimental design is a critical stage of the research process, prior work has focused primarily on code implementation and execution, overlooking the importance of this stage, and no benchmark exists to evaluate AI's ability to conduct systematic experiment design. To bridge this gap, we propose SCOPE, a Scientific COmprehensive Planning Evaluation Benchmark constructed from 300 high-quality latest papers across 19 research domains from top-tier venues (e.g., ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR),evaluating LLMs on two dimensions: High-Level planning completeness (main, ablation, and analysis experiments) and Low-Level configuration accuracy and rationality (datasets, baselines, and metrics). Benchmarking reveals three findings: (1) most LLMs cannot directly design high-quality experiments; (2) all LLMs exhibit a performance bottleneck in low-level configuration; and (3) search mode does not improve design quality. Furthermore, to address these challenges, we propose OptED, a novel agentic workflow to optimize LLM-based experimental design, that enhances LLM-based experimental planning through stage isolation, tool augmentation, and rule-based constraints, effectively alleviating the configuration bottleneck.

</details>


### [283] [Hybrid LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agents for Complex Sequential Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03502)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé Ulrich Gaba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LLM增强的强化学习智能体，将LLM的规划能力与RL的动作优化能力相结合，在长时序决策任务中实现了比单独使用LLM或RL更好的采样效率、成功率和动作连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基智能体在长时序决策任务中难以精确优化动作并与环境交互；而RL虽然擅长序列控制，但缺乏高层抽象和任务分解能力。两者各自的局限性促使研究者探索混合方案。

Method: 提出LLM增强的强化学习智能体架构：LLM负责生成子目标、结构化计划和上下文指导（高层规划），RL智能体通过与环境的交互来优化低层动作（底层控制），两者协同完成复杂序列决策任务。

Result: 在序列决策任务上，该混合方法相比RL-only和LLM-only基线，实现了更高的采样效率、更高的任务成功率以及更连贯的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: LLM与RL的混合范式是构建更强大自主系统的一个有前景的方向，通过互补各自的优势来应对复杂决策任务。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool-use, enabling new forms of autonomous agents. However, LLM-based agents struggle with long-horizon sequential decision tasks that require precise action optimization and environment interaction. Reinforcement Learning (RL), while effective for sequential control, often lacks the high-level abstraction and task decomposition abilities needed for complex scenarios. This paper introduces an LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent that integrates LLM-driven planning with RL-based action optimization. The proposed architecture leverages the LLM to generate subgoals, structured plans, and contextual guidance, while the RL agent refines low-level actions through interaction with the environment. Experiments on sequential decision tasks demonstrate improved sample efficiency, higher success rates, and more coherent action trajectories compared to RL-only and LLM-only baselines. This hybrid paradigm highlights a promising direction for building more capable autonomous systems.

</details>


### [284] [When Many Answers Are Valid, Voting Fails: Symbolic Verification for Best-of-K Causal Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03506)
*Omatharv Bharat Vaidya,Connor Thomas Jerzak,Zayne Rea Sprague,Fangcong Yin,Nhat Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CALVER，一种无需训练的符号验证器，通过评分结构化推理轨迹是否符合Pearl因果准则（如d-分离、后门调整、干预）来选择最可靠的推理答案。在CLEAR数据集的"find-one-valid"问题上，CALVER达到42.1%准确率，远超多数投票、奖励模型、LLM裁判等方法（均约30%），且优势随采样预算扩大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性方法假设多数投票选择最频繁的答案是可靠的，但在因果推理中此假设失效：采样轨迹常常重复相同的混淆误差，且投票分散在多个合法答案上，导致无效答案获胜。需要一种不依赖参考答案的因果有效性验证机制。

Method: CALVER是一种训练免费（training-free）的符号验证器，对每条结构化推理轨迹依据Pearl因果准则进行评分，包括d-分离、后门调整和干预等因果标准，然后选择得分最高的候选答案，全程无需参考标准答案。它仅需要因果结构，可以直接提供或从文本中自动构建。

Result: 在CLEAR的find-one-valid查询中，CALVER达到42.1%准确率，而多数投票、奖励模型、LLM裁判和模型置信度等方法在相同的冻结池上均仅约30%。将裁判扩展到72B参数也无法缩小差距。在审核后的干净核心子集中，21个图合法选择中有11个与基准答案不同但仍满足谓词要求。优势随采样预算扩大而增加，在十个已发表贝叶斯网络上可复现。

Conclusion: CALVER通过因果有效性聚合推理选择，为因果推理中的答案验证提供了可行方案。它在多种设置下均优于传统自一致性方法，且计算开销极低（CPU上毫秒级评分）。只要具备因果结构（直接提供或从文本构建），即可应用该方法进行选择聚合。

Abstract: Self-consistency assumes the most frequent answer among sampled reasoning traces is the most reliable, but this can fail in causal reasoning: samples often repeat the same confounding error, and votes fragment across multiple valid answers, letting an invalid answer win despite a valid minority trace. We introduce CALVER (Causal Axiom-Level VERification), a training-free symbolic verifier that scores structured traces against Pearl's causal criteria, including -separation, backdoor adjustment, and intervention, and selects the highest-scoring candidate without consulting a reference answer. On CLEAR find-one-valid queries that admit multiple graph-valid answers, CALVER reaches 42.1% where plurality, a reward model, an LLM judge, and model confidence remain near 30% on identical frozen pools. Scaling the judge to 72B does not close the gap. In an audited clean-core subset, 11 of 21 graph-valid CALVER selections differ from the benchmark's listed answer while still satisfying the requested predicate. The advantage widens with the sampling budget and reproduces across ten published Bayesian networks, a second model family, and settings where the model must build the graph from text. CALVER also improves thresholded average-treatment-effect decisions against exact ground truth, generalizes to logic under a truth-table checker, and scores each candidate in milliseconds on CPU. CALVER needs only a causal structure, supplied outright or built from the text; wherever that holds, selection can aggregate via causal validity.

</details>


### [285] [Reversing Arrows in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03512)
*Sefika Efeoglu,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大规模语言模型（LLMs）在逆关系方向性上的建模能力。通过包含5,457个实例、27个逆关系标签的基准测试，在多项选择提示框架下评估了五个开源LLM，并考察了关系描述和实体表示对性能的影响。研究发现LLMs在逆关系分类上存在系统性不对称，关系描述并不总能提升性能，且模型性能对实体表示的变化敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在文本到知识图谱生成任务上表现优异，但其是否准确建模了逆关系的方向依赖语义（如mother与child之间参数顺序颠倒导致语义改变）仍不清楚。此前缺乏对这一问题的系统性研究。

Method: 构建包含5,457个实例、覆盖27个逆关系标签的基准测试集；在多项选择提示框架下评估五个开源LLM；通过将原始实体替换为合成实体和掩码实体，考察关系描述和实体表示对性能的影响。

Result: 发现LLMs在逆关系分类上存在系统性不对称；关系描述并不总能稳定提升模型性能；模型性能对实体表示的变化表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: LLMs在逆关系方向性建模方面存在系统性缺陷，现有方法（如添加关系描述）并不能一致地解决这一问题，且实体表示的扰动会影响模型表现，提示未来需要更鲁棒的方法来改善LLMs对方向依赖语义的建模。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on text-to-knowledge graph generation and related tasks. Nevertheless, it is still unclear whether they accurately model the direction-dependent semantics of inverse relations, in which reversing the order of the arguments alters the meaning of a relation (e.g., \textit{mother} versus \textit{child}). To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first systematic study of inverse relation directionality in LLMs, using a benchmark consisting of 5,457 instances spanning 27 distinct inverse relation labels. We evaluate five open-source LLMs under a multiple-choice prompting framework and further examine the influence of relation descriptions and entity representations by substituting the original entities with synthetic and masked entities. Our findings reveal systematic asymmetries in inverse relation classification across LLMs, indicate that relation descriptions do not consistently improve performance, and show that model performance can be sensitive to variations in entity representations.

</details>


### [286] [Dr. AGENTONOMICS: A Didactic Experiment of AGENTONOMICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03524)
*Fengjunjie Pan,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AGENTONOMICS框架及其实例化应用Dr. AGENTONOMICS——一个在TUM商学院课程中开发的教学代理，它既是学生学习的研究对象，也是学习框架的媒介工具，并提出了其向讲师代理、设计顾问和元代理三种累积性角色扩展的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将AI代理作为经济实体进行设计、管理和治理，并通过教学实践验证该框架的可行性，同时研究代理如何能够教授、应用甚至复制其设计所依据的框架本身。

Method: 采用基于Web的检索增强型导师原型系统，构建统一的AGENTONOMICS设计与管理参考框架（ADMRF），通过共享接口、智能层、工具、知识库和生态系统连接，由编排器选择任务特定的算法来支持四种累积性代理角色。

Result: 当前已实现web-based检索增强型导师原型，并论证了系统可扩展至三种额外角色（虚拟讲师、设计顾问和元代理），且这些角色具有累积性——共享相同的底层架构组件。

Conclusion: AGENTONOMICS框架及其教学代理应用展示了一种多元中心AI经济的可能性，为AI代理作为自我反思和自我复制系统的设计管理提供了新范式，并邀请学术界进一步讨论相关议题。

Abstract: AGENTONOMICS is a framework that treats AI agents as economic entities that can be designed, managed, and governed through an integrated management architecture. Dr. AGENTONOMICS is its first application: a lecture agent developed in the context of the TUM course on AI agents in business administration. Conceived during the winter semester 2025/26 and first introduced to students in the summer semester 2026, it serves as a didactic experiment in which the agent is both the object that students study and the medium through which they learn and apply the framework. The current prototype is a web-based, retrieval-grounded tutor that explains AGENTONOMICS concepts and supports student questions. This report argues that the same system can grow beyond tutoring into three additional cumulative roles: an avatar lecturer that delivers multimodal instruction, a design consultant that guides students through the AGENTONOMICS Design & Management Reference Framework (ADMRF), and a meta-agent that helps construct the agents students have specified. These roles are cumulative because they share the same interface, intelligence layer, tools, knowledge base, and ecosystem connection, while an orchestrator selects the role-specific algorithm required for each task. We present the architecture of the prototype, outline its development roadmap, and discuss its implications for a polycentric AI economy. This report is intended to invite further discussion on how agents can teach, apply, and eventually reproduce the frameworks by which they are designed.

</details>


### [287] [Behaviorally Adaptive Visual Diversion for Inclusive and Resilient Digital Assessment Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03531)
*Gupta Lovi Raj,kaur Kamalpreet,Dama Sriram,Parali Prajithaa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为自适应视觉干扰（BAVD）框架，通过合成非语义视觉场与评估内容复合，并根据考生行为动态调节，在不改变底层内容的前提下降低屏幕截图/共享的有用性，同时兼顾视觉处理障碍考生的可访问性需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字评估安全机制（浏览器锁定、摄像头监控、行为分析）通常是独立设计和评估的，且常忽视学习者的可访问性需求，缺乏一个既保证评估安全又不损害合法考生体验的统合解决方案。

Method: 提出BAVD理论框架，采用耦合动力系统表示，包含Diversion Field Generator、Rendering Tensor、Behavior Tensor、Composite Integrity Functional和Multi-dimensional Entropy Model五个核心组件，并推导内容保真度、渲染稳定性、熵有界性、完整性追踪及闭环自适应稳定性的理论性质。

Result: 框架建立了内容保真度、渲染稳定性、熵有界性、完整性追踪和闭环自适应稳定性的数学理论基础，明确了威胁模型、部署假设与局限性，并给出了可访问性与防截屏捕获之间的权衡分析。

Conclusion: 该工作为行为自适应且可访问性感知的评估交付提供了数学基础，为未来在可信数字评估平台中的实证验证奠定基础，但其有效性仍需实际数据检验。

Abstract: Institutions increasingly rely on browser lockdown, webcam monitoring, and behavioral analytics to secure high-stakes digital assessments, yet these mechanisms are commonly designed and evaluated independently and often overlook learner accessibility. This paper introduces Behaviorally-Adaptive Visual Diversion (BAVD), a theoretical framework in which a synthetic, non-semantic visual field is composited with assessment content and adaptively modulated according to observed candidate behavior. The underlying assessment content is never altered; only its visual presentation is modified to reduce the usefulness of unauthorized screen capture or screen sharing while remaining minimally intrusive for legitimate candidates. The framework further incorporates an accessibility-aware attenuation mechanism that reduces or suppresses diversion intensity for candidates with approved visual-processing accommodations. We formulate the model using a coupled dynamical-systems representation comprising a Diversion Field Generator, Rendering Tensor, Behavior Tensor, Composite Integrity Functional, and Multi-dimensional Entropy Model, and establish theoretical properties for content fidelity, rendering stability, entropy boundedness, integrity tracking, and closed-loop adaptation stability. The framework explicitly states its threat model, identifies deployment assumptions and limitations, and discusses the trade-off between accessibility and capture resistance. This work provides a mathematically grounded foundation for behaviorally adaptive and accessibility-aware assessment delivery and offers a basis for future empirical validation in trusted digital assessment platforms.

</details>


### [288] [Soft Guidance Starts to Outperform CoT Prompting as LLMs Improve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03550)
*Denys Pushkin,Albert Q. Jiang,Aryo Lotfi,Colin Sandon,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现现代中等规模语言模型在数学推理任务上，简单的零样本设置优于少样本CoT提示，推理专用模型无需额外成本即可超越官方报告结果，并提出了"引导-干扰"权衡来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示一直是评估模型推理能力的标准基线，但许多现代LLM在遇到推理任务时已经"原生"产生CoT式回答，这促使研究者重新审视标准CoT提示的有效性。

Method: 在数学问题求解任务上评估多个现代中等规模语言模型，比较零样本设置与少样本CoT提示的性能差异。

Result: 推理专用模型在零样本设置下表现优于少样本CoT基线（如Mathstral在GSM8K上从约77%提升到约84%），通用模型用零样本CoT提示也足以超越少样本CoT基线。

Conclusion: 标准CoT提示要求风格适应、格式合规以及可能不需要的上下文化，这些会分散模型对核心推理任务的注意力。随着模型能力增强，使用标准CoT提示越来越成为一种干扰源。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting remains the standard baseline for evaluating models' reasoning abilities. Originally, this technique was introduced to elicit step-by-step reasoning from large language models (LLMs), which would otherwise tend to directly output the final answer. However, many modern LLMs produce CoT-style responses \textit{natively} when presented with reasoning tasks, which made us revisit the effectiveness of standard CoT prompting.
  We evaluate several modern mid-sized language models on a math problem-solving task and find that models specialized for reasoning achieve better performance in a simple zero-shot setting than when using few-shot CoT examples - significantly surpassing officially reported results at no additional cost (e.g., from $\sim$77\% to $\sim$84\% for Mathstral on GSM8K). For the tested general-purpose model, a zero-shot CoT prompt is also sufficient to outperform a few-shot CoT baseline. We attribute this to a `guidance-distraction' tradeoff: standard CoT prompting also demands style adaptation, formatting compliance, and potentially undesired contextualization, which can distract models from the core reasoning task.
  Our findings suggest that using standard CoT prompting increasingly acts as a source of distraction as models grow stronger.

</details>


### [289] [Enhancing Tabular Learners with Context-Aware Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03565)
*Günther Schindler,Maximilian Schambach,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，利用预填充KV缓存的上下文感知策略，将Gemma 3语言模型的语义理解能力与表格学习器的统计能力相结合，通过上下文化行嵌入显著提升表格学习器在语义丰富数据集上的性能，尤其在低数据场景下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格学习器将文本特征视为离散符号，忽略了特征名称和单元格内容中的丰富语义信息，导致模型在"语义真空"中运行，无法充分利用数据的语义上下文。

Method: 提出CASE（Context-Aware Semantic Embeddings）框架，核心策略是通过预填充自定义训练的Gemma 3表格语言模型的KV缓存，用代表性行样本建立数据集的语义锚点，使生成的行嵌入动态上下文化，消除语义歧义并锚定领域特定上下文。

Result: 在CARTE、TextTab和TabArena等多个基准上的实验表明，CASE在语义丰富数据集上显著提升了表格学习器的性能，尤其在低数据（低样本）场景中提升最为明显。

Conclusion: CASE有效弥合了LLM语义理解与表格学习器统计分析之间的鸿沟，通过上下文感知的嵌入策略证明了语义上下文能显著增强表格学习的表现，为表格学习与语义理解融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: While modern tabular learners excel at capturing statistical patterns, they frequently operate in a semantic vacuum, treating textual features as discrete symbols, ignoring the rich semantics inherent in feature names or cell entries. We propose CASE (Context-Aware Semantic Embeddings), a novel framework that bridges the gap between the semantic understanding of Large Language Models (LLMs) and the statistical capabilities of tabular learners. Unlike existing methods that embed rows in isolation, CASE utilizes a contextualization strategy: we pre-fill the KV cache of a custom-trained Gemma 3-based Tabular Language Model with a representative sample of rows to establish a persistent anchor of the dataset's semantics. This ensures that generated row embeddings are dynamically contextualized, resolving semantic ambiguities and anchoring representations in domain-specific context. Our experiments across several benchmarks (CARTE, TextTab, and TabArena) demonstrate that CASE substantially improves the performance of tabular learners on semantically rich datasets, particularly in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [290] [Adversarial Fast-Moving Real-World Domains as Test Beds for Benchmarking AI Scientist Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03569)
*William Bolton,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的AI科学家能力评估框架，利用F1赛车设计与MTG卡牌构筑两个真实世界中具有对抗性的快速变化领域来评估AI模型的想法生成、推理和新颖性能力。结果表明模型能生成合理输出但难以对齐专家解决方案，关键能力差距在于筛选、优先级排序和连贯新颖性，而非单纯的想法生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估AI科学家生成新颖想法的基准存在缺陷：要么依赖合成任务，要么依赖回溯性目标，可能受到先前暴露数据的混淆。作者假设利用复杂、对抗性、快速变化的真实世界领域，由专家独立生成可观测输出，可以为评估AI科学家的推理、新颖性和假设形成能力提供实际解决方案。

Method: 在两个结构不同的真实领域实例化评估框架：(1) F1领域，模型为2026赛季构思赛车设计概念，以真实的季前创新作为金标准；(2) MTG领域，模型从近期更新的卡牌池中提出构筑套牌，以19个职业巡回赛套牌列表为评估基准。通过模型输出与现实专家解决方案的匹配程度来量化评估。

Result: 两个领域中模型都能产生看似合理的输出，但很少有输出与真实世界的专家解决方案一致。在F1中，最佳模型GPT-5.2在40个真实创新中匹配了10项。在MTG中，Gemini 3 Flash的最佳套牌从第三名PT套牌中恢复了7张新卡中的5张；在全部108套牌中，模型最常选择的卡牌也是PT套牌最广泛采用的卡牌（Spearman ρ=0.74, p=0.0003）。

Conclusion: 结果显示AI科学家在想法生成方面已具备一定能力，但关键的能力差距在于筛选（filtering）、优先级排序（prioritization）和连贯新颖性（coherent novelty）——即从大量生成的想法中甄别并组织出真正的创新。

Abstract: Benchmarking the ability of AI scientists to generate novel ideas is notoriously difficult. Existing benchmarks in this field have made progress in evaluating scientific reasoning and research replication, but often rely on synthetic tasks or retrospective targets, which may be confounded by prior exposure. We hypothesize that complex, adversarial, fast-moving real-world domains where expert practitioners independently generate observable outputs can provide a practical solution to fill this gap and evaluate the capabilities needed for AI scientists, including reasoning, novelty, and hypothesis formulation. We instantiate this framework in two structurally different domains, Formula 1 (F1), where models ideate around car design concepts for the 2026 season, and real pre-season innovations provide a ground truth, and Magic: The Gathering (MTG), where models propose decks from a recently updated card pool and are evaluated against 19 Pro Tour (PT) decklists. Across both domains, models produce plausible outputs, but few align with real-world expert solutions. In F1, the best model, GPT-5.2 matched 10 of 40 real innovations with 166 ideas proposed across runs. In MTG, the best deck from Gemini 3 Flash recovered 5 of 7 new-set cards from the third-place PT deck, and across all 108 decks, the cards models selected most often were also the cards most widely adopted by PT decks (Spearman $ρ= 0.74$, $p = 0.0003$). These results suggest that a key capability gap for AI scientists is not idea generation, but filtering, prioritization, and coherent novelty.

</details>


### [291] [Policy Fragmentation or Institutional Alignment? Institutional Governance of AI in Universities and Business Schools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03584)
*Lydia Manikonda,Dominique Outlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过自然语言处理（NLP）分析了美国34个州的高等教育机构（HEI）的人工智能（AI）政策，发现大学层面政策侧重数据安全与风险缓解，而学院层面政策（若存在）则关注教学应用与工具使用。商学院层面的独立AI政策较少，与学科学习目标存在错位，对教师、学生及认证工作构成挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育机构在AI快速渗透高技能领域的背景下，面临如何在课程中引入AI工具同时保持基础原理教学的挑战。机构政策和治理层面对AI政策的制定存在困惑，尤其当政策在不同层级（大学整体与各学院）上制定时，缺乏对政策内容和层级差异的系统性理解。

Method: 收集美国34个州的高等教育机构AI政策作为研究样本，采用自然语言处理（NLP）技术对机构AI政策进行文本分析，比较大学层面政策与学院层面（特别是商学院）政策在内容侧重点上的差异。

Result: 研究发现大学层面政策强调数据安全与风险缓解，而学院层面政策侧重教学应用与工具使用。商学院中只有相对较少的机构制定了区别于大学框架的独立AI政策，导致与学科特定学习目标不一致，给教师、学生和认证工作带来挑战。

Conclusion: AI政策制定应与更广泛的机构政策保持一致，同时兼顾学科特定的学习目标和不断演变的劳动力市场需求。研究为高等教育机构在不同层级制定和协调AI政策提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly transforming high-skilled domains, requiring higher education institutions (HEI) to balance the teaching of foundational principles with the integration of emerging tools to ensure workforce readiness. While HEI are increasingly adopting AI, many continue to grapple with how it should be incorporated into curricula and governed through policy, especially when such policies are set at different levels of an institution. This research analyzes AI policies across HEI from 34 states in the United States to investigate what these policies entail and how policies set across institutions as well as within different levels at an institution differ. Using natural language processing (NLP) to analyze institutional AI policies, we find a clear divergence: university-level policies emphasize data security and risk mitigation whereas school-level policies, when present, focus on pedagogical applications and tool usage. When focusing on business school specific policies, relatively few business schools maintain AI policies distinct from university frameworks, creating misalignment with discipline-specific learning objectives. This gap poses challenges particularly for faculty and students as well as for accreditation purposes. Our insights suggest that guidelines should be aligned with broader institutional policies while addressing discipline-specific learning objectives and evolving workforce demands.

</details>


### [292] [From Social Coding to Agentic Coding: Productivity and Relational Reconfiguration in Open-Source Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03585)
*Mengying Zhou,Yongjie Yin,Yang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于LLM的多智能体仿真，以GitHub真实数据初始化，对比引入编码代理（CA）与未引入的并行社区状态。结果显示CA使计划任务和完成任务分别提升34.0%和39.0%，中位完成时间从45分钟降至20分钟，但采用率仅26.0%，收益集中在活跃且连接良好的开发者。同时，人类直接交互从32.4%降至11.6%，CA介入模式增至57.3%，且CA生成的知识语料覆盖率仅为22.3%（真实人类语料为81.1%），揭示了生产力与公共知识之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社区作为数字公共基础设施，不仅生产代码，还生成了公共知识和人际关系的可见协作。编码代理（CA）虽提升开发效率，但将部分活动从公开的人类交互转向私密的人机循环，这一转变对社区公共知识生成和知识可用性的影响尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用基于LLM的多智能体仿真方法，使用1,084名活跃开发者及其仓库关系的真实GitHub数据进行初始化。经过历史提交预热后，将相同社区状态分叉为无CA和有CA两个并行条件，进行4周仿真对比实验，并通过标准化检索基准评估知识语料覆盖能力。

Result: CA引入使计划任务提升34.0%、完成任务提升39.0%，中位完成时间从45分钟缩短至20分钟。但采用率仅26.0%，收益集中于本身更活跃和连接更好的开发者。人类直接交互从32.4%降至11.6%，CA介入模式增至57.3%（其中40.3%为CA辅助自循环）。CA知识语料在标准化基准上仅达22.3%覆盖率，远低于真实人类语料的81.1%，且需更多检索步骤、成功率更低。

Conclusion: 编码代理在提高技术生产效率的同时，将更多工作推向代理中介或私密循环，导致公共记录对未来贡献者的可用性下降，形成了生产力与公共知识之间的张力。这一发现对开源社区治理和AI工具设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Open-source software communities are a form of digital public infrastructure that not only produces code, but also generates public knowledge and interpersonal relationships through visible collaboration. Generative coding agents (CAs) are an advanced tool to improve development efficiency while shifting part of activities from public human interaction to private human-agent loops. We study this shift using an LLM-based multi-agent simulation initialized with real GitHub data from 1,084 active developers and their repository relationships. After a warm-up with historical commits, we branch the same community state into parallel No-CA and CA conditions for 4-week simulations. CA introduction increases planned and completed tasks by 34.0% and 39.0%, respectively, and reduces median completion time from 45 to 20 minutes. However, adoption reaches only 26.0%, and the gains concentrate among developers who are already more active and well connected. CAs also restructure task execution pathways. Direct human-human interaction declines from 32.4% to 11.6%, while CA-involved modes increase to 57.3%, including 40.3% completed through CA-assisted self-loops. Public knowledge generated under CA condition also provides less support for later tasks. On a standardized retrieval benchmark, the CA corpus achieves 22.3% knowledge coverage, far below the 81.1% achieved by the real-human corpus, and requires more retrieval steps with a lower success rate. These results reveal a productivity-public knowledge tension: coding agents increase technical production, but more work shifts to agent-mediated or private loops, leaving public records less useful to future contributors.

</details>


### [293] [FOUND-AF: Benchmarking ECG Foundation Models for Atrial Fibrillation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03597)
*Amirhossein Taleshinosrati,Yangyang Wang,Atitaya Phoemsuk,Vahid Abolghasemi,Naser Hossein Motlagh,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,Daniel Teichmann,Abdolrahman Peimankar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FOUND-AF统一基准框架，在相同实验条件下系统评估九种公开ECG基座模型在四种异构数据集上的房颤检测性能，发现ECGFounder模型在准确率和计算效率之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG基座模型在房颤检测中的相对有效性不清楚，因为不同研究使用了不同的数据集、预处理流程、分类器和验证协议，缺乏统一的公平比较基准。

Method: 构建了FOUND-AF基准框架，以冻结特征提取器的方式使用九种来自五个家族的公开基座模型（包括HuBERT-ECG、CLEF、ST-MEM、ECG-JEPA和ECGFounder），采用标准化预处理、模型原生重采样、固定XGBoost分类器和记录级分组交叉验证，在四种异构ECG数据集（AFDB、CinC2017、CPSC2021、LTAFDB）上进行评估，并包含分类指标、ROC分析、配对记录级bootstrap比较（Holm校正）、嵌入空间可视化和计算效率分析。

Result: ECGFounder模型在各数据集上一致性地取得了最强的综合性能，并在准确率、模型大小、推理时间和内存使用之间提供了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: FOUND-AF提供了一个可复现的框架来选择ECG基座模型，并证明了紧凑的、临床预训练的编码器能够在异构采集环境下支持稳健且计算高效的房颤检测。

Abstract: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most common sustained cardiac arrhythmia and is associated with increased risks of stroke, heart failure, and mortality. Recent ECG foundation models offer transferable representations for automated AF detection. However, their relative effectiveness remains unclear because existing studies use different datasets, preprocessing procedures, classifiers, and validation protocols. This study presents FOUND-AF, a unified, leakage-controlled, and deployment-oriented benchmarking framework that evaluates the quality of pretrained ECG representations under identical experimental conditions. Nine publicly available foundation models from five families, including HuBERT-ECG, CLEF, ST-MEM, ECG-JEPA, and ECGFounder, were evaluated across four heterogeneous ECG datasets, namely AFDB, CinC2017, CPSC2021, and LTAFDB. All models were used as frozen feature extractors with standardized preprocessing, model-native resampling, a fixed XGBoost classifier, and recording-level grouped cross-validation. The evaluation included classification metrics, receiver operating characteristic analysis, paired recording-level bootstrap comparisons with Holm correction, embedding-space visualization, and computational efficiency profiling. The ECGFounder model consistently achieved the strongest overall performance across datasets while offering a favorable trade-off between accuracy, model size, inference time, and memory usage. FOUND-AF therefore provides a reproducible framework for selecting ECG foundation models and demonstrates that compact, clinically pretrained encoders can support robust and computationally efficient AF detection across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

</details>


### [294] [Large language models for partial differential equation workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03600)
*Han Wan,Rui Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLM）在偏微分方程（PDE）研究中的应用进展，涵盖三个关键阶段：控制模型的发现与公式化、可执行数值求解器的生成与修订，以及利用仿真反馈支持控制、设计与优化。当前系统主要充当工作流级接口，但仍受限于高质量数据集和基准的稀缺，以及仿真与现实世界系统之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE在科学与工程中不仅是孤立公式，而是连接建模假设、控制方程、数值求解器、诊断与决策的可执行工作流。LLM开始通过连接自然语言、符号数学、代码、求解器输出与反馈来支持这类工作流，因此有必要系统审视其进展与局限。

Method: 采用综述方法，从三个维度系统梳理LLM辅助PDE研究：1）控制模型的发现与公式化；2）可执行数值求解器的生成与修订；3）仿真反馈在控制、设计与优化中的应用。

Result: 研究发现现有系统主要作为工作流级接口发挥作用，但在知识发现和真实世界应用中缺乏高质量数据集与基准，且仿真结果与现实工程系统之间仍存在显著差距，限制了数值模拟、控制策略和优化设计的直接转移。

Conclusion: LLM辅助PDE工作流是发展科学AI系统的关键试验平台，未来需要弥合语言、计算、物理约束与真实世界决策之间的联系，以克服数据稀缺和仿真-现实差距等核心挑战。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) become actionable in science and engineering not as isolated formulae, but as executable workflows that connect modelling assumptions, governing equations, numerical solvers, diagnostics, and decisions. Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to support such workflows by linking natural language, symbolic mathematics, code, solver outputs, and feedback. Here we examine recent advances in LLM-assisted PDE research across three stages: the discovery and formulation of governing models, the generation and revision of executable numerical solvers, and the use of simulation feedback to support control, design, and optimization. Across these stages, current systems act primarily as workflow-level interfaces. Despite this progress, the field remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality datasets and benchmarks, especially for knowledge discovery and real-world applications, where expert annotation, executable problem construction, and task-level feedback require substantial domain effort. A further challenge is the persistent gap between simulation-based results and real-world scientific and engineering systems, which limits the direct transfer of numerical simulations, control policies, and optimized designs to practical settings. These challenges make LLM-assisted PDE workflows a critical testbed for developing scientific AI systems that can connect language, computation, physical constraints, and real-world decision-making.

</details>


### [295] [FraQ: Efficient Coordinate-Space Recompression for Federated Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03605)
*Shenghui Li,Thiemo Voigt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FraQ是一种高效的联邦LoRA坐标空间重压缩方法，通过在权重空间精确聚合后使用正交基和Gram矩阵恢复奇异谱，以低开销选择最小满足能量阈值的秩，构建全局适配器，在保持精度的同时显著降低下行通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调中LoRA的两因子参数化会导致跨客户端聚合不匹配（简单平均因子无法恢复诱导更新的平均值），而通过全权重空间精确聚合再重压缩虽然能解决该问题，但分解稠密矩阵的计算和内存开销过大。

Method: FraQ从堆叠因子中精确表示聚合结果，将其分解为正交基和紧凑坐标矩阵；通过小规模Gram矩阵恢复奇异谱，选择满足预设能量阈值的最小秩，并将选定的坐标子空间映射回基以构造全局适配器。

Result: 在文本分类和常识推理基准上，FraQ在接近未压缩基线的精度的同时，大幅减少了下行通信量，且服务器端重压缩开销较低。

Conclusion: FraQ有效解决了联邦LoRA聚合不匹配与重压缩计算开销之间的权衡，实现了高精度、低通信成本和低服务器开销的联邦微调。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient collaborative adaptation of Large Language Models (LLMs) without centralizing private data. However, LoRA's two-factor parameterization creates an aggregation mismatch across clients: naively averaging the factors does not recover the average of their induced updates. This mismatch can be avoided by forming the exact aggregate in the full weight space and then recompressing it, but decomposing the resulting dense matrix is computationally expensive and memory-intensive. We propose FraQ, an efficient coordinate-space recompression method for federated LoRA. Starting from stacked factors that exactly represent the aggregate, FraQ factorizes it into an orthonormal basis and a compact coordinate matrix. It then recovers the singular spectrum from a small Gram matrix, selects the smallest rank satisfying a prescribed energy threshold, and maps the selected coordinate subspace back through the basis to construct the global adapter. Experiments on text classification and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that FraQ achieves accuracy close to uncompressed baselines while substantially reducing downlink communication with low server-side recompression overhead.

</details>


### [296] [Learning Clinical-Trial Strategy: Offline Policy Training for Decision Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03606)
*William Bolton,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了肿瘤药物临床开发中的离线决策问题，构建了包含31.7k条异构数据、881个离线决策片段的数据集，并对比了四种离线目标与四种前沿LLM智能体。结果显示离线训练模型显著优于基线，其中奖励加权行为克隆表现最佳（indication F1达46.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床开发是在不确定性下进行的序贯决策，赞助商需要从不一致的异构证据中规划实验组合。现有研究缺乏系统性的方法将多源临床证据转化为可学习的决策模型。

Method: 将肿瘤临床开发建模为离线决策问题，构建结合试验注册、监管审评、申办方备案、使用数据和流行病学的时间序列数据集（881个决策片段），对比行为克隆、奖励加权行为克隆、学习奖励训练和基于价值的隐式Q-learning四种离线目标，以及四种共享日期门控检索框架的LLM智能体。

Result: 离线训练模型在所有划分（药物、申办方、药物类别和时间）上均优于未微调基线；在2025年8月后的无污染验证集中优势尤为明显。奖励加权行为克隆表现最佳：indication F1为46.2%，严格F1为14.2%，而最佳工具智能体分别为25.0%和2.1%。

Conclusion: 结构化离线学习能够有效教导智能体规划临床试验，为临床开发决策提供了数据驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Clinical development is sequential decision-making under uncertainty, where a sponsor must plan a portfolio of experiments from heterogeneous evidence. We study this setting by framing oncology clinical development as an offline decision-making problem in which an agent predicts the next six-month trial portfolio of an oncology drug program from information available at the decision date. To support this, we construct a temporal dataset that combines 31.7k heterogeneous public data records, including trial registries, regulatory reviews, sponsor filings, utilization data, and epidemiology, into 881 offline decision episodes across 45 historical programs. We compare four offline objectives: behavioral cloning, reward-weighted behavioral cloning, learned-reward training, and value-based implicit Q-learning against four frontier LLM agents that share a common date-gated retrieval scaffold across held-out drug, sponsor, drug-class, and temporal splits. Models trained offline outperform the non-fine-tuned baselines, particularly in the post-August 2025 contamination-clean holdout. Reward-weighted behavioral cloning performs the best, obtaining 46.2% indication F1 and 14.2% strict F1 against 25.0% and 2.1%, respectively, for the best-performing tool agent on each metric. These results suggest that structured offline learning can teach agents to plan clinical experiments.

</details>


### [297] [Formal Verification of Agentic Systems over Operational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03609)
*Alejandro J. Mercado,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体系统的形式化验证问题。作者将这类系统形式化为"有状态工具启用的智能体部署"（STEADs），证明了对其的FO-CTL规范验证是不可判定的，但在有限域限制下找到了精确保持规范的条件，使验证复杂度为PSPACE完全。关键要求是数据中不透明标识符的重命名必须对应地重命名所选的工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体系统越来越多地部署在真实工作流中，处理持久性操作数据。现有验证方法主要局限于智能体接口层面的行为约束，无法提供系统级别的保障，因此需要对整个工作流执行和数据演化提供正式验证保证。

Method: 作者形式化了STEADs（有状态工具启用的智能体部署）及其语义，提出用FO-CTL（一阶计算树逻辑）规范来验证系统。通过识别有限域限制下的充分条件（标识符重命名与工具调用选择的对应关系），实现精确的规范保持，并提出规范化部署包装器以强制保证该条件。

Result: 证明了STEADs的FO-CTL验证问题是不可判定的；在有限域限制和标识符重命名条件满足时，验证问题为PSPACE完全；LLM驱动的智能体可能违反该条件，但规范化部署包装器可以对任意基础智能体保证该条件；计算规范化表示的复杂度是图同构困难。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM驱动的智能体系统提供了首个系统级别的形式化验证框架，在有限域条件下实现了可判定的验证，并通过案例管理工作流进行了说明，为实际部署中的可靠性保障奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic systems driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world workflows where they act on persistent operational data. Before deployment, these systems need to be verified against business requirements that govern workflow execution and data evolution. However, existing approaches do not provide such system-level guarantees, as they mainly constrain or analyse behaviour at the agent's interface level. We study here the verification of agentic systems comprising a single LLM and a tool orchestration harness over relational operational data. We formalise them as Stateful Tool-Enabled Agentic Deployments (STEADs), give their semantics, define the problem of verifying them against First-Order Computation Tree Logic (FO-CTL) specifications, and show that it is undecidable. We identify sufficient conditions for exact preservation of FO-CTL specifications under a finite-domain restriction, over which verification is PSPACE-complete. The key requirement is that renaming opaque identifiers in the data must correspondingly rename the selected tool calls. We show that LLM-driven agents can violate this condition and introduce a canonical deployment wrapper that guarantees it for arbitrary base agents while preserving already-equivariant behaviour. We prove that computing canonical representations required by this construction is graph-isomorphism-hard. Finally, we illustrate our framework on an LLM agent orchestrating a case-management workflow.

</details>


### [298] [Unequal Verdicts: Investigating Gender Bias in LLM-Based Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03627)
*Razieh Chalehchaleh,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地调查了LLM在假新闻检测中的性别偏见问题，通过在LIAR基准上引入三种性别变体（中性、男性、女性），评估了六个SOTA LLM，发现所有模型均表现出性别敏感性，9.79%-35.13%的陈述在不同性别变体下获得不一致的标签，且五种模型表现出统计显著的定向性别偏见（部分呈"男性怀疑"模式），表明性别偏见严重损害了LLM假新闻检测的可靠性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于自动化事实核查，但其在这一场景中的性别偏见问题尚未得到充分研究，作者旨在填补这一空白，首次系统性地探究LLM假新闻检测中的性别偏见。

Method: 对LIAR基准数据集进行增强，为每条陈述构建三种性别变体（中性、男性、女性）的说话者职位头衔，评估六个SOTA LLM在多个偏见与公平性指标上的表现，并识别偏见的两种主要表现形式：不稳定性（判断不一致）和方向性（系统性偏好）。

Result: 所有模型均表现出性别敏感性：9.79%-35.13%的陈述在三种变体间标签不一致，男女组对比的翻转率为6.5%-23.6%；五种模型表现出统计显著的定向效应，最强效应呈现"男性怀疑"模式。

Conclusion: 性别偏见同时损害了LLM假新闻检测的可靠性和公平性，凸显了开发偏见感知的评估与缓解策略的必要性；增强后的数据集已公开，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated fact-checking, yet their susceptibility to gender bias in this context remains underexplored. This study presents the first systematic investigation of gender bias in LLM-based fake news detection using real-world data. We augment the LIAR benchmark with three gender variants of speaker job titles (Neutral, Male, Female) for each statement to test whether veracity judgments vary solely based on gender presentation. Six state-of-the-art LLMs are evaluated across multiple bias and fairness metrics. All models exhibit gender sensitivity: 9.79%-35.13% of statements receive inconsistent labels across the three variants, with Male-Female comparisons showing 6.5%-23.6% flip rates. Two primary bias manifestations are identified: instability (inconsistent judgments) and directionality (systematic favoritism). Five models show statistically significant directional effects, with the strongest effects displaying male-skeptic patterns. These findings demonstrate that gender bias undermines both reliability and fairness in LLM-based fake news detection, highlighting the need for bias-aware evaluation and mitigation strategies. The augmented dataset is publicly released to support future research.

</details>


### [299] [Cross-Layer Interaction under Weight-Space Ablation: A Closed-Form Attention Jacobian Bound and a Test on a Real Pretrained Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03629)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了同伴论文关于激活修补与权重空间消融一致性的理论结果，将其从单一残差块推广到跨层场景，精确分离了同块项与跨层余项，并在真实模型Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上验证了雅可比界，同时在该模型中发现了一个涌现的间接宾语识别电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伴论文的结果局限于单一残差块且仅在小规模合成任务上验证。作者希望将该理论结果推广到多层架构，并检验其在真实规模模型上的适用性，同时探索该理论框架对涌现电路的预测能力。

Method: 首先将跨层消融的交互作用精确分解为逐层的同块项与跨层余项；其次对两层情形将余项精确表达为混合二阶导数的二重积分，并通过推导注意力子块的雅可比界闭式解来界定该余项；最后在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上使用激活修补方法搜索间接宾语识别电路，并测试其坍缩、解离与交互性质。

Result: 1) 跨层交互分解为同块项加跨层余项；2) 注意力子块的雅可比界以闭式形式导出，在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct的真实权重上无一违例地得到验证；3) 同伴论文中未显式给出的曲率常数得以闭式表达；4) 在模型中发现了涌现的间接宾语识别电路，五个实例中共享载体浮现，但坍缩和解离仅在大多数（而非全部）实例上成立，五个中有三个在超出同块情形的层对上测量到非零交互。

Conclusion: 该工作将交互分析从单块推进到多层，提供了跨层余项的精确刻画和可验证的界，但在真实模型上的实证结果呈现混合图景：理论同块情形下的性质在涌现电路上并非普遍成立，跨层情形的交互无法被现有同块定理覆盖，提示需要进一步的理论推广。

Abstract: A companion paper studies when activation patching and weight-space ablation agree, inside an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream. For the one composition in that model where two carriers are architecturally dependent, an attention head and its own layer's normalization-MLP composition, it derives an exact first-order interaction formula, zero when only the MLP is ablated and second-order bounded when the head is also ablated. That result is confined to a single residual block and checked only on small transformers on a synthetic task.
  This paper extends the result past both limits. First, the interaction from ablating carriers spanning several layers decomposes exactly into same-block terms, one per touched layer, plus a cross-layer remainder on which the decomposition makes no claim of smallness. Second, we isolate that remainder exactly, for two layers, as a double integral of a mixed second derivative, and name the missing ingredient needed to bound it: a Jacobian bound for the attention sub-block. We derive this bound in closed form and verify it, without a single violation, against Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct's real weights, though we do not yet chain it across layers. We also give, in closed form, the curvature constant the companion paper's bound leaves unexhibited.
  Third, on that same model, we search for and find an emergent circuit for indirect object identification, never designed into it, using the original activation-patching method for this task, and test collapse, dissociation, and interaction on it. The result is mixed: a shared carrier emerges across all five tested instances, collapse and dissociation hold on most but not all, and a nonzero interaction is measurable on three of five, at layer pairs outside the same-block case the companion theorem covers.

</details>


### [300] [When Teachers Mislead: Spurious-Signal-Aware On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03632)
*Yinuo Jiang,Yongjie Ye,Zhou Tao,Xiang Zhuang,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen,Tiankai Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-OPD框架，用于在在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中识别并过滤"虚假信号"（即弱输入依赖但产生大梯度的token级教师信号），通过输入根基性和优化影响双重判据实现细粒度的信号选择，在LLM和VLM实验中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性OPD方法仅关注信号的置信度、信息量或可学习性，却忽略了语言模型token级判断可能受输入无关的语言先验、格式惯例或刻板推理模板驱动，这类"虚假信号"虽产生大梯度但对任务改进贡献甚微。

Method: SA-OPD包含两个核心组件：（1）一个轻量级的输入根基性代理，用于估计token级蒸馏信号是否真正依赖输入；（2）仅过滤同时满足低输入根基性和极端蒸馏发散度的token，从而在不牺牲有效信号的前提下移除高影响的虚假更新。

Result: 在LLM和VLM两类设置下的大规模实验中，SA-OPD一致优于Vanilla OPD及多种竞争性选择性蒸馏方法，验证了输入根基性作为OPD监督信号选择的关键维度。

Conclusion: 输入根基性被确立为OPD监督选择中的重要维度，SA-OPD提供了一种简单有效的策略来缓解虚假更新问题，为在线蒸馏的信号筛选提供了新视角。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) transfers teacher capabilities by supervising student-sampled trajectories with dense token-level teacher signals. Recent selective OPD methods improve this process by prioritizing signals that are confident, informative, or learnable. However, the assumptions overlook a fundamental failure mode of language models: their token-level judgments can be driven by input-agnostic language priors, formatting conventions, or stereotyped reasoning templates rather than task-specific evidence. We refer to such optimization-relevant but weakly input-grounded supervision as spurious signals in OPD, which may produce large gradients while contributing little task-improving direction. To mitigate this issue, we propose SA-OPD, a Spurious-Signal-Aware On-Policy Distillation framework that identifies and filters misleading token-level supervision based on input-groundedness and optimization impact. SA-OPD introduces a lightweight input-groundedness proxy estimating whether a token-level distillation signal truly depends on the input. It then filters only tokens that simultaneously exhibit low input-groundedness and extreme distillation divergence, thereby removing high-impact spurious updates and achieving fine-grained OPD optimization. Extensive experiments on both large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) settings demonstrate that SA-OPD consistently outperforms Vanilla OPD and competitive selective methods. These results establish input-groundedness as a key dimension for OPD supervision selection and offer a simple, effective strategy for mitigating spurious updates.

</details>


### [301] [Is Inter-Seed Cross-Play Enough? Evaluating the Robustness of Zero-Shot Coordination Algorithms to Implementation Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03644)
*Maksymilian Wolski,Nicholas Hoernle,Johannes Forkel,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估了零样本协调（ZSC）算法对实现细节差异的鲁棒性，提出了一种新的评估方案"跨实现交叉对弈"，并发现对于Other-Play算法而言，标准ZSC评估是跨实现评估的合理代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZSC算法在实际部署中需要与未见过的智能体协调，但现有评估几乎都只使用单一实现配合不同随机种子进行测试，忽视了独立实现同一算法时产生的规范歧义和实现细节差异，这引发了对算法鲁棒性的质疑。

Method: 提出"跨实现交叉对弈"（cross-implementation cross-play）评估方案，系统改变先前研究已证明会影响多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法性能的实现细节，并使用该方案评估流行的ZSC算法Other-Play。

Result: 实验结果表明，对于Other-Play算法，标准ZSC评估结果与更全面的跨实现评估结果高度一致，说明标准评估在实践中是合理的代理方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果为ZSC算法的评估实践提供了积极的证据，表明在当前标准评估框架下获得的结果具有较好的稳健性，但同时也为更严格的评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: AI agents deployed in real-world settings must be capable of coordinating with humans and other AI agents they have not encountered before. Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) algorithms aim to achieve this by specifying high-level learning rules such that independently engineered agents can coordinate with each other at test time. Rigorous evaluation of ZSC algorithms remains difficult: ideally, multiple independent implementations of each proposed algorithm must be used, reflecting the variation that arises when independent parties interpret and implement the same specification. In practice, however, ZSC algorithms have almost exclusively been evaluated using a single implementation trained across different random seeds, with only a handful of works additionally varying the neural network architecture. This leaves open questions about robustness to specification ambiguities and implementation details. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of this robustness. We introduce a new evaluation scheme, cross-implementation cross-play, varying implementation details that prior work has shown to affect the performance of multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms, and we evaluate Other-Play, a popular ZSC algorithm, with this scheme. Our findings are encouraging and suggest that, for Other-Play, the standard ZSC evaluation is, in fact, a reasonable proxy for this more thorough cross-implementation evaluation.

</details>


### [302] [AutoSND: From Execution Evidence to Structural Policies for Automated Network Dismantling Heuristic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03653)
*Zhijing Hu,Changjun Fan,Yufan Deng,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSND，一个三阶段树搜索框架，用于自动设计完整的网络拆解程序。该框架通过阶段性地从简单启发式探索、编译候选记录为结构策略、再到基于策略继续搜索，最终获得质量优先和速度优先的程序AutoSND-Q/S，在12个真实网络和3个大型真实网络上展现出更好的搜索性能和稳定性，并发现了更具竞争力且结构可解释的网络拆解程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络拆解是分析复杂系统鲁棒性和脆弱性的基础，但实际的启发式方法需要在有效性和计算效率之间权衡，且通常依赖研究者手动设计。现有的基于大语言模型的自动启发式设计方法虽然能生成和筛选候选方案，但难以将候选质量或执行中的失败状态转化为结构层面的指导信息，限制了后续生成过程。

Method: 提出AutoSND三阶段树搜索框架：阶段I从简单启发式广泛探索并存档执行证据；阶段II将候选记录编译为关于局部信号、邻域访问和状态更新范围的结构策略；阶段III在这些策略指导下继续树搜索，最终获得质量优先和速度优先的候选程序AutoSND-Q/S。

Result: 在12个真实网络和3个大型真实网络上的实验表明，AutoSND取得了更好的搜索性能和稳定性，发现了更具竞争力且结构可解释的网络拆解程序。最终候选程序形成了可解释的结构：以残差度为主干，用有界局部信号调整节点顺序，并限制状态更新范围。

Conclusion: AutoSND通过三阶段树搜索框架有效解决了现有大语言模型自动启发式设计中缺乏结构级指导的问题，能够自动发现性能优越且结构可解释的网络拆解程序，为复杂系统鲁棒性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Network dismantling is fundamental to analyzing the robustness and vulnerability of complex systems, yet practical heuristics must balance effectiveness and computational efficiency, and are usually designed manually by researchers. Existing large language model based automatic heuristic design methods can generate and screen candidates, yet they have difficulty further transforming candidate quality or failure states during execution into structural-level guid- ance for subsequent generation. We propose AutoSND, a three stage tree search framework for complete network dismantling pro- grams. Stage I broadly explores from simple heuristics and archives execution evidence. Stage II compiles candidate records into struc- tural policies concerning local signals, neighborhood access, and state update ranges. Stage III continues tree search conditioned on these policies and obtains the final quality prioritized and speed prioritized candidates, AutoSND-Q/S. Experiments on 12 real world networks and 3 large real world networks show that AutoSND achieves better search performance and stability and discovers more competitive and structurally interpretable network disman- tling programs. The final candidates form an interpretable structure that uses residual degree as the backbone, adjusts node order with bounded local signals, and restricts the state update range. Code is available at https://github.com/MirrorNew/AutoSND.

</details>


### [303] [Taming the Implicit: Dual-Channel Risk-Aware Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Continual Multimodal Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03660)
*Yibei Liu,Jiajun Chen,Qianle Zhang,Tangyue Jin,Mengying Zhu,Meng Xi,Yangyang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPO（风险感知策略优化），这是首个用于多模态大语言模型持续后训练中显式风险治理的双通道框架。RAPO通过策略通道的风险感知策略缩放和数据通道的风险感知动态桶采样，在无需跨任务记忆的情况下即插即用地适配任何强化微调（RFT）算法，在MLLM-CL基准上将遗忘率相对降低79.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）被广泛认为在持续后训练中天然抵抗灾难性遗忘，但在显著的任务分布偏移下，代表性RFT算法的遗忘急剧恶化。作者指出其根源在于RFT固有的隐式奖励方差正则化无法抑制失控的优化风险，因此需要显式的风险治理机制。

Method: RAPO包含双通道设计：1）策略通道——风险感知策略缩放，通过回滚可靠性和Fisher启发的局部预测敏感性自适应校准每个样本的更新幅度；2）数据通道——风险感知动态桶采样，通过动态风险分层重组训练批次，引导优化朝向信息丰富且稳定的样本。该框架为即插即用策略，无需跨任务记忆即可适配任意RFT算法。

Result: 在公开的MLLM-CL基准上，RAPO相较于其RLOO骨干将最终遗忘率降低79.8%，同时保持新任务的竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作反驳了RFT天然抗遗忘的普遍认知，证明了在持续后训练中需要显式风险治理，RAPO作为通用插件在不增加跨任务存储开销的前提下显著缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) is widely believed to inherently resist catastrophic forgetting in continual post-training of multimodal large language models. Under pronounced task distributional shifts, however, forgetting across representative RFT algorithms escalates sharply. This stems from the implicit reward-variance regularization inherent to RFT, which proves incapable of suppressing uncontrolled optimization risk. We propose Risk-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO), the first dual-channel framework for explicit risk governance in continual RFT. On the policy channel, Risk-Aware Policy Scaling adaptively calibrates per-sample update magnitude via rollout reliability and Fisher-inspired local predictive sensitivity; on the data channel, Risk-Aware Dynamic Bucket Sampling reorganizes training batches through dynamic risk stratification, steering optimization toward informative yet stable samples. As a plug-and-play strategy requiring no cross-task memory, RAPO generalizes to any RFT algorithm without modification. On the public MLLM-CL benchmark, RAPO reduces final forgetting by 79.8% relative to its RLOO backbone while retaining new-task competitiveness.

</details>


### [304] [Shielding for Higher-Order Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03662)
*Filip Cano,Thomas A. Henzinger,Konstantin Kueffner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高阶平滑约束（如速度、加速度、加加速度限制）的安全盾（safety shield）合成方法。通过将微分安全属性用有限差分在离散状态空间上定义，将安全盾合成问题归约为普通安全博弈，并提出了一个迭代合成算法，该算法按导数约束的阶数递增求解，利用先前结果剪枝状态空间，从而提升合成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典安全盾基于状态谓词（当前物理状态安全/不安全）来合成，这在许多网络物理应用中过于粗糙。例如车辆避障不仅要避免碰撞，还要满足速度限制、加速度引起的力限制以及加加速度限制（防止伤害）。这些要求本质上是对状态导数的约束，经典方法无法直接处理这类高阶平滑性约束。

Method: 1) 在离散状态空间上使用有限差分定义微分安全属性，并刻画其表达能力；2) 将安全盾合成归约为在历史状态空间上的普通安全博弈；3) 给出合成算法，对k阶属性需要存储恰好k个过去状态，并证明该记忆量是必要的；4) 提出迭代合成过程，按导数约束阶数递增求解，每步用当前解剪枝状态空间，构建最大化容许的安全盾。

Result: 算法对k阶属性存储恰好k个历史状态（证明该记忆量必要），迭代合成过程按导数约束阶数递增处理，利用每轮结果剪枝状态空间，使安全盾合成在实践中更高效，避免探索已知不安全的大片状态空间区域。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了安全盾合成以支持高阶平滑性约束（基于状态导数），通过有限差分离散化和安全博弈归约实现了可计算的合成方法，并提出了高效的迭代剪枝策略，为网络物理系统中的高保真安全约束提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Safety shields are runtime enforcement mechanisms that restrict the actions of a controller to guarantee safety. Classical shields are usually synthesised for state predicates: the current physical state is either safe or unsafe, and the shield disables precisely those actions that can force the system into an unsafe state in the future. In many cyber-physical applications this view is too coarse. A vehicle approaching an obstacle should not only avoid collision, but also respect speed regulations, force limits induced by acceleration, and jerk limits to prevent injuries. From a physical perspective, these requirements are predicated over the derivatives of the state. This paper develops a finite-state safety-game construction for such high-order smoothness constraints. We define differential safety properties using finite differences over a discretised state space, characterise their expressiveness, and reduce shield synthesis to an ordinary safety game over a history state space. We give a synthesis algorithm whose shields store exactly $k$ past states for properties of order $k$ and prove that this memory is necessary. We describe an iterative synthesis procedure for a maximally permissive shield that operates over hierarchies of derivative constraints. The algorithm solves constraints iteratively in increasing order and uses the solution at each iteration to prune the state space for the next constraint. This makes shield synthesis more efficient in practice, as the algorithm refrains from exploring large regions of the state space that are known to be unsafe.

</details>


### [305] [PhyAI: Real-Time Physical AI at the Edge, Scalable Rollouts in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03682)
*Chenghua Wang,Daliang Xu,Dongqi Cai,Duojin Sun,Hao Zhang,Haoze Qian,Huaiyuan Zhang,Jinshuo Cui,Kezhao Zhao,Longxi Gao,Mengwei Xu,Rongjie Yi,Tianyue Zhang,Weikai Xie,Xiyuan Tan,Xuanzhe Liu,Yingying Qin,Yiwen Lu,Yuan Yao,Yuezhi Zu,Yunhan Guo,Ziqi Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PhyAI，一个统一的物理AI推理引擎，通过模型适配器机制在单一运行时上支持VLA模型和世界动作模型（WAMs），可在板载、边缘和云端多GPU部署，相比官方实现获得1.40x-4.65x加速，并引入控制时间Roofline来区分推理受限与环境受限的控制场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI策略在其全生命周期（模型评估、云强化学习、边缘GPU服务、板载部署）中都需要推理，但这些场景虽然共享相同的检查点和动作语义，却往往依赖分离的推理程序，导致代码碎片化、部署效率低下。作者旨在构建一个统一推理引擎来解决这一问题。

Method: PhyAI采用单一运行时的架构，将架构特定的条件化、求解器、缓存和输出逻辑保留在模型适配器中，同时共享图执行、内核、内存管理和并行服务。同一代码库可在板载、边缘和云端部署中运行VLA模型和WAMs，支持单GPU或多GPU，并通过适配器接口快速集成新模型（如发布当天即可集成MiniCPM-Robot）。

Result: PhyAI在pi0、pi0.5、GR00T N1.7和MiniCPM-Robot上比官方实现快1.40x-4.65x；在Cosmos3-Nano-Policy-DROID上，8块H20 GPU（CFG=2, TP=4）上延迟从2.46秒降至1.18秒（2.08x加速）。详细profile显示不同模型需要不同执行策略，并利用控制时间Roofline区分了推理受限与环境受限的控制行为。

Conclusion: PhyAI证明了单一推理引擎可以在不同物理AI部署场景中提供有竞争力的延迟（而非每种配置下最快）。虽然专用运行时在某些配置下仍更快，但统一运行时在代码共享和部署简化方面具有显著优势，其分析工具也为理解模型执行瓶颈提供了新视角。

Abstract: Physical AI policies require inference throughout their lifecycle, including model evaluation, cloud reinforcement learning rollout, edge GPU serving, and onboard deployment. Although these settings share the same checkpoint and action semantics, they often rely on separate inference programs. To unify them, we build PhyAI, a Physical AI inference engine with a single runtime that keeps architecture-specific conditioning, solver, cache, and output logic in model adapters while sharing graph execution, kernels, memory management, and parallel services. The same codebase runs vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs) on single or multiple GPUs across onboard, edge, and cloud deployments. We used the adapter interface to add MiniCPM-Robot on the day of its release. PhyAI achieves 1.40x-4.65x speedups over the official implementations of pi0, pi0.5, GR00T N1.7, and MiniCPM-Robot. On Cosmos3-Nano-Policy-DROID it reduces latency from 2.46 to 1.18 s on eight H20 GPUs (CFG=2, TP=4), a 2.08x speedup. Specialized runtimes remain faster in several configurations, so our goal is one runtime with competitive latency rather than the fastest result in every case. Detailed profiles reveal why different models need different execution policies: on a Hopper-series GPU at batch size one, the pi0.5 action expert accounts for 8.8% of FLOPs but 57.2% of latency; at batch size 32 its share drops to 13.5% and throughput reaches about 100 samples/s. Cosmos3 remains generation-dominated and gains only 14.3% throughput as batch size increases from 1 to 16. We further introduce the control-time Roofline, which distinguishes inference-bound from environment-bound control; the measured pi0.5 points on four LIBERO suites are environment-bound while Cosmos3 stays inference-bound. Code and benchmarks: https://github.com/mingti-org/phyai.

</details>


### [306] [LiveEvalBench: Toward Open-World Evaluation for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03689)
*Yiyao Wang,Zhen Wen,Yinghao Tang,Yixiao Fu,Lin Yuan,Xiaolau Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LiveEvalBench，一个将前端项目生成评估从静态评估重构为智能体化、自适应、可扩展的协作审查框架，通过三个角色（构建工程师、代码工程师、UI测试员）在全生命周期中收集证据，并与人类专家判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将网页生成视为静态评估问题，但前端工件本质上是交互式的、允许多样化且同样有效的实现方式，且迭代速度快于刚性流水线。需要一种新范式来解决这些差距。

Method: 将评估重构为协作审查工作流：Build Engineer、Code Engineer和UI Tester三个角色共同收集全生命周期证据（部署、代码检查、浏览器交互）。自适应协议结合共享评分标准（保证跨模型可比性）与基于实现的标准（适配每个工件）。框架支持增量集成新评估角色和评估维度。

Result: 在多样化的真实网页生成场景实验中，LiveEvalBench与人类专家判断高度一致，并能为前沿模型的网页生成能力提供细粒度洞察。

Conclusion: LiveEvalBench通过将网页生成评估转变为智能体化、自适应的协作审查过程，有效解决了传统静态基准的局限性，为前端生成模型提供了更准确和可扩展的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable of synthesizing executable frontend projects, yet existing benchmarks still treat web generation as a static evaluation problem. We argue that frontend artifacts demand a different paradigm: they are interactive rather than static, admit diverse yet equally valid implementations, and evolve faster than rigid pipelines can accommodate. To address these gaps, we present LiveEvalBench, an automated framework that reformulates web-generation evaluation as an agentic, adaptive, and extensible process. LiveEvalBench instantiates evaluation as a collaborative review workflow, in which a Build Engineer, a Code Engineer, and a UI Tester collectively gather evidence across the full lifecycle of a frontend project, from deployment and code inspection to browser-based interaction. To handle implementation diversity, an adaptive protocol couples shared rubrics for cross-model comparability with implementation-grounded criteria tailored to each artifact. The framework further supports incremental integration of new evaluator roles and assessment dimensions without pipeline redesign. Experiments across diverse real-world web-generation scenarios show that LiveEvalBench aligns closely with human expert judgment and provides fine-grained insights into frontier models' web generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wyysteelhead/LiveEvalBench

</details>


### [307] [TARL: Transaction-Aware Reliable Ledgers for Executable Memory Management in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03699)
*Han Xiao,Hongjun Xu,Xin Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TARL，一种将记忆更新从简单二值判断（写/不写）细化为五种可执行操作（添加、忽略、修订、拒绝、延迟验证）的持久记忆状态更新框架，并配套构建了TARL-Mem基准数据集，在多种评测场景下显著提升了动作预测准确率和记忆状态恢复能力，减少了记忆污染与累积性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久记忆系统将记忆更新简化为二值的写入/保持决策，无法区分新信息应被添加、忽略、修订旧信念、拒绝还是延迟验证等本质不同的操作。这些不同选择可能共享相同的二值标签，却产生根本不同的记忆状态，导致单次更新错误会在后续检索和推理中反复放大。

Method: TARL框架将每条陈述映射到五种可执行动作之一，通过识别受影响的记忆、解析时间范围、比较来源可靠性，并更新接受（accepted）、待定（pending）和拒绝（rejected）三个账本。训练阶段通过对比不同更新操作产生的记忆状态，引导模型选择能导向正确结果的操作。同时提出了TARL-Mem基准，包含细粒度动作标签和下一状态目标。

Result: 在域内、跨来源、时间性、反事实和序列化等五类评测中，TARL在动作预测和状态恢复上均取得提升，减少了记忆污染，保留了冲突性证据，并限制了累积性错误传播。

Conclusion: TARL通过细粒度的记忆状态更新框架有效解决了持久记忆系统中更新操作粒度不足的问题，为长期记忆智能体的可靠记忆维护提供了新的方法论和评测基准。

Abstract: Persistent memory helps long-term agents retain knowledge, yet a single update error can repeatedly distort future retrieval and reasoning. Most existing systems reduce memory updating to a binary Write/Hold decision, which cannot distinguish whether new information should be added, ignored, used to revise an outdated belief, rejected as unreliable, or deferred for verification. These choices may share the same binary label while producing fundamentally different memory states. We introduce TARL, a memory state update framework that maps each statement to one of five executable actions. TARL identifies the affected memory, resolves its temporal scope, compares source reliability, and updates accepted, pending, and rejected ledgers.
