It is further trained by comparing the memory states produced by alternative update operations, encouraging the model to select the operation that leads to the correct result. We also introduce TARL-Mem, a benchmark with fine-grained action labels and next-state targets. Across in-domain, cross-source, temporal, counterfactual, and sequential evaluations, TARL improves action prediction and state recovery, reduces memory pollution, preserves conflicting evidence, and limits cumulative corruption. The complete model implementation is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [308] [Less Traffic, Better Outcomes: Competition-Aware Request Dispatch in Real-Time Ad Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03705)
*Jonaid Shianifar,Blaz Mramor,Fangda Zou,Matthieu C. Martin,Xingsheng Guo,Zhihua Zhu,Rong Zhou,Bichen Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种竞争感知的请求分发框架，通过分布式出价预测和概率转发来决定是否将RTB广告请求发送给每个DSP，在减少34.2%请求量的同时使净收入提升4.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTB广告交易平台将几乎所有请求转发给DSP，但实际产生出价的仅占少数。这种过度分发导致DSP在计算和预算约束下节流参与，降低了有限竞价能力的有效利用，损害了拍卖效果。

Method: 提出一个竞争感知的请求分发框架，包含两核心组件：(1) 分布式出价预测——预测每个DSP对请求的出价分布；(2) 概率转发机制——基于预测结果决定请求是否发送给每个DSP。系统通过轻量级策略优化随时间自适应调整每个DSP的阈值，以适应非平稳的市场条件。研究在日请求量超200亿的生产平台上开展了四个连续的在线实验验证。

Result: 在完整的多DSP部署中，策略将DSP请求量减少34.2%，同时在14天窗口内净收入提升4.6%（p<0.001）。进一步分析揭示了不同流量段之间存在显著的异质性，聚合指标可能具有误导性。分段和每DSP分析表明策略能够展现DSP之间的比较优势，在不增加总请求量的情况下改善货币化效果。

Conclusion: 竞争感知的请求分发能够通过减少无效请求转发来提升RTB生态系统的整体效率，并且段级分析比聚合指标更能揭示策略的真实效果，为广告系统中的智能分发决策提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Real-time bidding (RTB) ad exchanges typically forward nearly all incoming requests to demand-side platforms (DSPs), even though only a small fraction receive bids. This over-distribution weakens auction outcomes: DSPs throttle participation under compute and budget constraints, reducing the effective use of limited bidding capacity. We present a competition-aware request dispatch framework that uses distributional bid prediction and probabilistic forwarding to decide whether each request should be sent to each DSP. The system adapts per-DSP thresholds over time through lightweight policy optimization to track non-stationary market conditions. We evaluate the framework through four sequential online experiments on a production platform serving over 20 billion daily requests. A full multi-DSP deployment reduces DSP request volume under the policy by 34.2% while increasing net revenue by 4.6% (p<0.001) in a recent 14-day window after an initial DSP adaptation period. Further analysis highlights strong heterogeneity across traffic segments and reveals that aggregate metrics can be misleading. Segment-level and per-DSP analyses suggest that the policy surfaces comparative advantages among DSPs, improving monetized outcomes without increasing overall request volume.

</details>


### [309] [When Outputs Disperse, Does Epistemic Revision Follow? A Black-Box Coupling Diagnostic for Machine Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03722)
*Molood Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"弥散-修订耦合"诊断框架，用于检验LLM集体中输出多样性是否伴随真正的认知立场修订，而非仅表面重构。通过Coherence Index（CI）与Meta-Predictive Clarity System（MPCS）两通道测量，发现gpt-4o-mini在条件性异议下假前提恢复率提升+17.7点，而gemini-2.5-flash同样干预无效果（26.1% vs 27.1%），揭示其差异源于"框架内异议"——Gemini倾向于重构而非让步（94% vs 24%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体智慧研究默认分歧即认知多样性，但LLM集体中模型可能产出看似多样实则结论不变的输出。现有代理指标（输出多样性）无法区分真正的立场修订与前提保持性重构，需要黑盒诊断方法验证"干预增加输出弥散"是否伴随"真实认知修订"。

Method: 提出双通道黑盒诊断框架：输出通道用Coherence Index（CI）验证干预是否改变输出弥散；认知通道通过逐轮立场标注测量集体是否真正修订立场。结合Meta-Predictive Clarity System（MPCS），在输出过度收敛时插入Re-Differentiation Protocol（RDP）。评估使用两种模型配置的五agent集体，共310对情节/条件。

Result: gpt-4o-mini上条件性异议使假前提恢复率提升+17.7点（p<1e-6），静态人格多样性反而损害恢复（-8.1，p=.007）；gemini-2.5-flash上同样干预无显著增益（26.1% vs 27.1%，p=.84）且两处理效应差异显著（z=3.79，p<.001）。机制标注显示Gemini通过框架内异议保留假前提，94%的RDP后响应为重构而非让步（GPT仅24%）。

Conclusion: 输出多样性不能作为LLM集体认知修订的可靠代理指标；不同模型对同一弥散干预的响应差异巨大。建议在报告准确性之外，同时报告逐干预立场转变率和前提保持率，以全面评估集体推理质量。

Abstract: Collective intelligence research treats disagreement as evidence of epistemic diversity: if agents express different views, the group should retain capacity to revise. In LLM collectives this proxy can break: agents can produce diverse-looking arguments while preserving the same conclusion. We operationalize dispersion-revision coupling: the degree to which an intervention that verifiably increases the dispersion of a collective's outputs in embedding space is accompanied by genuine revision of its epistemic stance rather than premise-preserving reformulation. The diagnostic is black-box: it operates on generated text alone and makes no claims about the internal representations of the generating models. Two channels are measured independently: an output channel, the Coherence Index (CI), verifies that the intervention changed output dispersion; an epistemic channel, per-turn stance annotation, measures whether the collective revised. We propose CI with the Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS), which inserts a Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP) when outputs over-converge, as a reusable method for estimating this coupling regime. We evaluate five-agent collectives from two configurations (gpt-4o-mini and gemini-2.5-flash; 310 paired episodes per condition). On gpt-4o-mini, conditional dissent improves false-premise recovery by +17.7 points (p<1e-6) while static persona diversity harms recovery (-8.1, p=.007). On gemini-2.5-flash, the same intervention at a comparable budget yields no gain (26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84) despite a verified dispersion drop; the two treatment effects differ from each other (z=3.79, p<.001). Mechanism tagging shows Gemini preserves the false premise via intra-framework dissent: 94% of tagged post-RDP responses reformulate rather than concede (vs 24% on GPT). We recommend reporting per-intervention stance shift and premise-preservation rate alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [310] [SAT-Edge-Agent: Hardware-in-the-Loop Edge-Agent Orchestration for Onboard Satellite Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03728)
*Longji He,Jeto Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了SAT-Edge-Agent，一个部署在商用ARM异构边缘SoC上的星载边缘智能代理系统，通过浏览器工作区与FastAPI协调本地语言服务和YOLO风格定向目标检测端点，在20/20次测试中实现单图像平均29.353秒、双图像60.937秒的端到端延迟，检测器仅占总体延迟的约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载卫星智能需要一个任务层，将任务意图转化为本地工具调用、暴露执行状态，并在通信和功耗约束下返回机器可消费的结果，但目前缺乏可复现的硬件在环（HIL）边界验证系统。

Method: 采用商用ARM异构边缘SoC搭建硬件在环测试平台，以浏览器工作区+FastAPI代理协调本地OpenAI兼容语言服务与YOLO风格定向目标检测端点（返回FAIR1M元数据支撑的结构化结果），设计单图像和连续双图像两类固定工作负载各重复20次进行性能测试和剖析。

Result: 两类工作负载均完成20/20次，平均全代理延迟为29.353秒和60.937秒，P95为31.166秒和66.882秒；平均检测器时间861.386ms和1510.920ms，仅占全代理均值的2.93%和2.48%；平均CPU利用率约20.5-20.8%；NPU负载字段虽显示100%但仅代表共享加速器软件域值。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了可复现的卫星边缘代理编排的硬件在环基准边界，但不声称检测精度、新地理定位方法、校准能效或飞行就绪性；公开证据包提供脱敏记录和复现脚本以支持结果验证。

Abstract: Onboard satellite intelligence requires a task layer that translates mission intent into local tool calls, exposes execution state, and returns machine-consumable artifacts under communication and power constraints. We present SAT-Edge-Agent, a hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) edge-agent system deployed on a commercial off-the-shelf ARM-based heterogeneous edge system-on-chip. A browser workspace and FastAPI agent coordinate a local OpenAI-compatible language service with a project-internal YOLO-style oriented-object-detection endpoint that returns FAIR1M metadata-backed structured results. Two fixed FAIR1M workloads, one single-image and one serial two-image request, were repeated 20 times each and completed 20/20 attempts. Mean Full-Agent latency was 29.353 s and 60.937 s, with empirical P95 values of 31.166 s and 66.882 s. Mean detector time was 861.386 ms and 1510.920 ms, only 2.93% and 2.48% of the corresponding Full-Agent means. Profiling indicates that most visible latency occurs outside detector execution. Mean CPU utilization was 20.761% and 20.482%. A 200-ms NPU-load field averaged 100% for both workloads, but it represents a shared-accelerator software field rather than detector-only occupancy or calibrated utilization. The public evidence package provides sanitized request-level records, redacted JSON, normalized SSE examples, and scripts reproducing the reported statistics. These results establish a reproducible HIL boundary for observable satellite edge-agent orchestration, but do not establish detector accuracy, a new geolocation method, calibrated energy efficiency, or flight readiness.

</details>


### [311] [CARE-Bench: Benchmarking Patient-Facing LLM Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03731)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARE-Bench，一个基于来源的真实患者分诊基准测试，用于评估患者面向医疗LLM和代理的逐轮当前动作分类性能。该基准包含500个案例、1059个患者披露前缀，并对11个模型在269轮保留测试数据上进行评估。结果显示未提示时宏F1仅为31.2-50.4，提示后虽有提升（46.9-63.4），但仍存在大量阈值错误，特别是在需要询问更多信息时仅33.5%的输出保持了该步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者面向的医疗LLM和代理越来越多地在临床接触前回答症状问题，关键安全问题是用户下一步应采取什么行动。现有评估缺乏对顺序性患者分诊中"当前动作"的明确基准，需要一种来源可靠的基准来评估此类系统的安全性和时序决策能力。

Method: 构建CARE-Bench基准：从医疗对话、咨询和随访提问来源重建500个案例和1059个患者披露前缀，定义为四标签逐轮当前动作分类任务。对11个模型在269轮保留测试数据上采用未提示和最小提示的开放式协议进行评估，使用固定的GPT-5.5映射器将响应编码到四标签动作空间。

Result: 未提示宏F1较低（31.2-50.4）；提示改善了11个模型中的10个，宏F1范围为46.9-63.4，但仍存在大量阈值错误。当正确动作是询问更多信息时，仅有33.5%的提示输出保留了该步骤，表明模型常在获得必要的澄清信息之前就推荐了护理措施。

Conclusion: 患者面向的分诊不是一个简单的提示工程技术问题。即使在提示后错误仍持续存在，支持在部署前对动作时序进行明确评估，以确保患者安全。

Abstract: Patient-facing medical LLMs and agents increasingly answer symptom questions before clinician contact, where the key safety question is what action the user should take next. We introduce CARE-Bench, a source-grounded benchmark that evaluates sequential patient-facing triage as a four-label per-turn current-action task. CARE-Bench contains 500 cases and 1,059 evaluated patient-disclosure prefixes reconstructed from medical dialogue, consultation, and follow-up-question sources. We evaluate 11 models on 269 held-out rounds under unprompted and minimally prompted open-ended protocols, using a fixed GPT-5.5 mapper to code each response into the four-label action space. Unprompted macro-F1 remains low, ranging from 31.2 to 50.4. Prompting improves 10 of 11 models, with prompted macro-F1 ranging from 46.9 to 63.4, but substantial threshold errors remain. Prompted models often recommend care before needed clarification is obtained; when the correct action was to ask for more information, only 33.5% of prompted outputs preserved the step. The persistence of these errors after prompting suggests that patient-facing triage is not a simple prompting problem and supports explicit evaluation of action timing before deployment.

</details>


### [312] [Failure-Informed Image Self-Augmentation for Multimodal Large Language Model Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03733)
*Chunyang Jiang,Pingping Zhang,Yuzhi Zhao,Wenao Ma,Zhijian Hou,Mengyang Wu,Yiyang Cai,Senkang Hu,Sitong Cheng,Chi-Min Chan,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FISA框架，通过从模型自身失败案例中构建增强图像，实现多模态大语言模型的自我改进，无需外部监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型依赖大规模高质量标注数据，成本高昂；现有自增强方法多为文本中心，图像增强未充分利用模型的实际缺陷。

Method: 从模型失败案例中生成视觉上具有挑战性但保持答案一致的图像复杂度，通过自我检查验证其效用，并应用双重保真度过滤以避免语义扭曲。

Result: 在视觉问答基准上，该方法在分布内和分布外设定下均持续提升性能；与文本自增强方法兼容，合成样本的数据效率优于通用图像增强基线。

Conclusion: FISA有效利用模型自身失败信息进行图像自增强，是一种低成本、高性能的MLLM自我改进方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across vision-language tasks, but their progress depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality multimodal data that are costly to annotate. Self-augmentation offers a promising alternative by enabling models to expand their own training data without external supervision. However, existing MLLM self-augmentation methods are largely text-centric, while image augmentation remains underexplored and typically relies on generic or handcrafted transformations that are weakly aligned with the model's actual incapability. We propose Failure-informed Image Self-Augmentation (\textbf{FISA}), a framework for MLLM self-improvement that constructs augmented images from the model's own failure cases. Our method generates visually challenging yet answer-preserving image complications, verifies their utility through self-examination, and applies dual fidelity filtering to avoid semantic distortion. Experiments on visual question answering benchmarks show that the proposed method consistently improves performance across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Further experiments validate the compatibility of FISA with existing textual self-augmentation approaches, the superior data efficiency of the synthesized samples over generic image augmentation baselines, and the practical effectiveness of the proposed filtering strategy.

</details>


### [313] [AgenticECO: An Agentic Framework for ECO on 3D Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03738)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Libo Shen,Zhiqiang Jia,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticECO，一种基于证据门控的工具使用代理工作流，用于3D-IC工程设计变更（ECO），配套EcoRoute最小扰动路由层，使修复可归因于编辑操作。在9个匹配的自然缺陷案例中，AgenticECO在相同预算下清除7/9个案例（对照组仅2/9），平均扰动仅0.66%，且经过三项预注册视觉研究和盲诊断验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在后摩尔时代3D集成趋势下，传统的后布线ECO依赖人工专家经验；且标准的"编辑后完全重布线"实践会将修复与路由器抖动混淆，导致签核指标无法归因于引发该指标的编辑操作。

Method: 1) AgenticECO：基于证据门控的工具使用代理工作流，运行于开源TaiWei流程；2) EcoRoute：最小扰动ECO布线层，驱动未修改的钉扎路由器，使修复可归因于编辑；3) 配套三项预注册视觉研究和一项预注册盲诊断实验进行验证。

Result: 在9个匹配自然缺陷案例、相同预算下，AgenticECO清除7个vs对照组2个（全重布线和存量修复均仅2个），平均扰动0.66%，时钟网零触及；跨骨干网重跑清除全部9个。受控研究表明：保留条件下修复移动是必要的，占用感知选择带来合法落点而非修复成功，收紧时钟时最小扰动翻转接受/拒绝结果。盲诊断精确恢复所有预留注入缺陷，是唯一零错误编辑的组别。

Conclusion: AgenticECO结合EcoRoute实现了3D-IC ECO中编辑与修复效果的可归因性，在扰动最小化、时钟网络保护及缺陷修复成功率上均显著优于现有方法，所有接受的结果均通过布线、重新提取、max/min时序、DRC和结构等价门控验证。

Abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [314] [MissClick: Exploiting Digit-Serialized Coordinates to Attack GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03740)
*Yu Ran,Wentao Zhao,Xin Zhang,Yi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MissClick，一种针对GUI视觉基础模型坐标生成过程的白盒对抗攻击方法。该方法利用坐标数字token的数值和位值结构特性，设计了两种目标特定的攻击目标：MissClick-U用于非定向破坏（最大化软坐标位移），MissClick-T用于定向劫持（最小化位加权目标数字损失）。在OS-Atlas和UGround模型上，跨桌面、网页和移动平台，非定向攻击成功率最高达75.07%，定向攻击成功率最高达62.67%，显著优于现有攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉基础模型将屏幕坐标生成为数字token序列，但其坐标生成过程的安全隐患被忽视。由于坐标数字是分类token，改变百位数字一位会导致坐标组件变化100个单位，从而造成执行点击的大幅位移。此外，非定向和定向攻击具有不同的成功条件，需要不同的攻击目标，因此需要针对性和结构感知的攻击方法。

Method: 提出MissClick白盒对抗攻击框架，包含两个目标特定的目标函数：MissClick-U最大化软坐标位移以实现非定向破坏，MissClick-T最小化位加权目标数字损失以实现定向劫持。该方法利用坐标输出的数值和位值结构特性，而非将其视为普通文本处理。

Result: 在OS-Atlas和UGround模型上的桌面、网页和移动平台实验中，MissClick-U非定向攻击成功率分别达到75.07%和72.93%（较基线提升16.62和30.72个百分点），MissClick-T定向攻击成功率分别达到44.86%和62.67%（较基线提升31.73和47.06个百分点）。目标对比实验显示软坐标位移最适合非定向攻击，位加权目标数字优化最适合定向攻击。

Conclusion: 攻击目标对比揭示非定向和定向攻击各自的最优目标函数选择：软坐标位移最高非定向攻击成功率，位加权目标数字优化最高定向攻击成功率，表明两种攻击目标需要不同的优化策略，验证了MissClick目标感知设计方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent GUI visual grounding models generate screen coordinates as sequences of digit tokens that are parsed into numerical values and mapped to executable clicks. The security implications of this coordinate generation process have been largely overlooked. We observe that each coordinate digit is predicted as a categorical token, yet after parsing, changing a hundreds-place digit by one changes the corresponding numerical coordinate component by 100 units, which can induce a large displacement of the executed click. This observation motivates attack objectives that account for the numerical and place-value structure of coordinate outputs rather than treating them as ordinary text. Moreover, untargeted and targeted attacks impose different success conditions--displacing the click outside the correct region versus into an attacker-specified region--and therefore benefit from different objectives. We propose MissClick, a simple and effective white-box adversarial attack with two goal-specific objectives: MissClick-U maximizes soft-coordinate displacement for untargeted disruption, while MissClick-T minimizes a place-weighted target-digit loss for targeted hijacking. Compared with existing attacks against GUI grounding models on OS-Atlas and UGround across desktop, web, and mobile platforms, MissClick-U achieves untargeted success rates of 75.07\% and 72.93\% (+16.62 and +30.72 pp), and MissClick-T achieves targeted success rates of 44.86\% and 62.67\% (+31.73 and +47.06 pp). Attack objective comparison further shows that soft-coordinate displacement yields the highest untargeted attack success rate, whereas place-weighted target-digit optimization yields the highest targeted attack success rate, revealing distinct objective preferences for the two attack goals.

</details>


### [315] [Agents Catching Agents: Shortcut Cascades and Benchmark Gaming in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03744)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Agastya Munnangi,Aldo Marzullo,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Quang Bui,Mohammad Shahin,Armaan Grewal,Emmanuel Paul Kwesiga,Anqi Peter Li,Josephine Nanyonjo,Aaditya Panchal,Arshnoor Bhutani,Nikhil Jaiswal,Milit S. Patel,Maximin Lange,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨基于语言模型智能体委员会（committee）的临床决策支持系统是否会被"捷径"（shortcuts）所操纵。结果表明，社会性合理但错误的信号（如两个同行声称同一错误答案，或虚假的预筛查系统标志）会导致智能体在38%的情况下采用错误答案。门控（gate）监督无法区分虚假采纳与诚实同意（假阳性率100%），而同源评审（judge）在文本上有效但在影像上失效，只有独立于自我报告的裁判（referee）能跨模态有效检测（77-88%精确率，13-21%假阳性率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持正朝着多语言模型智能体在共享工作空间中进行委员会式协商的方向发展。研究团队想探究这种委员会是否会被基准测试奖励但临床医生会忽略的"捷径"（shortcuts）所操纵，即委员会是否容易被社会性的错误共识所"带偏"。

Method: 在六个公共数据集上的七个队列中进行实验，涵盖文本（MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR报告）、影像（NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert）和表格ICU记录（SUPPORT2）。测试了三种操纵形式：同行断言、虚假预筛查标志、隐藏评分标准；并评估了三种监督机制（门控gate、同行-lineage评审judge、独立裁判referee）的检测能力。

Result: Gemini委员会在孤立呈现捷径时具有抵抗力（仅翻转5-16%），但当两个同行断言同一错误答案时，被测试的智能体在38%情况下会采纳它。门控监督假阳性率100%无法区分采纳与诚实同意；同源评审在文本上达到100%精确率/93%召回率但在影像上失效；独立裁判在影像上达到77-88%精确率、13-21%假阳性率。增加视觉显著性不影响传播，但增加第二个同行的声音将传播率提高50%。隐藏评分标准几乎不被人察觉（文本1/10、影像1/134的漂移者能指出。

Conclusion: 委员会智能体的最大弱点是社会性合理性的信息（socially plausible shortcuts），而非明显的"作弊"。要有效检测这种操纵，需要独立于智能体自我报告的裁判机制（referee），而门控和同源评审均存在显著局限性。

Abstract: Clinical decision support is moving toward committees of language-model agents deliberating on a shared workspace. We ask whether such committees can be gamed by shortcuts, cues a benchmark rewards but a clinician would ignore. Across seven cohorts on six public datasets spanning text (MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR reports), imaging (NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert) and tabular ICU records (SUPPORT2), Gemini committees resist these cues in isolation (flip 5-16%), yet a socially plausible shortcut spreads: when two peers assert the same wrong answer, the holdout under test adopts it in 38% of cases, as does a false "pre-screen" system flag, on both capability tiers. Of three oversight agents, a gate cannot separate adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a same-lineage judge reading only the transcript flags adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but collapses onto the gate in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout transfers to imaging (77-88% precision, 13-21% false-positive rate). Tripling a cue's visual salience does not move contagion, whereas a second peer voice raises it by half again. Gaming a hidden rubric is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters name the rubric they moved toward. What games a committee is social plausibility, and only a referee independent of self-report catches it. Code: https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

</details>


### [316] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型推理模型（LRMs）在"推理忠实度"与"不安全推理拒绝能力"之间存在对齐张力，提出了一种名为TRR的新型推理替换技术来测试这一张力，并证明可以通过表示引导（representation steering）来独立放大安全方向，在不损害基础能力的前提下提升安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理为模型监控提供了窗口，但监控依赖于推理忠实度（即输出严格源自推理轨迹）。然而，模型既需要足够忠实以便被监控，又需要足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全推理，这两者之间存在潜在的矛盾张力。当前LRMs是否真的存在这种平衡问题，以及如何解决，是本文关注的核心。

Method: 1) 提出HazMart数据集，设置在自主AI店主的场景中，由人工编写。2) 提出Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR)技术，不同于以往在提示中提供提示线索的方法，TRR直接干预推理链，替换为不安全或不合逻辑的思维。3) 对DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B和QwQ-32B进行忠实度和安全性评估。4) 对QwQ-32B进行机制分析，识别与两个属性相关的反相关内部方向。5) 应用表示引导技术放大安全方向。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B表现出高忠实度（97.5%）但拒绝不安全推理的能力很差（12.3%）；而QwQ-32B更鲁棒（73.9%安全性）但忠实度较低（74.7%）。机制分析显示，QwQ-32B中这两个属性由在动作承诺token处达到峰值的反相关内部方向表征。通过表示引导放大安全方向，可将安全行为提升9个百分点，同时保持基础能力。

Conclusion: 当前LRMs确实存在忠实度与安全性之间的对齐张力，且不同模型在这两个维度上表现出不同的权衡。通过机制层面的理解和表示引导干预，可以独立放大安全方向而无需牺牲模型的基础能力，这为更好地平衡模型的可监控性与安全性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [317] [GDPevo: Evaluating Agent Self-Evolution on Real Business Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03764)
*Leijun Zhou,Zhihao Liu,Xiang Qu,Chenxu Liu,Yifei Liu,Yanke Yu,Jingzhe Xu,Xuejun Wu,Buyue Qian,Xi Chen,Yaowei Zheng,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GDPevo，一个基于GDP相关企业工作流的智能体自我进化评估基准，通过规则杂交机制确保测试性能提升可归因于训练经验，并配套全自动化数据流水线以应对数据污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化评估基准存在局限：任务领域覆盖有限（缺乏经济价值高的领域）、训练与测试任务设计不足以归因测试提升、易受数据污染影响。

Method: 提出规则杂交机制：将企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，在训练任务中分配规则子集，并在留出测试任务中重新组合这些规则，从而确保测试性能提升可归因于训练经验。构建GDPevo基准，涵盖CRM、ERP、金融、医疗、法律和数据中心化工作流，并配套全自动化数据流水线。

Result: GDPevo V1包含12组120个任务（每组5个训练+5个测试任务），自动化流水线可在两天内扩展至V2的24组240个任务。评估四种智能体在四种监督类型下的表现，自我进化持续提升留出测试准确率，最高提升16.44个百分点，但最佳进化智能体仍远低于全知Oracle的91.6%上限。

Conclusion: 自我进化能力能显著提升智能体在GDP相关企业工作流上的表现，但当前智能体的自我进化能力远未完全实现，仍有巨大的提升空间。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution updates an agent's persistent state from prior experience and reuses it to solve related tasks more effectively. Evaluating self-evolution is difficult: existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of economically valuable task domains, do not always design training and test tasks such that test-time gains can be attributed to training experience, and remain vulnerable to data contamination. We present GDPevo, an evolution-native benchmark grounded in GDP-related enterprise workflows, together with the fully automated data pipeline that generates it. Its core mechanism, rule hybridization, decomposes each enterprise workflow into atomic business rules, distributes subsets of these rules across training tasks, and recombines them in held-out test tasks so that test-time gains are attributable. GDPevo spans CRM, ERP, finance, healthcare, legal, and data-centric workflows. Its V1 release contains 120 tasks in 12 groups, with five training and five held-out test tasks per group. Full automation enables the pipeline to expand the suite to 240 tasks in 24 groups (V2) within two days, providing a practical response to contamination. Using GDPevo, we evaluate four agents, each comprising a harness and a model, under four supervision types. Self-evolution consistently improves held-out accuracy by up to 16.44 percentage points. But the best evolved agents remain far below the fully informed oracle ceiling of 91.6%, indicating that the self-evolution ability of current agents remains far from fully realized. We publicly release the pipeline, benchmark, and full evaluation results at https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo.

</details>


### [318] [Computing Actual Causes for Neural Network Predictions under Structured Causal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03772)
*Jannick Strobel,Muqsit Azeem,Stefan Leue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Halpern-Pearl（HP）实际因果关系，结合布尔结构因果模型（SCM）对神经网络预测进行解释，并通过边界传播和分支定界技术高效计算所有最小实际原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释方法（如特征归因或最小充分集）通常假设输入特征独立，在输入存在结构化依赖关系时会产生误导性解释。因此需要能够显式建模输入依赖关系的可解释性方法。

Method: 将解释形式化为HP实际原因，使用布尔SCM建模输入依赖；采用边界传播和分支定界技术计算HP原因，并提供完备性和最小性的形式化保证。

Result: 在可扩展性上显著优于暴力搜索和ILP基线；在搜索空间高达2.3×10^13个候选（原因、偶然性）对、最多28个节点的SCM实例上（180秒预算内）表现优于启发式搜索。案例研究表明忽略输入依赖会虚增14.9%的虚假原因。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够高效、完整且最小地计算基于HP实际原因的解释，同时建模输入依赖关系以避免产生虚假解释。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural networks is a central challenge in trustworthy AI. Existing explanation methods, such as those based on feature attribution or minimal sufficient sets, typically treat input features as independent, which can yield misleading explanations when inputs exhibit structured dependencies. We address this by formalizing explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes, modeling input dependencies using Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We compute HP causes by applying bound propagation and branch-and-bound techniques, while providing formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Our experiments show that we substantially outperform brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, and outperform heuristic search as graph size grows, computing all minimal actual causes on instances with search spaces of up to $2.3\times10^{13}$ candidate (cause, contingency) pairs, on SCMs with up to 28 nodes, within a 180s per-instance budget. In a case study, we further show that ignoring input dependencies inflates the number of reported causes, 14.9% of which are spurious under our SCM.

</details>


### [319] [KnowHal: A Knowledge-Driven Benchmark for Comprehensive Multimodal Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03782)
*Ruihan Li,Jiyang Tan,Kailin Jiang,Huining Li,Hengyang Lu,Yu Huang,Qian Li,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KnowHal基准，将知识幻觉纳入多模态大语言模型（MLLM）幻觉评估的统一框架，涵盖实体、属性、关系和知识四个维度，通过1,800个配对正负问题样本评测了14个代表性MLLM，发现知识维度是最大挑战，且多数模型对虚假前提的鲁棒性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM幻觉基准主要关注实体、属性和关系幻觉，知识相关的失败往往被单独研究，缺乏一个能够统一评估不同幻觉维度的框架，因此需要构建涵盖知识幻觉的综合评估基准。

Method: 构建KnowHal基准，包含四个维度（实体、属性、关系、知识），采用配对正负问题设计（共享图像和实体），通过半自动化流程构建（结合LLM辅助、CLIP过滤和人工验证），共1,800个样本覆盖10个领域和50个类别，并评估14个代表性MLLM。

Result: 知识维度对所有评估的模型几乎都是最大的挑战；大多数模型在负向问题上表现显著下降，显示出对虚假前提的鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: KnowHal通过统一四个幻觉维度并采用配对问题设计，填补了现有评估框架的重要空白，能够更全面地评估MLLM中的幻觉问题。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a critical challenge for developing trustworthy Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing benchmarks mainly focus on entity, attribute, and relation hallucinations, knowledge-related failures are often investigated separately, lacking a unified evaluation framework across different hallucination dimensions. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{KnowHal}, a benchmark that explicitly incorporates knowledge hallucination into multimodal hallucination evaluation spanning four dimensions: entity, attribute, relation, and knowledge. KnowHal constructs paired positive and negative questions over shared images and entities, enabling controlled comparisons among perceptual errors, knowledge-related errors, and false-premise acceptance. The benchmark contains 1,800 samples across 10 domains and 50 categories, constructed through a semi-automated pipeline combining LLM assistance, CLIP-based filtering, and human verification. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs on KnowHal and conduct extensive analyses. Results show that the knowledge dimension consistently presents the greatest challenge for nearly all evaluated models, while most models exhibit substantial performance degradation on negative questions, revealing limited robustness to false premises. By unifying four hallucination dimensions with paired question design, KnowHal addresses an important gap in existing evaluation frameworks and enables a more comprehensive assessment of hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [320] [Does Forgetting Transfer Across Modalities? A Real-World Benchmark for Cross-Modal Knowledge Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03791)
*Chunlin Liu,Junnian Chen,Haitong Jiang,Jianyu Zhao,Yingsen Pang,Jingchen Li,Jiabiao He,Youming Lu,Jinhe Bi,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNLINK-VL，一个用于视觉-语言模型（VLM）跨模态知识遗忘的真实世界基准，包含四个子集分别评估目标知识的直接遗忘、关系知识的传播、非目标知识的保留和语义等价查询的鲁棒性。实验发现跨模态转移存在显著不对称性：多模态遗忘在文本评估下有效，而纯文本遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识遗忘研究主要关注单一模态内的遗忘，跨模态知识遗忘的真实世界转移尚未被充分研究，导致仅通过单一模态（尤其是文本）评估可能高估遗忘效果。

Method: 构建UNLINK-VL基准，从Wikidata选取可视觉识别的真实世界实体作为遗忘目标，关联图像及一跳和多跳事实。在事后遗忘设置下（原始遗忘和保留语料不可用），分别训练文本-only和多模态遗忘模型，并在文本、视觉和跨模态场景中评估遗忘效果与保留能力。

Result: 实验揭示了跨模态转移的显著不对称性：多模态遗忘在文本评估下仍然有效，而文本-only遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景；同时，被评估的方法大多保留了模型的一般能力。

Conclusion: 仅依赖模态内评估（特别是文本-only评估）可能大幅高估VLM中知识遗忘的有效性，凸显了跨模态遗忘和跨模态评估的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), like Large Language Models (LLMs), may memorize sensitive, copyrighted, or harmful knowledge from their pretraining corpora. Removing such knowledge is essential for building trustworthy AI systems. However, existing studies primarily focus on forgetting within individual modalities. Although recent work has begun to explore cross-modal consistency in unlearning, the cross-modal transfer of real-world knowledge unlearning remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce UNLINK-VL, a real-world benchmark for cross-modal knowledge unlearning in VLMs. Under a post-hoc unlearning setting in which the original forget and retain corpora are unavailable, UNLINK-VL selects visually identifiable real-world entities as unlearning targets and associates them with corresponding images and one-hop and multi-hop facts derived from Wikidata. The benchmark comprises four complementary subsets that evaluate direct forgetting of target knowledge, the propagation of forgetting through relational knowledge, the preservation of related non-target knowledge, and robustness to semantically equivalent queries. We train models under text-only and multimodal unlearning settings and evaluate forgetting effectiveness and retained utility across textual, visual, and cross-modal scenarios. Extensive experiments reveal a pronounced asymmetry in cross-modal transfer: multimodal unlearning remains effective under textual evaluation, whereas text-only unlearning transfers poorly to visual and cross-modal scenarios. Meanwhile, the evaluated methods largely preserve the models' general capabilities. These findings demonstrate that relying solely on intra-modal evaluation, particularly text-only evaluation, may substantially overestimate the effectiveness of knowledge unlearning in VLMs, underscoring the need for cross-modal unlearning and evaluation.

</details>


### [321] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContinualSkillBench，一个用于上下文持续技能学习的动态评估框架，覆盖5个领域、100个相互关联的子任务。实验发现顺序执行普遍提升性能但收益差异大，上下文学习与显式技能维护平均表现相当，表明改进主要来自上下文适应而非可复用技能抽象，且越弱的模型倾向于积累更多碎片化技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架虽配备了外部技能库，但尚不清楚这些系统能否有效进化技能，以及得到的技能是否真的能提升任务解决能力，缺乏系统的评估框架来衡量这一问题。

Method: 引入ContinualSkillBench动态评估框架，覆盖五个代表性领域，每个领域包含100个按难度递增排列且存在跨任务技能复用机会的相互关联子任务，用于评估上下文持续技能学习能力。

Result: 顺序执行通常能提升性能但收益因模型和领域而异；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均表现相当；显式技能在处理需要可复用流程或精确输出的任务时有选择性优势；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。

Conclusion: 当前的上下文技能进化机制能够支持持续适应，但仍难以将经验稳定整合为稳健且可迁移的技能，显式技能维护虽平均表现不占优但在特定场景下具有价值。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [322] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentGuard框架，将连续潜在推理引入守卫模型，通过分阶段课程学习将文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，在不生成显式推理的情况下进行安全审查，同时通过辅助解码器保持可检查性，实现了更高的准确率和极低的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的守卫模型能提升LLM安全性，但每次交互都解码显式推理代价高昂。虽有潜在推理方法可减少token生成，但在安全审核领域尚未被充分探索，且缺乏部署所需的检查接口。

Method: LatentGuard采用分阶段课程学习，逐步将任务对齐的文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，直接从连续表示预测安全判定；同时设置独立的辅助解码器，按需生成紧凑的审计产物，使推理生成脱离标准推理路径。

Result: LatentGuard-8B在平均加权F1上从83.95提升至84.91（对比GuardReasoner-8B），关键路径推理成本从268.56个生成的推理token降至1.60个潜在推理token，审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数。

Conclusion: LatentGuard展示了高效且可检查的LLM安全防护部署路径，在保持性能提升的同时大幅降低推理成本，为可部署的LLM安全机制提供了新方案。

Abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [323] [Oilbird: Training-Free Speculative Decoding with Keys the Verifier Already Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03839)
*Tao Jin,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Zhenzhu Yan,Teeradaj Racharak,Naoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Oilbird，一种无需训练的推测解码方法。它诊断出基于精确后缀匹配的草稿生成器存在"寻址失败"而非"覆盖不足"的问题，并引入基于隐藏状态的语义草稿源，与现有词法草稿树合并，在多个基准上将接受长度提升24-29%，在API-Bank上实现4.4倍自回归解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练推测解码方法通过精确后缀匹配来生成草稿，但这种方法在工具调用场景中会遗漏池中已有的正确草稿——因为前缀几乎相同但新生成的几个token不同，导致一个token被拒绝后其后正确的延续也被丢弃。作者希望解决这个寻址层面的缺陷。

Method: 首先跨十个基准逐位置诊断失败原因，发现约一半的遗漏草稿其实存在于池中但无法通过精确匹配访问。随后提出第二种语义草稿源：利用验证器在已提交token上计算的隐藏状态对同一池重新建索引，并将语义草稿与现有词法草稿树合并，实现双重草稿来源的协同。

Result: 在三个已发布的草稿器中，在匹配的池大小和预算条件下，Oilbird将接受长度提升24-29%。在API-Bank基准上，Oilbird达到4.4倍自回归解码速度，优于最强无训练基线的3.9倍和EAGLE-3的2.0倍。

Conclusion: 诊断表明无训练推测解码的瓶颈在于寻址方式而非草稿覆盖范围；利用验证器已有的隐藏状态作为语义索引可以显著提高草稿命中率，从而提升端到端解码速度。

Abstract: Training-free speculative decoding drafts by matching an exact suffix of the context against a pool of earlier context. That lookup misses correct drafts already in the pool, most visibly on tool-calling traffic, where a request repeats almost everything but the few values minted for it, and where one rejected token discards the correct continuation behind it. We diagnose the failure position by position across ten benchmarks and find it to be a problem of addressing rather than of coverage: on our densest tool-calling benchmark, about half of what the strongest exact-match drafter misses is present in the pool yet unreachable by exact matching. We therefore propose a second, semantic draft source: the same pool, re-keyed by the hidden state the verifier has already computed at each committed token, together with a merge that lets it ride inside an existing lexical drafter's tree. In three published drafters, at matched pool and budget, it lifts accepted length by 24-29%. Oilbird reaches 4.4x autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank, against 3.9x for the strongest training-free baseline in our harness and 2.0x for EAGLE-3.

</details>


### [324] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAFIA框架，一种仅查询（query-only）的记忆攻击方法，通过记忆探测和事实注入来对抗大规模良性记忆池和输入审计，实现了高达90.7%的攻击成功率，同时将审计检测率从83.3%抑制到不超过7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强型LLM代理依赖丰富上下文进行长时推理和行动，但其记忆模块暴露了恶意记录的攻击面。现有仅查询攻击在两种现实场景中失效：大规模良性记忆池（检索竞争激烈）和主动输入审计（语义检查严格），因此需要更强大的攻击框架来揭示这些安全漏洞。

Method: MAFIA包含两个核心组件：(1) 放置策略——通过记忆探测、预算分配和调度实现检索竞争性注入；(2) 载荷设计——利用紧凑的事实伪装（compact factual cloaks）绕过审计，在保持高语义相似度的同时保留恶意效果。

Result: 大量实验表明MAFIA达到最高90.7%的攻击成功率，并将审计检测率从峰值83.3%抑制至最多7.4%，暴露了代理记忆系统的关键安全漏洞。

Conclusion: MAFIA揭示了当前代理记忆系统在扩展威胁模型下的脆弱性，表明现有的仅查询攻击无法有效应对检索竞争与审计检查的双重挑战，研究代码已公开。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [325] [ADMITBench: A Safety-Governed Reference Framework for Evaluating the Admissibility of Industrial LLM Advisories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03866)
*Yash Misra,Javal Vyas,Siddharth Gutta,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ADMITBench，一个用于评估工业大语言模型咨询建议的参考框架，在行动层面通过版本化、安全治理的评估契约来检查建议是否有证据支持、是否在权限和程序范围内，以及是否符合工厂特定后果检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中LLM生成的操作建议需要严格的安全治理评估，但目前缺乏在行动层面进行标准化评估的参考框架。

Method: ADMITBench实现了版本化、安全治理的评估契约，包含三层检查：证据支持性、权限与程序合规性、以及基于工厂特定评估配置的后果可接受性检查。采用显式、不可补偿的资格检查机制，并从版本化工厂配置文件中派生。

Result: 发布了Release 0.1.0作为公开参考实现，用于技术研究和评估目的。

Conclusion: ADMITBench是用于技术和研究评估的公开参考实现，并非物理执行授权；其中的"安全治理"仅指通过显式不可补偿检查确定资格，不代表评估器、模型或工厂已获得安全认证。

Abstract: This white paper presents ADMITBench, a reference framework for evaluating industrial LLM advisories at the level of the proposed action. The framework implements a versioned, safety-governed evaluation contract that checks whether a recommendation is supported by the available evidence, permitted under the stated authority and procedure, and acceptable under the plant-specific consequence checks encoded in the selected evaluation profile. In this report, \emph{safety-governed} means that eligibility is determined through explicit, non-compensatory checks derived from a versioned plant profile; it does not mean that the evaluator, model, or plant has been safety-certified. Release 0.1.0 is a public reference implementation for technical and research evaluation, not an authorisation for physical execution.

</details>


### [326] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型Qwen3-32B中时间视野的线性表征，通过对比线性探针识别短期与长期的表征方向，并利用对比激活加法引导技术定向改变模型的时间相关偏好、推荐和能力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并控制大语言模型的时间相关偏好对于AI系统在涉及延迟成本与收益的决策和建议场景中至关重要，同时关系到长视野规划的安全性问题。

Method: 在教师强制的时序选择答案上训练对比线性探针，找到模型中残差流上的短期与长期方向；然后评估对比激活加法引导技术在二元时序选择、货币跨期选择及旅行规划能力基准上的效果。

Result: 时间视野方向可通过简单的对比线性探针识别，引导技术能引起大幅度的双向偏好变化；在分布外货币选择任务中显著改变模型对小而快与大而慢奖励的无差异阈值；适度的时间引导还能提升规划相关的能力指标。

Conclusion: 模型跨期偏好是可测量且可引导的，这对AI在涉及延迟成本与收益的建议场景以及长视野规划的安全性问题具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [327] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示自适应跟踪器中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性这一结构缺陷，并提出对抗性路径反转(API)框架，通过极微小扰动精确操纵门控决策迫使推理进入篡改的计算路径，从而瓦解模型表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机平台的资源约束推动了自适应Transformer跟踪器的发展，但其"按需计算"的动态路由架构隐藏着严重的结构缺陷——计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性导致网络对微小输入扰动极其敏感，这是一个此前被忽视的、可直接利用的新攻击面。

Method: 形式化刻画自适应跟踪架构中路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性，提出Adversarial Path-Inversion (API)框架：生成不可感知的扰动精确操纵门控模块的决策，迫使推理进入被篡改的计算路径，利用原始路径与反转路径之间的严重不一致性来破坏模型表征能力。

Result: 在多个最先进的自适应跟踪器上的大量实验表明，API在扰动隐身性、攻击有效性和推理速度方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态跟踪网络的安全分析开辟了新维度，并为未来构建鲁棒的自适应跟踪架构提供了理论警示。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [328] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将多元对齐问题从"输出多样化"重新定位为"社会性协调问题"，引入社会学理论（角色、互动、权力场域）来指导AI系统设计，提出基于角色的表示、结构化协调机制和情境敏感评估等具体设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在日益多样的社会情境中被部署，但现有对齐方法缺乏对"价值观如何在社会中被组织、争论和协调"的清晰理解。传统的单一价值观对齐框架已不足以应对多元合法的社会视角。

Method: 借鉴社会学中的成熟理论传统，将视角理解为：由角色结构化、通过互动塑造、分布在权力与专业场域中。将这些理论洞察转化为具体的设计含义，包括基于角色的表示、视角间的结构化协调、情境敏感评估，以及对智能体系统在角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈回路等多层面的对齐要求。

Result: 提出一个设计空间，使系统能够以结构化和可问责的方式参与多种视角；将多元对齐重新定位为"社会性协调"而非简单的输出多样化，并识别了未来在真实环境中实施和实证评估的研究方向。

Conclusion: 社会理论为多元对齐提供了必要的概念和设计资源；未来的多元对齐需要超越输出层面的对齐，深入到AI系统生产输出的全过程（角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则、反馈回路），实现社会性协调。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [329] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Álvarez和Ruggieri（2025）提出的因果感知框架，将结构性和参数性因果感知操作化，设计了计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法以及度量分歧的距离指标，并在德国信用数据集上展示了因果感知如何影响多专家决策中的准确性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知虽然是一个有前景的概率框架，但此前仅停留在理论层面，缺乏实际实现和验证。作者旨在弥合理论与应用之间的鸿沟，为因果感知提供首个计算实现。

Method: 将因果感知区分为两类：结构性因果感知（代理对因果图存在分歧）和参数性因果感知（代理对因果图达成一致但对权重存在分歧）。设计了计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法，并提出合适的距离度量来量化代理间的分歧程度。使用德国信用数据集进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明：感知判定结果对距离度量和阈值的选择敏感；因果感知会改变公平性评估和基于阈值的决策；偏差相对于代理的SCM具有情境性，表明公平性问题中竞争性世界观的不可忽视性。

Conclusion: 因果感知会影响多专家决策中的准确性和公平性评估，偏差是情境化的而非绝对的，不同代理的因果模型视角会导致不同的公平性判断，因此在公平性问题上必须考虑竞争性的世界观。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [330] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对Transformer推理过程的新解释——SIDPP（序列级交互动态并行处理），认为Transformer并非简单复现训练统计规律，而是在推理时构建并应用由提示生成的参数化变换，且该机制可能在人类大脑皮层中以类似方式实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"随机鹦鹉"观点，即认为大语言模型只是复现训练中学到的统计规律。作者主张Transformer在推理阶段实际上是在构建和应用依赖于提示的动态变换，这是一种更主动、更有解释力的计算范式。

Method: 将Transformer解释为"以概念变换概念"的系统：token向量是被变换的概念，由矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换是"变换性概念"。这些变换可以是静态的（训练固定）或动态的（由输入序列生成）。在机制上，它们对应一组简单的神经网络，其新颖之处在于"输出-权重互连"——即某些网络的输出决定其他网络的权重，从而实现了从提示中构建变换并修改token表示的过程。

Result: 动态处理的贡献随提示长度增长，可能等于或超过静态处理，作者称之为"强提示敏感性"。此外，该框架对可解释性、可预测性、可控性以及设计更小的可持续系统具有意义。作者还论证人类神经系统具备实现SIDPP的机制，推测人类语言处理本身可能就是一种SIDPP。

Conclusion: SIDPP为理解Transformer提供了一种超越"统计鹦鹉"的全新视角，不仅适用于人工智能系统，还可能揭示了人类语言处理的神经计算本质，即人类大脑皮层可能以功能架构相似的方式实现了SIDPP式的语言处理。

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [331] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT框架，基于人类辅导研究构建了Tutor-Strategy和Student-Move两个互补的分类法，利用TACTCorpus（260个真实对话、32,379个标注）对Qwen3.5-4B进行监督微调和分类法对齐的GRPO后训练，得到TACTutor模型。在TACTBench基准上提升20.30%并超越所有闭源基线，在50名学习者的盲测中获得最高评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于ESL对话辅导时，仅能流畅生成回复，但缺乏基于学习者行为和对话上下文的适应性教学策略选择能力。人类辅导研究中的原则虽已存在，但多为任务特异性的，未能充分整合到LLM的训练和评估中，需要一个人性化的框架来弥补这一差距。

Method: 1) 基于文献构建两个互补分类法：Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy（13种辅导策略）和Student-Move Taxonomy（按移动类型和状态刻画学习者行为）；2) 构建TACTCorpus，丰富260个真实师生对话，包含32,379个标注及质量控制增强训练数据；3) 通过监督微调（SFT）后进行分类法对齐的GRPO后训练Qwen3.5-4B，优化脚手架质量而非仅参考模仿。

Result: TACTutor在TACTBench（含78个真实辅导场景的策略平衡诊断基准）上相较于骨干模型提升20.30%，在同协议下超越所有评估的闭源基线，同时在既有外部教育基准上保持骨干性能；在50名学习者的盲测研究中获得最高的总体平均评分。

Conclusion: TACT框架提供了开放的数据、基准和模型权重，为开发教学适应性ESL辅导系统奠定了开放基础，证明了将人类辅导分类学整合到LLM训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [332] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于基础模型的AI智能体在进行最优规划时，在社会困境博弈中会稳定地收敛到合作行为，这与经典博弈论预测的相互背叛相矛盾。作者提出了"嵌入式贝叶斯智能体"理论模型和"嵌入式均衡"这一新的解概念，用以解释和刻画现代AI智能体的社会行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型驱动的自主智能体被越来越多地整合到社会和经济系统中，理解其集体行为的原理对于确保安全与合作至关重要。经典博弈论基于"解耦能动性"假设，但现代AI智能体在决策时会联合预测自身未来行动与外部观察，这使经典框架可能不再适用。

Method: 首先通过实验观察基础模型智能体在社会困境中的行为，发现其一致收敛到合作。然后提出"嵌入式贝叶斯智能体"理论模型，将智能体从解耦能动性转向嵌入式能动性，使其将自身视为所居宇宙的一部分，并对自身决策算法保持认知不确定性。通过相似性推断机制，智能体将自身规划中的决策作为证据，推断相似伙伴也会做出类似决策。最终形式化为"嵌入式均衡"作为Nash均衡的替代解概念。

Result: 实验表明基础模型智能体在风格化社会困境中通过最优规划稳定收敛到合作，直接反驳了经典博弈论预测的相互背叛。理论分析证明嵌入式智能体通过相似性推断机制能够产生合作行为，嵌入式均衡为这一现象提供了形式化基础。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现代AI智能体的社会行为与经典博弈论预测存在根本性分歧，提出了新的理论框架（嵌入式贝叶斯智能体与嵌入式均衡），为理解基础模型智能体的合作行为提供了新的博弈论基础，对AI安全和社会系统中智能体的协作设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [333] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应测试时缩放方法，使用轻量级模糊控制器将可解释信号（提示复杂度和模型置信度）映射到每个查询的采样预算，在保持性能的同时减少平均样本数，使推理计算可检查而非固定或黑盒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时缩放方法使用固定的每查询预算，在简单和困难提示上花费相同的计算量，既不高效也难以检查，无法解释为什么某个提示获得特定的样本数。

Method: 提出自适应测试时缩放方法，使用轻量级模糊控制器，将估计的提示复杂度和模型置信度等可解释信号映射为每个查询的采样预算。简单或高置信度提示分配更少样本，困难或低置信度提示分配更多样本。在公平对齐协议下评估，匹配解码设置并控制答案选择，与best-of-N、计算感知缩放和基于自置信度的基线进行比较。

Result: 在问答和数学推理任务上，跨越多个模型和数据集，自适应模糊控制优于多个标准基线，并与选择器匹配的全预算控制性能接近，同时减少了平均样本数量。

Conclusion: 可解释的自适应采样是大语言模型更高效测试时推理的一个实用方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [334] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE（人类输入变异引擎），包含语音转录扰动和QWERTY键盘扰动两类工具集，用于评估大语言模型对人类不同输入通道（语音和键盘）扰动的鲁棒性，并揭示了模型性能下降的根源在于问题token的丢失而非新增token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类向语言模型的输入可以通过打字或说话两种方式完成，而不同通道会留下不同的扰动特征：键盘输入带有拼写噪声，语音输入带有转写不流畅和AI转写工具的重组结构。但现有的模型评估很少系统性地量化这些真实人类输入扰动对LLM性能的影响。

Method: 构建HIVE工具集，分别生成语音转录扰动和QWERTY键盘扰动，并在多种指令微调模型上进行系统评测，对比两种扰动通道对模型准确率的影响，同时控制变量分析扰动的结构特征、token存活率、跨任务类型差异、数据污染、轻量适配和推理预算等因素。

Result: 发现七项结论：(i)语音转录扰动在所有测试的指令微调模型上均降低准确率，且成本来自转录结构而非填充词；(ii)键盘扰动成本较低，模型能吸收大量扰动；(iii)两种扰动的影响可归因于问题token的存活率，破坏token才是伤害来源；(iv)通道差异仅在需要构建或推演答案的任务中出现，多选题无差异；(v)危害并非仅来自测试集污染；(vi)轻量适配无法消除该影响；(vii)增加思考预算几乎完全恢复键盘通道性能，但对语音通道无效，压缩语音反而更差。

Conclusion: 大语言模型对键盘类扰动相对鲁棒，但对语音转录类扰动的鲁棒性不足，且这种脆弱性源于核心信息token的丢失，无法通过轻量适配解决，推理时思考预算也仅对键盘通道有效。这提示未来需要针对语音输入通道设计专门的鲁棒性解决方案。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [335] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectRL框架，利用专家模型在困难问题上失败的轨迹（称为"黄金负轨迹"）作为反思信号进行策略训练，通过反思推理和策略迁移提升LLM的推理能力，在9个基准、4个骨干模型和4种on-policy训练方法上均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹引导式on-policy训练方法依赖专家模型生成的成功轨迹作为监督信号，但当专家在困难问题上失败时，这些失败轨迹往往被直接丢弃为负样本，导致困难问题上缺乏有效的训练信号。作者发现失败轨迹虽然不能作为示范进行模仿，但可以作为有缺陷的轨迹供模型反思，且反思一条有缺陷的轨迹比从零开始直接解题更容易、更有效。

Method: 作者提出ReflectRL框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 利用黄金负轨迹引发模型的反思推理能力（Reflective Reasoning）；(2) 通过Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition将反思获得的行为迁移回直接推理模式。整个框架是轻量级、即插即用的，可集成到现有on-policy训练方法中。

Result: 在9个基准测试、4种LLM骨干模型和4种on-policy训练方法上的实验表明，ReflectRL能够一致性地提升推理性能，且额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹并非无用，通过反思学习可以转化为有价值的训练信号，ReflectRL为on-policy训练中困难问题的处理提供了一种新的有效范 paradigm。

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [336] [Caved or Convinced: Temporal Sampling Gates Claim Deference in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03160)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Jingling Xue,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文研究了视频大语言模型在时间顺序判断中的一种双重失败模式：既会屈从于虚假声明，也会拒绝真实声明。作者将失败原因分离为"可用性"（采样帧是否包含两个事件）和"加权"（证据是否被信任）两个因素，并提出一种反转测试通过前后打分来消除模型的顺序先验，显著提升顺序判断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频谄媚（sycophancy）研究只关注模型屈从虚假声明这一种失败，且通过教导模型减少对用户的信任来缓解，但这在文本和图像模型中已被证明会加剧拒绝真实声明的问题。此外，视频领域中的两种失败被归为同一原因，缺乏区分。

Method: 作者设计了两种保持声明不变但改变证据的干预方法：帧保留重排（改变声明真假）和采样偏移（固定帧预算下捕获或遗漏两个事件）。基于此分离"可用性"和"加权"两个失败因素，并提出反转测试——对采样帧进行正向和反向打分，相减以抵消模型顺序先验，从而决定回答、重新采样或弃权。

Result: 在九个模型中，当事件被遗漏时，模型接受真假声明的比率相同（Youden's J=0），证明可用性是必要条件但非充分条件。九个模型中有五个能读取顺序，但其中四个仍会屈从虚假声明，存在加权天花板。应用反转测试后，能读顺序的模型顺序准确率提升至0.92-1.00，不能读的模型选择弃权而非猜测。

Conclusion: 视频模型的时间判断失败需要区分"可用性"和"加权"两个维度分别处理。由于无法对从未采样的证据进行信任校准，反转测试通过消除顺序先验来避免对不可用证据的猜测，是一种有效的补救方案。

Abstract: When asked which of two events came first, video large language models can fail in two opposite ways: cave to a false claim, or reject a true one. Prior video sycophancy work measures only the first and mitigates it by teaching the model to trust the user less, a fix known in text and image models to worsen the second. In video, both failures come from two causes the literature treats as one: availability, whether the sparse sampled frames contain the two events, and weighting, whether that evidence is trusted over the user. We separate them with two interventions that keep the claim fixed: a frame-preserving reorder that flips the claim's truth, and a sampling-offset shift that captures or misses both events at a fixed frame budget. When the events are missed, the two twins present identical frames, so each of the nine models we evaluate accepts a true and a false claim at the same rate, making Youden's $J=0$ by construction. Availability is necessary but not sufficient. Five of the nine read the order, yet four of those five still cave to the false claim, so their deference hits a weighting ceiling. Since trust cannot be calibrated over evidence that was never sampled, we propose a reversal test that cancels the model's order prior by scoring the sampled frames forward and reversed, then answers, resamples, or abstains without reading the claim. The test raises the order accuracy to 0.92-1.00 on the models that read the order and abstains rather than guesses on those that cannot.

</details>


### [337] [Hear to See: Discerning Stateful Listening for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03264)
*Leiye Liu,Miao Zhang,Jiahong Jiang,Jingjing Li,Jialong Zhong,Kai Peng,Tingwei Liu,Wei Ji,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Hear to See (H2S)框架解决音视频实例分割中的两个核心挑战：混合音频与视觉实例的精确匹配，以及音频-视频信号时间不同步时的鲁棒跟踪。通过声学语义投影器（ASP）和异步动态调制器（ADM）两个机制，在AVISeg数据集上达到SOTA性能（48.54 mAP），超越此前最优方法7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频实例分割方法难以匹配重叠的声学事件与视觉实例，也无法处理音频-视频信号的时间不对齐问题。因此论文提出两个关键问题：如何在重叠声源与视觉实例间建立精确对应关系？如何在时间错位下维持鲁棒跟踪？

Method: 提出H2S框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 声学语义投影器（ASP）——解耦混合音频，建立从语义域到空间域的层次化对应关系；(2) 异步动态调制器（ADM）——通过音频调制的Mamba架构自适应调整状态转换，在动态变化时优先当前信息，在稳定期保持连续性。

Result: 在AVISeg数据集上，H2S达到SOTA性能，使用COCO预训练的ResNet50获得48.54 mAP，超过此前最佳方法7.8%。

Conclusion: H2S通过解耦混合音频建立语义到空间的层次对应，并利用音频调制的Mamba机制处理异步动态，有效解决了音视频实例分割中的重叠匹配和时间不对齐难题。代码将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Audio-visual instance segmentation (AVIS) requires accurately identifying and tracking individual sounding objects with pixel-level masks. Existing methods struggle to match overlapping acoustic events with visual instances and handle asynchronous audio-visual dynamics. Therefore, two critical questions arise: how can a model establish precise correspondence between overlapping sound sources and visual instances, and how can a model maintain robust tracking when audio and visual signals are temporally misaligned?This paper proposes Hear to See (H2S), addressing these challenges through two mechanisms. The Acoustic-Semantic Projector (ASP) disentangles mixed audio and establishes hierarchical correspondence from semantic to spatial domains. The Asynchronous Dynamics Modulator (ADM) adaptively adjusts state transitions via audio-modulated Mamba, prioritizing current information during dynamic variations and maintaining continuity in stable periods.Experiments on AVISeg show H2S achieves SOTA performance, attaining 48.54 mAP with a COCO pretrained ResNet50 and surpassing the previous by 7.8\%. The code will be open-sourced once the paper is accepted. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/leiyeliu/H2S.

</details>


### [338] [Balancing Efficiency and Efficacy: Training-Free Attention-Guided Switching Between Explicit and Latent Thoughts for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03450)
*Haoqian Kang,Liupeng Li,Kuofeng Gao,Jinpeng Wang,Zhenyu Lu,Bin Chen,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的MLLM推理策略AGS，通过视觉-文本注意力比率动态解耦感知与推理，在不同token上自适应触发潜在推理或显式文本生成，兼顾准确率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型推理方法存在瓶颈：显式CoT计算昂贵且易产生视觉幻觉，潜在推理方法需昂贵训练，而直接迁移无训练LLM推理机制性能不稳定，原因在于其依赖的token级熵混淆了感知模糊性与逻辑不确定性。

Method: 提出视觉-文本注意力比率（vision-to-text attention ratio）作为新指标，动态衡量模型认知焦点；基于此设计Attention-Guided Switching（AGS）框架，对感知token触发潜在推理以在连续空间中保留高保真视觉信息，对逻辑token强制执行显式文本生成以维持结构锚定。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法达到最先进性能，显著提升准确率与推理效率，减少了自回归步数和延迟。

Conclusion: 通过解耦感知与推理并依据注意力信号动态切换推理模式，无需训练即可在MLLM中实现高效且准确的推理，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires both fine-grained visual perception and rigorous logical deduction. Explicit text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is computationally expensive and prone to visual hallucinations, while existing latent reasoning methods typically require costly training. Furthermore, directly adapting training-free LLM reasoning mechanisms to the multimodal setting yields unstable performance. We identify that this failure stems from their reliance on token-level entropy, which fundamentally conflates perceptual ambiguity (e.g., unclear visual details) with logical uncertainty (e.g., complex reasoning steps). To overcome this bottleneck, we present a novel training-free inference strategy for MLLMs that explicitly decouples perception and reasoning. We propose a novel metric, the vision-to-text attention ratio, to dynamically gauge the model's cognitive focus. Guided by this metric, our proposed framework, Attention-Guided Switching (AGS), adaptively triggers latent reasoning for perceptual tokens to preserve high-fidelity visual information in the continuous space, while enforcing explicit text generation for logical tokens to maintain structural anchoring. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly improving both accuracy and inference efficiency by reducing autoregressive steps and latency. Code is released at https://github.com/swordAndSnow/MM26-AGS.

</details>


### [339] [Adaptive Modality Reliability Diagnosis and Restoration for Robust Multimodal Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03475)
*Suraj Kumar,Mohnish Raj,Soumi Chattopadhayay,Chandranath Adak,Ayan Dutta*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: PRIME是一个闭环可靠性引导的多模态意图识别框架，通过诊断、修复、再评估三个环节对每个样本的模态质量进行动态管理，在保持干净数据性能的同时提升对缺失、噪声、冲突和不平衡模态条件的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态意图识别方法在模态质量下降时（噪声、缺失、语义冲突、主导不均衡）只能隐含地推断模态重要性并重新加权或抑制不可靠输入，无法判断一个退化的模态是否可以被修复并重新信任。

Method: PRIME包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过上下文对数方差表示模态弱点，利用预测置信度、认知分歧、跨模态共识和特征退化等诊断证据，并使用受控模态损坏和异方差不确定性目标显式训练可靠性估计器；(2) 原型条件变分修复模块，利用估计的弱点控制从互补模态重建退化表示；(3) 修复后重新估计可靠性，并以逆方差方式进行多模态融合。

Result: 实验表明PRIME在干净数据上保持竞争性能，同时在模态缺失、噪声、冲突和模态不平衡条件下显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过闭环的诊断-修复-再评估机制，PRIME证明了退化模态可以被修复后重新信任，相比直接丢弃不可靠模态的传统方法在多模态意图识别任务中具有更优的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Multimodal intent recognition combines linguistic, acoustic, and visual evidence, but individual modalities may be noisy, missing, semantically conflicting, or disproportionately dominant. Existing methods typically infer modality importance implicitly and either reweight or suppress unreliable inputs, without determining whether a degraded modality can be repaired and subsequently trusted. We propose PRIME (Precision-weighted Reliability Inference and Modality rEstoration), a closed-loop reliability guided framework that jointly diagnoses, restores, and reassesses modality quality at the sample level. PRIME represents the weakness of each modality through a contextual log-variance estimated from complementary diagnostic evidence, including predictive confidence, epistemic disagreement, cross-modal consensus, and feature degeneracy. Because modality-reliability annotations are unavailable, the estimator is explicitly trained using controlled modality corruption with known degradation severity, together with a heteroscedastic uncertainty objective. Rather than directly discarding an unreliable modality, PRIME uses its estimated weakness to control a prototype-conditioned variational restoration module that reconstructs the degraded representation from complementary modalities. Crucially, reliability is re-estimated after restoration, allowing the model to determine whether the repaired representation has become sufficiently trustworthy to contribute to prediction. The resulting post-restoration precisions are used for inverse-variance multimodal fusion. Experiments on multimodal intent-recognition benchmarks show that PRIME maintains competitive clean-data performance while improving robustness under missing, noisy, conflicting, and modality-imbalanced conditions.

</details>
